# Project History

✅ **THR-783 — step-outcome effects wired; `onFailureEffects` was a field nothing declared and nothing read** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — The THR-101 tavern migration (2026-04-24) converted the legacy `appliesWound` flag into a `failureMetadata.onFailureEffects` block at five sites. That key was **never declared on `ActionStepOutcomeMetadata` and never read by any engine module** — a repo-wide grep returned only the five authoring sites plus two changelog mentions — so from 2026-04-24 until today a lost tavern brawl marked nobody. The legacy side was dead too: `buildOutcomeCompat` (`src/engine/encounter.ts:96`) hardcodes `appliesWound: false`, so both the old mechanism and its replacement were inert simultaneously. **Verdict: wired, not deleted**, and generalised while wiring — the new key is `effects` on `ActionStepOutcomeMetadata`, read **symmetrically** from whichever side fired, rather than a doubly-negative `onFailureEffects` that would have been legal-but-meaningless inside `successMetadata`. It dispatches through the *same* `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` the reaction path uses (by wrapping the list in a synthetic single-use reaction), so the entire effect vocabulary — conditions, hidden marks, seeds, intel, bonds, reach signatures — became authorable per step for free, and no effect kind can be live on one path and dead on the other, which is the failure mode the ticket exists to close. **The per-key membership sweep (Done-when 4) is complete and clean:** parsing every `successMetadata`/`failureMetadata` literal in `src/` finds exactly five authored keys — `reputationDelta` (285), `rewardPool` (239), `tierPromotionEligible` (143), the nested `categoryWeights`/`tagFilters`, and `onFailureEffects` (5) — and `onFailureEffects` was the **only** one with no reader. **The regression suite was falsified against the unwired build** — stubbing the engine hook turns it 5-of-11 red, precisely the five that assert the effect *fires*; the six that stay green are the negative cases (success and `near_miss` don't fire, empty list no-ops, missing runtime fails soft) plus content binding, which pass on both builds by construction. `near_miss` is asserted rather than assumed, because `isStepSuccess` counts it as a **success** and it is the one band a reader is likely to guess wrong. Two correctness details the wiring would have silently missed: the dispatcher is state-returning while `executeStepResult` mutates in place (the changed fields are copied back), and `condition_attachment` sets `touchedStructure` **without touching itself**, so the helper honours the same `touchStructure`/`touchWorld` contract `phaseAutonomousAftermath` does — without it the edge lands and every structural cache serves stale. **The finding worth more than the fix: the effect still cannot land in a live game, for a reason upstream of this ticket.** Driving the original repro (`spawn encounter @hero tavern.brawl`) now resolves `["success_at_cost", "failure"]` — the failure side fires correctly — and still mints zero edges, because **`trait.condition.wounded` does not exist as a graph node**. `ensureTraitNodes` is the only path that inserts the 13 condition + mastery definitions, and it is called exclusively from the orchestrator's loop over legacy `state.encounterProgress`, which the unified pipeline no longer populates; measured at tick 60 on seed 42, 1 of 6 condition and 2 of 7 mastery definitions are present, all minted on demand elsewhere — a partial set proves the seeder never ran. Filed as **THR-809** (which also carries the latent `allDefs[0]` short-circuit: the guard tests a *mastery* id, so one minted node skips the other twelve permanently). Reported as a first-class result rather than absorbed, per the THR-761 impediment on reachability. Full suite **13390 green (896 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3500, DOWN 3** from the 3503 baseline — declaring `effects` cleared the excess-property errors the five sites were generating, which independently confirms the field was undeclared (baseline refreshed to hold the new floor); `vite build` 9.96s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events. Wiki freshness satisfied by **updating** both flagged pages (Encounters manual, Divine actions), not by exemption — behaviour changed. Browser-verify exempt: engine, content-data and docs only, no `src/components/`, view or HexMapV2 surface touched.

✅ **THR-800 — 40 of 62 dead trait refs repointed onto live definitions; the remaining 22 are a producer problem, not a vocabulary one** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — THR-786 built `validateTraitRefs` and measured **62 authored trait refs that no definition could satisfy** — every one a gate permanently false, so mortals who earn `Battle-Hardened` or a reputation title got no ambition steering and `scoreDesirability`'s boosting/blocking contribution was 0 in every run ever played. The ticket offered two routes — repoint the refs, or **mint the missing traits** — and the material finding is that **route 2 does not work**: every trait producer mints from a **closed, hardcoded set** (`phaseEconomicTraits` by named constant, `phaseEncounterTraits` by reach→mastery map, `phaseReputationTraits` by reach, `corePersonality` by continuum, `personalityTraitEmerge` by axis). Adding a definition to a content file gives a ref something to resolve *to* while leaving it held by no bearer: the gate stays false and the sweep goes **quiet**, which is strictly worse than a visible dead ref. Minting without producer work is gate theater, so repointing is the only route that actually revives a gate. **40 refs repointed**, each onto a definition some phase genuinely mints — the 4 choice-set predicates (`negotiator` → `trait.mastery.silver-tongue`, `dauntless` → `trait.personality.iron.virtue`, `leader` → `trait.personality.star.virtue`, `interrogator` → `trait.mastery.keen-eyed`, each confirmed against its own option prose), 29 ambition selection keys, and 9 graph-condition milestone/abandonment flags where the mapping is exact rather than approximate (`forgiven` → the same Core virtue the revenge ambitions already block on; `enlightened` → `trait.mastery.spell-weaver`, since the arcane milestone *is* reaching Veil mastery; `miracle_worker` → `trait.mastery.anointed`, which the milestone's own prose names — "Miracle — or mastery pushed past what anyone thought possible"). **The 22 left dead are left dead deliberately, in four classes.** Four are `destiny_*`, and the `destiny` category is *reserved and empty by design* per the UL shard shipped hours earlier — repointing would misname the concept and minting would contradict canon of the same day. Two (`living`, `rare_ore_secured`) are **not traits at all**: an aliveness check and a resource state miscoded as `agent_has_trait` because that was the available condition shape; they want a new `AmbitionCondition` variant. Three are narrative flags an ambition would have to mint about its own bearer. Thirteen have no live counterpart, and **seven of those are `blockingTraits`, where a wrong repoint is worse than a dead one** — a dead blocker is permissive, a mistaken blocker silently excludes agents who should qualify, so no blocker was force-fit. All 22 carry their class reason in `KNOWN_DEAD` and are filed as **THR-808**. **The regression test pins a set, not a count**: Done-when #1 asked for `dead.length === 0`, which is not honestly reachable, and a `toBeLessThan(23)` cap would let content rot back in underneath it — `toEqual` on the exact remaining refs fails in *both* directions, so a new dead ref cannot be authored and an existing one cannot leave the list without someone editing it deliberately, which is the per-ref accounting Done-when #2 asked for. **Two pre-existing tests went red, correctly**: `traitPredicate.contract.test.ts` and `traitRefIndex.test.ts` both asserted these refs *stay* dead — they were written by THR-786 to pin the defect, so fixing it had to repoint them (the CLAUDE.md rule that retiring a contract repoints the tests asserting its dead side). The empty-graph sweep test kept its shape and gained a note explaining that it tests the *sweep* while the new file tests the *content*. The 21 phantom grant keys are unchanged and now justified at each of the 5 grant-site files: they gate correctly (the THR-737 grant→gate loop closes on the bare key) but carry no display name, visibility or contributions, which collides with the "always visible once known" rule — count pinned so a new bare-key grant is deliberate. Full suite **13379 green (895 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3503 unchanged**; `vite build` 12.16s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events. Browser-verify exempt: content data and tests only, no `src/components/`, view, or HexMapV2 surface.

✅ **THR-788 — Traits & Marks wiki page + the `Traits` UL shard; the trait authoring vocabulary is settled** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — The docs pillar of the THR-789 trait-trigger program, and the ticket THR-800 was mutex'd behind: rewriting 62 dead trait refs required the vocabulary they would be rewritten *to* to exist first. Two deliverables. **A new `Traits` UL shard** — the 8th, taking the glossary to 110 terms — defining Trait, Trait Assignment, Trait Category, Destiny, Trait Ref, TraitPredicate, Trait Visibility, Trait Hook, and Selection-Competence Separation, wired into the dashboard by adding `'traits'` to both `ULShardId` unions (generator + component) and `'Traits.md'` to `SHARD_FILE_TO_ID`; unknown shard files are skipped *silently* by the generator, so a shard added to the README table without the map entry would have shipped invisible. **A new `Traits & Marks` Manual wiki page** (`public/traits-marks-reference.html`, registered after *The Mortals* with 15 literal source paths — no globs, so no dead-glob class), dual-layer per the W1 precedent: player prose then Designer Notes with 7 IMPL / 8 DESIGN / 2 OPEN flags and a 22-row constants table. **Three things settled rather than restated.** (1) The `requiredTraits` **name collision** THR-786's Done-when #3 routed here is resolved by *naming both shapes* — *ambition trait keys* (bare strings, `types/ambition.ts`) and *template trait predicates* (`TraitPredicate[]`) — explicitly **not** by renaming either field, because one resolver already reconciles them and the shapes are load-bearing. (2) **`destiny` receives its first definition anywhere** while still carrying zero content: declared in `TraitCategory`, honored only by the agent sheet's display ordering (`agentDetail.ts:781`), no producer and no reader — written now so the wave that fills it inherits settled wording. (3) The "personality and reputation steer selection, mastery steers competence, never crossed" shorthand is **corrected in writing**: `trait.reputation.iron.positive` carries `domainContributions: {iron: 0.10, heart: 0.05}`, so the honest law is that competence only ever moves through the *capped* channels — not that reputation never touches it. Every number on the page is **measured, not estimated**: a throwaway vitest survey over the six definition files (removed before commit) gives 64 static definitions — reputation 19, personality 16, core 10, mastery 9, condition 7, scar 2, cultural 1 — with `innate`/`bestowed` minted at runtime and `destiny` neither. **The page's honest calls are its substance:** THR-800's 62 dead refs get a red callout stating the authoring rule (a hook must name a trait some producer mints, and it fails silently otherwise); grepping `src/data/` finds **zero** authored `traitVariants` and exactly **one** authored template trait gate (`blockedByTraits: ['trait.reputation.shadow.negative']` in `shadow-court-audience.ts`, which does resolve), so the hook surfaces are capability without content; and location traits are schema-legal (`has_trait.sourceNodeType` includes `location`/`sublocation`) with **no production caller** — all 14 `assignTrait` sites target an actor. **Two self-inflicted defects caught and fixed before commit rather than shipped:** the heading `Selection–Competence Separation` used an en-dash, which `slugifyHeading` does *not* strip, so the README anchor and the See-Also wikilink both missed (fixed to a plain hyphen; the rendered See-Also chips now resolve, visible in the evidence screenshot); and a one-liner containing `*known*` rendered literal asterisks because the term table is plain text, not markdown-tokenized. Found on the way and filed as **THR-806**: 16 shard terms have no README Term Index entry, and CLAUDE.md makes that index the always-load footprint, so they are invisible to any session that does not open their shard. **That ticket was corrected mid-investigation** — the first framing claimed the dashboard renders them summary-less, but the generator falls back to each term's first body sentence and emits **zero** `missing_one_liner` warnings, so the cost is agent-context only; the ticket now says so and warns that the existing warning can never catch this class. Full suite **13375 green (894 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3503 unchanged**; `vite build` 11.91s; `check:design-wiki` 23 pages, `check:wiki-freshness:blocking` no stale pages. Engine smoke N/A — no engine, tick-loop or type file touched. Browser-verified at a genuine 1920×1080 (PNG dimensions read from the file header): the UL dashboard showing the Traits shard selected with all 9 terms CANONICAL and the Trait Category detail pane rendering body, aliases and three resolved See-Also chips; plus the wiki page with nav injected and active, 10 category cards, 4 hook cards, 17 flags, no horizontal overflow (`scrollWidth` 1905 ≤ 1920). Console on the session's own server: 0 errors, 0 warnings on `?view=ul`; 1 error on the static page, the documented `favicon.ico` 404. Browser-verify substitution: unattended-Playwright route — the Browser pane refused to composite ("pane is not displayed") exactly as THR-754 anticipated, and the Playwright MCP was rooted at a *different* worktree, so screenshots were written to its allowed root and relocated (impediment logged).

✅ **THR-779 — 61 orphaned mortal-affinity templates: the 17 WIRE verdicts executed; the guild reward tier is reachable** (2026-07-26, Encounter Experience) — `generateUnifiedCandidates`, the array-scored path agents actually draw from, skips `regional` and `cosmic` scale outright, so a regional template can only reach mortals through the two scale-agnostic routes: the encounter-cache path or a planted `encounterFamily` seed. **61 shipped templates sat in none of the three**, and the audit's point was that they are not junk — 17 are the *reward tier of guild progression*, the `*.senior.*` / `*.elite.*` content a mortal climbs toward, plus three `fa.*` faction set-pieces and two `monster.encounter.*` ones. All 17 are now registered in a new `CACHE_REGISTERED_REGIONAL_TEMPLATES` export and consumed by `encounterCache.buildEntriesForLocationAndSublocations`, matched on `locationSubtypes` exactly as branching quest templates are. Registration rather than re-scaling is deliberate: `scale` stays load-bearing for `SCALE_PRIORITY` action ordering, so changing it to buy reachability would have moved a second thing silently. The two per-branch supplement loops collapsed into a single pass after the sublocation if/else, preserving entry order in both branches so cache-entry sequencing is byte-identical for existing content. **The three `fa.*` templates needed `locationSubtypes` authored first, having shipped with none at all** — the cache matches on that field, so registering a template without it registers it at no location: a silent no-op with exactly the shape of the bug being fixed, which is why the regression suite asserts the field is present rather than merely pinning the id list. **The finding that outgrew the ticket:** the plan's second clause — gate the guild tier behind `encounterChains` “so the reward tier unlocks with rank as authored” — was **deliberately not executed**, because its substrate is dead. `recordChainStageCompletion` has **zero production callers**, and `agent.properties.chainProgress` is read by four modules (`encounterFilterPipeline`, `encounterScoring`, `phaseAttention`, `kpi/branchingDistance`) and written by none, so it is permanently `{}` and `isChainStageUnlocked` can only ever return true for stage 0. Applying the gate would have moved 12 templates from *unreachable by scale* to *unreachable by an unopenable gate* — no net change, while looking fixed, and failing this ticket's own “the 17 are drawable” Done-when. Filed as **THR-803**, which carries the write path, the gate, and the **6 currently-dead starter-chain stages** (`forbidden_tome`, `arcane_duel`, `guild_negotiation`, `arena_combat`, `caravan_deal`, `smuggler_pact`) the same defect has been hiding since chains shipped. Evidence is deliberately not synthetic: a **live seed-42 medium world built through the real `initializeGameState`** (654 locations, 24,688 cache entries) shows **17/17 attaching**, 1–38 locations each — hand-built test locations would have proven the predicate without proving worldgen mints those place types. The guard was **falsified by deleting the cache consumer**, which turned it red naming all 17 ids. Audit doc corrected per its Done-when: 65 KILL → **48**, the 17 rows re-verdicted `WIRED`, batch 5 raised 152 → 164, and the finding-3 note records both the resolution and the un-executed gate. Full suite **13369 green (894 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3504 unchanged, zero net-new**; `vite build` 12.53s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events. Browser-verify exempt: engine + content data only, no `src/components/`, view, or HexMapV2 surface. Wiki gate satisfied by a new candidacy-cascade bullet on the encounters manual; `action-catalog.html` exempted with proof — it catalogues ascendant-facing actions and all 17 wired templates carry `['individual']`, confirmed by regenerating its payload byte-identical.

✅ **THR-787 — trait `category`/`subcategory` dead reads; reputation titles had never rendered** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — `TraitDefinitionProperties.subcategory` is the canonical field and every shipped trait definition stores it, but three sites read or wrote `category`. The live defect was `gatherNarrativeContext` (`src/engine/proseEnrichment.ts`), whose title filter tested `properties?.category === 'reputation'` — a field **no trait node has ever carried** — so `ctx.titles` was empty for every agent in every run ever played: `{title}` fell back silently to the plain agent name and the `{?has_title}` / `{?no_title}` conditionals were permanently false/true. Measured against a live 40-tick seeded browser game via a fiber-walk of the real `WorldGraph`: of **196** live trait nodes, `subcategory === 'reputation'` selects **19** (Feared Champion, Monopolist, Patron, Brutal Thug…) while `category === 'reputation'` selects **0**, and **zero** live trait nodes carry a `category` property at all — which simultaneously confirms the fix and confirms the removed `cultureGenerator` write had no reader. Same shape as THR-786's node-level `name` finding: a read site aimed at a vocabulary the write side never used, invisible in play because an empty title list degrades to a plausible sentence instead of an error. **The pre-existing tests were green for the defect's entire life** — `createTestGraph` built its reputation trait with `properties: { category: 'reputation' }`, so the fixture and the production filter agreed with each other and disagreed with all 64 shipped definitions; both title assertions passed while nothing in a real game could. The fixture is corrected, and the new regression block is deliberately anchored on **real entries from `REPUTATION_TRAIT_DEFINITIONS`** rather than a hand-made node so the same drift cannot make it vacuous again, with a membership guard asserting every shipped reputation definition stores `subcategory` and none stores `category`, and a negative control proving the filter is not a pass-through (a `mastery` trait yields no title). **Falsified against the unfixed build**: 3 of 4 new positive cases red plus the repointed fixture test, negative control correctly green. The other two sites are reported as **not live defects** rather than inflated into the headline: `cultureGenerator.ts` wrote a stray `category: 'innate'` on the culture trait node that contradicted its own `subcategory: 'cultural'` and had no reader anywhere (removed; the existing test already asserted `subcategory`), and `src/data/trait-modifiers.ts` is a standalone CMS table, never materialised as graph nodes, whose `category` field was self-consistently paired with its CMS column key and its own test — renamed to `subcategory` with both readers updated in the same commit so the repo speaks one trait vocabulary. Full suite **13358 green (893 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3517 unchanged** (one net-new error introduced by the guard's cast was fixed, not baselined); `vite build` 11.65s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events. Browser-verified at 1920×1080 (CMS → Traits → LOS Trait Modifiers, Category column populated across all 5 rows, proving the renamed key still binds), console 0 errors, `__DEBUG.validateTraitRefs()` reporting 196 live trait definitions.

✅ **THR-773 — Nudge Model WS0: engine substrate for nudges, band riders, the Broken mortal state, and the god's restore verb** (2026-07-26, Encounter Experience) — The buildable substrate under the THR-772 program pivot: *you nudge the physics, fate picks the outcome*. **The nudge hand** is an additive-optional `StepNudge[]` on `ActionStep` (278-importer type, zero signature changes): authored per-encounter cards, essence-priced, each contributing a **named** `nudge:<id>` forecast modifier into the same additive channel push and the company assist already use. Two **riders** remap the outcome band — `no_crit_fail` (`critical_failure → failure`) and `floor_at_cost` (`failure` **and** `near_miss` → `success_at_cost`, because a near miss is a failure texture and floors with it) — defined over the full six-value `StepOutcome` domain and taking **zero rng draws**; a `reroll_once` rider was designed and **rejected at audit** because any extra draw from the resolution stream shifts every downstream consumer for that action. Riders apply last, after push/resist/both floors, with the pre-rider outcome captured first so `resistSucceeded` is never misattributed to a rider on an action that never resisted. `bandProse` keys on `StepOutcome`, not `OutcomeBand` — the wrong enum would have type-checked. **Scope rule:** nudges exist only in the *attended* encounter, and the predicate is the shipped `AttentionTier === 'story_beat'`, so the tick loop is untouched and this is a player-attention feature rather than a sim feature. **The Broken state** is the first behavioural consumer of the `weakened`/`critical` threshold states, which had shipped since Phase 2 with **none at all** — grinding a mortal down changed a number and nothing else. Erosion is now band-, attention- and difficulty-scaled (attended crit-failure at difficulty 0.6 ≈ **0.48**, the pacing anchor reviewed in chat; background failure stays 0.03) and clamps on the resulting **ratio**, so erosion alone can never reach zero and death stays owned by the existing zero-state paths. The state is derived per read with one stored property (`brokenSince`) carrying hysteresis. **`BROKEN_GATE_ENABLED` ships `false`**: the candidacy exclusion and `broken_drift` movement pull are wired and tested but inert until WS5's rebuild encounters exist — a mortal locked out of every draw with nothing to draw is stun-lock, not story, and *failure is plot, not punishment*. Erosion and bookkeeping run regardless so telemetry accrues first. The **terminology collision is resolved rather than renamed**: the `QuintessenceThresholdState` literal `'broken'` (ratio 0, dissolution) is a 232-importer enum left untouched; the player-facing Broken *mortal state* is behavioural and begins far above zero, and the UL proposal defines both. **`divine.rekindle_thread`** ("The Lent Fire", Spirit) adds the `quintessence_restore` primitive — restores, clears the stamp, and leaves a `recent_event` receipt naming the god, because the mortal knowing whose fire is in them is the Two-Way Thread, not a side effect. It is deliberately **not** a graph-executor case (the switch's default carries a comment saying why): it needs full `GameState` for that receipt, so it rides the resolution-intercept path with `plant_trap`/`reveal_secret`. It is granted by a new `beat.milestone.the_guttering_thread` firing the first time a *threaded* mortal breaks — the card's own precondition, mirroring `the_empty_road` → Reunite — so it is reachable rather than an orphaned card, and the god meets the consequence before being handed the answer to it. **The bug the suite could not have caught:** a live 160-tick seed-42 run showed **0 broken agents where 2 were expected**. `WorldGraph.updateNode` *replaces* the map entry (fresh object **and** fresh `properties`) rather than mutating in place, so the node handle held across `phaseQuintessence`'s own passive-regen write was stale and every stamp was silently dropped. `reconcileBrokenState` now takes an **id** and re-reads; four regression tests pin it, one asserting the stale handle is provably a different object. Re-measured: 2 stamped agents, 0.36% of living mortals (kill criterion is >5%), and the hysteresis visibly correct — Tatter stamped at t157 crossing below 0.10, still stamped at 0.106 while climbing back, releasing only at 0.25. Also shipped: pure `classifyMotive` over the THR-631 receipt, three traces (`nudge_played` per-play; `agent_broken`/`agent_mended` **transition-only**), `__DEBUG.getEncounterNudges` / `getBrokenAgents`, 17 named constants, the rulebook's two `[DESIGN]` sections plus the **named** amendment to "Critical failure survives at every scale", wiring-guide Capability 14, and six wiki pages. Two interface contracts registered **deliberately not LIVE, each saying why**: `authored-nudge-hand-reaches-resolution` 🔴 LEAKED (no shipped template authors a hand yet — WS1) and `quintessence-threshold-gates-candidacy-and-movement` 🟠 PARTIAL (gate inert behind its flag — WS5); badging either LIVE would claim behaviour no run can produce. Full suite **13296 green (891 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3517 unchanged**; `vite build` 9.64s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events; wiki + generated freshness green. Browser-verify exempt per plan: engine + debug bridge only, no UI surface — WS2 (THR-775) owns the interface.

✅ **THR-761 — aftermath-applied conditions never expired; `apply_condition` wrote `durationTicks`, `decayConditions` reads `ticksRemaining`** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — Two duration fields existed on `has_trait` edges and only one was alive: `decayConditions` (`src/engine/conditionDecay.ts`) is the sole tick-driven expiry path and counts down `ticksRemaining`, while both condition-minting aftermath effects — `apply_condition` and `condition_attachment` in `encounterAftermath.ts` — wrote `durationTicks`, a property with **zero production readers** (the only hits compute it from the effect before writing, read it off a payload in UI code, or report it in a trace, which is what made it look consumed). Every condition applied through aftermath was therefore permanent regardless of its authored duration. Both sites now write `ticksRemaining` alongside `durationTicks`, guarded on `> 0` so the documented `0 = indefinite` contract still works by *omitting* the field and having the decay loop skip the edge. The **regression suite was written first and falsified against the unfixed build** — `conditionExpiry.test.ts` came back 4 of 5 red, the 5th being the indefinite case that correctly survives 40 ticks — and every test drives the real `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` plus the real `decayConditions` tick by tick, asserting the condition is *absent* on its authored tick rather than that the edge was written; the three pre-existing `conditionAttachment.test.ts` duration assertions and one in `encounterAftermath-multi-target.test.ts` asserted exactly that write and passed throughout the defect's life, so they are repointed to bind `ticksRemaining` as well (dead-side test rule). The ticket's open decision is recorded rather than deferred: the fields stay **distinct**, `ticksRemaining` as the live counter and `durationTicks` as the authored total kept for provenance and as the duration-UI progress denominator — documented at both call sites, in the `has_trait` row of `src/types/edgeSchema.ts`, in the systemic wiring guide's conditions section, and on the Encounters & Aftermath wiki page (behavior changed, so the freshness gate was satisfied by updating the page, not by taking the exemption). **The membership sweep narrowed the blast radius, and the honest reading is reported rather than inflated:** across every `type: 'has_trait'` `addEdge` in production only these two were wrong — the THR-719 `action_trigger` payload path, `rewardPool`, `seedAttachments` and `hexActionBridge` already wrote the correct field, and the culture / capability-growth / mentorship / bestowed-power grants are permanent by design — and **no reachable content authors either effect kind today**: the only authored uses are two *unregistered* example templates and five `onFailureEffects` blocks in `tavern-encounter-content.ts` whose enclosing field has no reader anywhere in the repo, so a 155-tick seeded `--auto-aftermath` run and a targeted `tavern.brawl` spawn both mint zero such edges. The mechanism was real and is now sound for the authors the wiring guide directs here; it was not yet producing observable in-play permanence. Full suite **13254 green (890 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3517 unchanged**; `vite build` 9.44s; 30-tick engine smoke reached tick 30 with 387 agents / 48 events; wiki + generated freshness green. Browser-verify exempt: engine + docs change, no UI surface touched (the condition-duration UI defect found on the way is filed, not fixed here). Filed **THR-783** (`onFailureEffects` has zero readers — five tavern step-failure wound applications never fire) and **THR-784** (condition duration UI reads `ticksRemaining` off the trait node instead of the edge, so the countdown never moves).

✅ **THR-760 — CLAUDE.md slimming pass, dedupe always-loaded context** (2026-07-26, Continuous Improvement) — The project `CLAUDE.md` loads into every session, including ~24 automated ones a day, and had grown to **90,292 chars (~22.6k tokens)** while the progressive-disclosure surfaces it was supposed to defer to (canon pages, skills, generated docs) were built around it. Five relocations, **not deletions** — every rule keeps exactly one authoritative home and CLAUDE.md keeps the pointer: the scheduled-task registry (four lane tables, reaper guardrails, slot-allocation policy, prompt-mirror rule) → `Docs/ops/scheduled-tasks-registry.md`; Design Governance (workflow checklist Steps 0–8.6 + Per-system required sections + maintenance bullets) → new canon page `Docs/canon/design-governance.md`; the Obsidian-vault KB detail (three layers, workflows, maintenance scripts, frontmatter conventions) → `Docs/documentation-ownership.md`, already the declared ownership authority; the 40-row Domain Skills table → an 8-line **load-order routing policy**, since each skill's own `description:` carries its triggers and the harness lists all of them every session; and the ~90-line Debug Bridge example block → a pointer at `src/debug-bridge.d.ts` plus a capability index, keeping the `__DEBUG.tick(n)` block in full because the `document.hidden` throttling rationale is a gotcha, not API docs. **Result: 63,226 chars (~15.8k tokens) — −27,066 chars, 30% off every session.** The **authority direction flipped** for one row: `Docs/canon/process.md` was a pointer page whose targets were CLAUDE.md sections, and Design Governance now points the other way (recorded in-page so a future session does not "fix" it back); the canon-split path was taken over inlining because `process.md` was already 103 lines against a ~200-line budget. Deleting the Debug Bridge examples was **gated per-API on the behavioral note surviving as JSDoc** — most already did (`fireAction` partial matching, `pickAftermathReaction` defaulting, `getArmies` being an unfiltered ground-truth read), and four groups that did not (tracing trio, health/crash trio, encounter-log export pair, plus a `listActions` comment that had drifted above the wrong declaration) had it **added** in `src/debug-bridge.d.ts` in the same PR. The reference sweep is the change's only real wiring and was run as specified including the line-by-line backstop: every surviving `CLAUDE.md` reference in `.claude/skills/` and `Docs/canon/` resolves to a **kept** section, and four stale pointers in the `weekly-project-hygiene` / `weekly-workflow-retro` scheduled-task prompts were repointed **in both the live prompt and its repo mirror together**, per the prompt-mirror rule. The global `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md` (71 lines of generic "reduce LLM coding mistakes" guidance, whose "if uncertain, ask" default actively conflicts with unattended lanes) was archived verbatim to `Docs/ops/retired-global-claude-md-2026-07-25.md` **before** deletion, after re-verifying it named no project. **Honest miss recorded rather than papered over:** the plan's `≤58,000 chars` Done-when was an absolute target against a baseline that drifted +3,039 chars overnight; every sanctioned cut landed and the plan explicitly forbids cutting kept sections to close the rest, so the residual ~2.2k gap is baseline drift plus the do-not-touch floor (impediment #213 — state size targets as deltas, not absolutes). Full suite **13249 green (889 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3517 unchanged**; `vite build` 12.60s; wiki + generated freshness green. Engine smoke N/A (no engine file touched — `.d.ts` comments only). Browser-verify exempt: docs + comment-only change.

✅ **THR-780 — aftermath reactions double-applied their graph mutations under StrictMode** (2026-07-26, Action System & Unlocks) — `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` mutates the live `WorldGraph` in place but was called from inside a `setGameState` updater, which `<StrictMode>` double-invokes in dev; accumulating effects landed twice (a 0.35 tally moved 0.70) while idempotent ones deduped and looked correct. Moved the call out of the updater to `resolveBeat`'s existing compute-then-commit shape, retiring the THR-114/THR-133 read-side workarounds. Regression guard falsified against the pre-fix build; live A/B 2×→1× on tally delta and trace counts. Filed THR-781 (pre-existing duplicate `faction_decay_rank` event ids).

✅ **THR-741 — aftermath reactions for the destructive player-castable cards** (2026-07-26, Action System & Unlocks) — The Divine Receipt's player choice row (THR-727) shipped live but almost always empty: only the six `invest.*` reach signatures authored an `aftermathConfig`, so every other cast resolved to a receipt with nothing to choose. New content module `src/data/divine-working-aftermath.ts` authors **12 reactions across the first tranche** — `hex.rend_earth`, `hex.corrupt_land`, `hex.scorch_earth`, `artifact.curse`, `loc.incite_unrest` — each set offering the same fork in the card's own idiom: **claim** the working (full-weight reach-polarity `reputation_tally` on the ascendant + a chronicle-significance `recent_event`) or **conceal** it (muted tally, quiet event, and a `concealed_action` `hidden_mark` whose `revealFamilies` are the other divine-working prefixes, so a *later* working is what surfaces the one you hid). Scorch Earth and Incite Unrest add a third, *positive*-polarity reading of the same act — burning as a shield rather than a warning, unrest as a presence behind the grievance — and polarity competes inside `phaseReputationTraits`, so the two claims are mutually exclusive over repeated casts (Feared Champion **or** Brutal Thug), a real fork rather than a flavour toggle. Six tuning constants named per NFP #1. **Zero engine work, verified rather than assumed:** the path is template-agnostic end to end (`aftermathConfig` → `resolveAftermathVariant` → `aftermathSummary.reactions` → `phasePlayerReceipts` → `DivineReceiptModal` → `applyAftermathReactionForAgent(gameState.ascendantId)`), and every candidate effect kind was traced to a live consumer **for an ascendant actor** before use — tallies to `phaseReputationTraits` (which scans `getNodesByType('actor')`, and the ascendant is `type: 'actor'` / `actorType: 'ascendant'`, so it qualifies), marks to `evaluateMarkReveals`/`consumeMatchingMarks` (prefix-matching `templateId.startsWith(family)`), events to `recentEvents`/`tickEvents`. **`intelligence` was dropped from the vocabulary despite the shipped `invest.*` precedent**: its only player-facing surface is `ThreadDetailView`, gated to agent threads, so a record granted to the god is written and unreadable — the ticket's "every effect must have a live consumer" is what excluded it. Adding reactions also lifts all five to the modal receipt tier (`decidePresentation` returns `'modal'` when `reactionCount > 0`), which is what puts the row in front of the player. **Found on the way and filed, not fixed: THR-780** — aftermath reactions double-apply their graph mutations in dev. `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` mutates the live `WorldGraph` inside a `setGameState(prev => …)` updater and `src/main.tsx` wraps the app in `<StrictMode>`, which double-invokes updaters in development; live browser showed `shadow.negative` moving `0.92 → 1.62` (`+0.70`) against an authored `0.35`, while the deterministic-`markId` mark deduped and looked correct — silent and uneven, dev-only, and pre-existing for every tally-carrying reaction (THR-741 is simply the first player-cast card whose reactions carry one). The blocking wiki gate was satisfied by **extending** `action-catalog` with an `aftermathReactionCount` field (schema 2 → 3, drawer row "Aftermath choices", generator test asserting parity with the registry) rather than taking `Wiki-freshness-exempt:`, since the change is behavior-affecting. Prose: register compliance **pass** on all five entries; the entries' `fail` band comes from pre-existing `static-string` flags on their own `description`/`narrative` fields, and the new `aftermath.fallback` overviews added no flags. Live browser (seeded, medium, tick 6): fired `loc.incite_unrest`, receipt queued at `presentation: 'modal'` carrying all three authored labels in the DOM; applying `incite_unrest_conceal` dispatched `hidden_mark` + `reputation_tally` + `recent_event` with `success: true` and planted the mark with its three reveal families; console clean. Full suite **13242 green (888 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3519 unchanged**; `vite build` 9.78s; 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 48 events); generated + wiki freshness green. Screenshot not obtainable — the Browser pane does not composite frames in a headless scheduled session (impediment class #199, codification tracked by THR-754); DOM assertion + `__DEBUG` state assertion stand in.

✅ **THR-776 — Nudge Model WS3 migration audit** (2026-07-26, Encounter Experience) — Classified every mortal-drawable encounter template into KEEP / REWRITE / KILL, producing `Docs/audits/2026-07-26-nudge-migration-audit.md`: **561 templates — 282 KEEP (50%), 214 REWRITE (38%), 65 KILL (12%)**, with per-family worklists carrying id, verdict, one-line reason, WS4 scene-image tag query and an S/M/L nudge-hand estimate. **The scope figure is corrected in the audit rather than transcribed:** the ticket's "531 mortal-drawable + 28 branching + 30 scenes" double-counts the branching set, since all 28 `LOCATION_BRANCHING_ENCOUNTER_TEMPLATES` entries also live in `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` — the honest de-duplicated corpus is 503 + 28 + 30 = **561**. The 531 reproduced exactly from the membership predicate, which is what confirms the predicate matches the one the design session ran. Verdicts come from an 11-rule ordered chain: the shipped scorers unmodified (`scoreProseEntry` with fields extracted exactly as `collectAuthoredProse.collectEncounters` builds them, so bands are comparable to the Prose QA tab, plus `scoreRegisterCompliance` from THR-609) and four scratch detectors specified in the doc's appendix so WS5 can re-run them (abstract-noun-suffix density ≥4.5/100w, vagueness lexicon ≥2.0/100w, not-X-but-Y ≥2, second-person-on-mortal-template ≥2; every threshold a named constant). The abstraction detectors sweep the **full** authored text including step narratives and afterimages, which the shipped collector does not reach — deliberate, since step prose is the bulk of what a player reads and where the abstraction problem lives. **Four findings reshape WS5.** (1) *Exposure is ~10:1 concentrated.* Headless seed-42 medium runs (`tick 120`, `tick 360` stopping at 166 on the twilight phase change) resolved 93 actions: **85 `encounter.*`, 4 `reputation.*`, 1 each `stone`/`eye`/`star`/`veil` — 91%** in one family, while guild (166 templates), `npc_*` (16), `social` (14), `tavern` (10) and `borderland` (20) drew **zero** across 166 ticks with 368 agents. Batch order follows: `encounter.*` REWRITE (48), then its KEEP set (133, hands only), then branching, then scenes, then the low-exposure tail. One seed is weak evidence of *never* but strong evidence of *low share*, and the doc says so. (2) *61 templates have no draw path.* Reachability was checked against all three agent-facing paths before anything was called orphaned — array-scored (`generateUnifiedCandidates`, which skips `cosmic`/`regional`), encounter-cache (scale-agnostic), and seeded-family (`encounterSeeding.matchFamily`, also scale-agnostic, gating on `individual` affinity + one of 47 planted `encounterFamily` prefixes). A first pass that gated on scale alone was wrong and was corrected after finding the seed path. 44 are `action.*` verbs carrying `['individual','faction']` — not player cards either, since player-castable requires `'ascendant'` (THR-659), and the faction phase dispatches from its own `FACTION_ACTION_TEMPLATES` map in `phaseFactionActions.ts`, not this array. The remaining 17 are the `*.senior.*`/`*.elite.*` guild tier plus `fa.*` and `monster.encounter.*`: **the reward tier of guild progression is unreachable** — an agent can rank up and never see what the rank unlocks. Orphan-KILL is explicitly scoped as "author no nudges here", not "delete"; the wire-or-delete decision is **THR-779** and gates the kill list. (3) *40 mortal-drawn templates are narrated to the god.* `npc_ask_information` reads *"You lean on their thoughts, parting the curtain of suspicion"* while carrying `actorAffinities: ['individual']` at `scale: 'personal'` — drawn by a mortal, addressed to a deity. Concentrated in `npc_*` ×16 and `ag.*` ×12. A speaker bug rather than a style preference: under the Nudge Model the god acts *through* the hand and the scene prose stays with the mortal, so each needs its narrator swapped during the rewrite. (4) *The predicate's "affinities absent" branch is dead* — zero shipped templates lack `actorAffinities` (THR-736 locked it with a registry invariant), so every corpus member qualifies by naming a mortal affinity and WS5 should not expect an opt-out tail. Known limits are stated rather than hidden: the abstract-noun proxy is suffix-based and penalises domain vocabulary (`devotion`, `judgement`), so it ranks rather than verdicts and every REWRITE it alone produces is a light pass; redundancy matching is within-family on normalised name+description only. Doc-only PR, harness kept in gitignored `.cache/`. Full suite **13232 green (887 files)**; typecheck ratchet **3519 unchanged**; `vite build` 9.72s; wiki-freshness blocking green. Engine smoke and browser-verify N/A (read-only audit, no code changed).

✅ **THR-736 — 18 shipped action templates carried no `actorAffinities`** (2026-07-26, Content Architecture) — `actorAffinities` is declared required on `UnifiedActionTemplate`, but 15 `mc.*` (mercenary-company) and 3 `encounter.shell_proof.*` templates shipped without it. **The consequence was the opposite of the intuitive one:** `generateUnifiedCandidates` gates on `if (template.actorAffinities && template.actorAffinities.length > 0)`, so an *absent* list skips the actor-type check entirely — those 18 were drawable by **every** `ActorType` (faction, culture, group, place-spirit, god), not excluded from candidacy. All 18 now declare `['individual']`, chosen per-template rather than mass-defaulted: the `mc.*` set is mercenary-company content a mortal joins and works (`mc.join`, `mc.quest.*`, `mc.senior.*`, `mc.elite.*`, `mc.social.*`, `mc.promotion`), and the proof pack is `source: 'agent'` / `targetCategory: 'self'` / personal-scale by construction. Net effect **narrows** candidacy. Verified the three proof-pack templates cannot be swept into group-eligibility: their reaches are `resolve`/`resolve`/`combat` (neither a `PHYSICAL_CHALLENGE_REACH` nor a `COMPANY_EXCLUDED_REACH`) and two are single-step, below `GROUP_ELIGIBLE_MIN_STEPS`. **The ticket's Done-when predicted a typecheck decrease; it does not occur, and the reason is the finding.** The ratchet held at **3519 unchanged**, with per-file counts identical before and after (`mercenary-encounter-content.ts` 46, `effect-shell-proof-templates.ts` 7) — these object literals already fail assignability on other properties (`reach: 'resolve'` is not a `ReachDomain`), and TypeScript reports the property-level mismatch rather than a separate missing-property error; the same masking hides absent `apCost`/`crudType` on the proof pack. The required-field violation was therefore invisible to `tsc` and always would have been, which makes the **runtime registry invariant** the only real gate. That test asserts the *list of offending ids* equals `[]`, deliberately not `.every(...)`: `.every()` on an empty array is vacuously true, so its predecessor (`malformed.every(t => /^(mc\.|encounter\.shell_proof\.)/.test(t.id))`) would have passed unconditionally the moment the set emptied — a guard that dies exactly when it starts mattering. Falsified by deleting one field and confirming the failure names `encounter.shell_proof.fate_card_trial` before trusting it. The `Array.isArray` guard in `groupEligibility.ts` simplified to `?? []`, keeping NFP #4 fail-soft for future content while dropping the "18 shipped rows" justification. Handoff mutex vs THR-74 PR 2b cleared by its own stated terms (THR-74 Done 2026-07-25; #790/#795/#807/#813/#815/#817/#819 all merged), recorded on the issue. Wiki page `encounters-manual-reference.html` updated in the same PR (its `sources` glob matches the changed content files) rather than taking the `Wiki-freshness-exempt` hatch, since the change is behavior-affecting. Full suite **13219 green (886 files)**; `vite build` 9.45s; 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 48 events); generated + wiki freshness green. Browser-verify exempt: engine/content data change, no UI-pillar surface touched.

✅ **THR-728 — Player-cast outcome variance with a safety floor** (2026-07-25, Action System & Unlocks) — `resolveUncontestedStep` returned `{ outcome: 'success', probability: 1 }` for every `source === 'player'` step before any capability, difficulty or shaper was consulted, so the Divine Receipt (THR-727) could only report a foregone conclusion, and the authored step difficulties on **84 of 138** ascendant-castable templates were computed and discarded. Per the user verdict (chat 2026-07-24, "yes, with a safety floor") casts now roll the shared sigmoid→d100 ladder, floored at `success_at_cost` — the working always lands (`isStepSuccess('success_at_cost')` is true, so `onSuccess` ops run) but the world can charge for the holding; `setback`/`catastrophe` are unreachable by construction and the upside is untouched. Zero-difficulty templates (all `divine.*` soul-verbs, `invest.*` signatures, observation) stay guaranteed as a thematic statement; push and resist stay mortal. The player floor is stacked **after** the THR-571 scale floor, not merged with it, so `[floor↑]` ("the incapable scraped through") and `[player-floor↑]` ("the god cannot fail") stay distinguishable — the latter registered as `playerFloorApplied` on `ResolutionInputTrace`. **The plan's single flagged grey zone was the whole feature.** `computeCapability(graph, ascendantId, reach)` returns **0.018** for every god at every reach: the ascendant node carries `domainAffinities` (2–5, the *ranking* weight `rankedAffinityReaches` uses to pick its two permanent reaches) and never `domainCapabilities` (10–40, what `generateDomainCapabilities` gives mortals on the raw scale the sigmoid consumes). Shipping the plan literally would have floored **94%** of casts onto one band (400-seed measurement) — a *flatter* receipt than the auto-success it replaced. The player path now maps affinity onto the raw scale (`ascendantCastRawBonus` → new `computeCapabilityWithRawBonus`); this is deliberately **not** folded into `computeRawScore`, because that score is what THR-613's Deepening tier-crossing beats are tuned against, and a test pins the separation. A first attempt that *did* fold it in was reverted for exactly that reason. Verified in the live seeded browser world against real shipped templates: `hex.survey` (eye 0.1) 66% strained / 25% clean / 7% fortunate / 2% surge, `hex.corrupt_land` (veil 0.4) 79/14/6/1, `loc.ward` 79/14/6/1, `divine.coincidence` 300/300 clean success, and **zero** `failure`/`critical_failure` across every sample; the live ascendant read `{eye:4, veil:3, shadow:2}` with cast capability 0.31/0.27/0.23 and 0.17 off-domain, so primary outranks secondary outranks off-domain. **UI:** additive `WheelSlot.maxStepDifficulty` (branch-step-safe) drives one prose line on the focused card — *"A perilous working."* / *"An uncertain working."* / *"A steady working."*, no percentages anywhere — and a guaranteed casting shows nothing, keeping its unchanged face. No change to `DivineReceiptModal`, `receipt-content.ts` or band content: all six frame lines were pre-authored and simply began to vary. **One shipped test assertion changed** — `bind_thread_location` (cosmic, difficulty 0.2) asserted `outcome === 'success'`, which encoded the removed rule; it now asserts the success *band*, since the thread edge is the test's real subject. Full suite **13115 green (881 files)** incl. 11 new; typecheck ratchet **3519 unchanged**; `vite build` 9.16s; 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events); wiki-freshness blocking green after correcting all three matched pages. Browser-verified @1920×1080 (Claude-in-Chrome): the production `ActionDrawer` rendering a real 0.45-difficulty card with its risk line and a real 0-difficulty card without one, 0 app console errors. Rulebook §4 carries the new rule; two interface-contract rows registered 🟢 LIVE. Reverts with one flag (`PLAYER_CAST_VARIANCE_ENABLED = false`).

✅ **THR-719 — Item on-use triggers as effect primitives** (2026-07-25, Content Architecture) — `src/engine/attachmentTriggers.ts` was a complete, tested on-use resolver with **zero production importers**: a sword whose tooltip promised it snapped on a critical failure had never snapped, and a cursed eye that promised to drain resolve had never drained. Per the user verdict (chat review 2026-07-23) it was **deleted, not adopted** — wiring it would have recreated a parallel resolution path beside the `effects[]` architecture, the exact drift class the interface map exists to kill. **Engine:** the production-wired `action_trigger` primitive (TB-104) extended four ways — outcome-band events (`encounter_critical_success`/`encounter_at_cost`/`encounter_critical_failure`) produced by a new `ladderEventsFor(outcome)` that maps the six-band `StepOutcome` ladder **by widening, not partitioning** (a critical success raises its own band *and* `encounter_success`, so the shipped Battle Spoils Talisman keeps its exact prior firing set); an optional `probability` guard rolled from a caller-supplied seeded stream (never `Math.random`) and evaluated **after** every cheap eligibility gate so an on-cooldown or fires-exhausted trigger never consumes a draw and perturbs reproducibility; an optional `narrativeTemplate` with the legacy `{actor}`/`{item_name}`/`{target}`/`{location}` tokens; and three item-behavior payload kinds. New `src/engine/effects/actionTriggerPayloads.ts` applies the graph-affecting payloads so the fire-check stays pure, wired at all three trigger call sites (`unifiedActionResolution.ts` — which also does the ladder mapping and surfaces fired prose as an `EncounterAftermathChange` — plus `orchestrator.ts` and `phaseMovement.ts`). **Payload kinds are derived from content, not guessed:** the plan predicted `condition_grant`/`self_remove`/`possession_grant`/`relationship_delta`; enumerating the nine authored blocks showed `possession_grant` and `relationship_delta` unused (dropped — unused vocabulary is the disease) and `condition_remove` used twice (added). **A latent bug caught by the port:** `decayConditions` counts down a `has_trait` edge's `ticksRemaining`, while `apply_condition` writes `durationTicks`, which has **zero edge-property readers** — granting on the plan's implied field would have minted conditions that never expire, the same silent-death class the ticket exists to end. The applier writes `ticksRemaining`, asserted in two tests. **Content:** all nine `onUseTriggers` blocks ported verbatim across `starter-attachments.ts` (3) and `anomaly-reward-catalog.ts` (6), prose unchanged; `first_use` → `action_complete` + `maxFires: 1`, `any_use` → `action_complete` with `cooldownTicks: 0` to preserve every-use rolling. Two grants that carried inline modifier bags instead of a node id were bound to real condition nodes — the Codex to the already-shipped `starter_revelation` (its `+0.15 star` matched exactly), and the Whispering Eye to a newly authored `starter_drained_resolve`. **UI:** `resolveAttachmentTooltip`, `AttachmentDetailView`, `agentAttachments`, ProwessTab/AttachmentsTab/AgentProfileModal all read `action_trigger` effects; the rendered strings are unchanged (⚡ 25% chance: Critical failure) but are now backed by behavior that fires. **Retirement:** `attachmentTriggers.ts`, its suite, the `onUseTriggers` field and the `OnUseTrigger`/`OnUseTriggerEffect`/`TriggerCondition` types are gone; dead-side test assertions were deleted where they only shape-checked a retired type, and the end-to-end `attachment-lifecycle-integration` test was **ported onto the live path** rather than dropped. Contract `attachment-on-use-triggers` 🔴 LEAKED → 🟢 LIVE with dated `verifiedLive` and both-side symbol evidence; rulebook, systemic wiring guide (new Capability 13), and three wiki pages updated in the same PR. Full suite **13104 green (880 files)** + 17 new tests; typecheck ratchet **3524 → 3519 (DOWN 5**, the deleted legacy code; baseline refreshed); `vite build` 9.71s; generated + wiki freshness green; 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events). Live seeded CLI proof: a Burned Codex handed out by the **reward system** granted `starter_revelation` with `ticksRemaining` visibly decaying, and an Amber Phial fired its authored prose into the aftermath — *“The golden sap burns going down, but the Forren's wounds close like flowers at dusk.”* Browser-verified @1920×1080 (Claude-in-Chrome): both production detail views render their Triggers section from `action_trigger` (Iron Blade ⚡ Critical failure 25% → self remove; Corroded Crown ⚡ Any use 15% → condition grant 25 ticks), 0 console errors, `__DEBUG.tick(40)` clean with 9 ported triggers and the legacy field absent from every catalog item.

✅ **THR-753 — Junction-safe worktree reaping** (2026-07-25, Continuous Improvement) — The hourly host git reaper (`C:/Users/chris/Dev/Projects/clean-stale-git.sh`, not version-controlled) removed worktrees with `git worktree remove --force`, which **follows a `node_modules` junction** into the home tree's single real install and empties it — the 2026-07-22 ×2 wipes and impediments #203/#207, each breaking hooks/dev/tests across every tree until a manual `npm install` repaired the home. **Fix:** new `sever_node_modules_reparse()` runs `cmd rmdir` (no `/s`) on the reparse point *before* every `git worktree remove` and every orphan-dir `rm -rf`, deleting only the link (never the target's contents); it detects a reparse point via git-bash `-L` **or** `fsutil reparsepoint query`, is harmless on a real directory (rmdir fails without deleting), and *refuses* the removal if the sever fails. New `check_home_node_modules()` runs after each removal pass and, if `node_modules/.bin/esbuild.exe` has vanished, logs one loud `HOME-TREE node_modules DAMAGED … REPAIR: run 'npm install'` line — the reaper never auto-installs (a dev action). `WORKTREE_MIN_IDLE_MINUTES` (180, THR-673 liveness guard) left untouched. The non-tracked script's full body is mirrored at `Docs/ops/clean-stale-git.sh.md` for recoverability, and CLAUDE.md §Scheduled Tasks gained a third load-bearing reaper bullet. Verified against a junctioned throwaway worktree: the **guarded** path removed the worktree while the target's `.bin` count stayed 20→20; the **unguarded** `git worktree remove --force` followed the junction and wiped the target 20→0 (the guard is load-bearing); the real home `.bin` was 99 before and after. `bash -n` clean; zero `src/` change; wiki-freshness blocking green. Browser-verify exempt: host-script + docs only.

✅ **THR-738 — Harden linear-autoclose against phantom-Done** (2026-07-25, Continuous Improvement) — The Linear auto-close swept THR-74 to Done ×2–3 on 2026-07-24 from bare/prose references, including a sentence that *documented* the discipline (`Fixes THR-74 still rides the final PR`). **Repo half:** the custom workflow's close-decision (`extractCloseableIssueIds`) is now **line-anchored** — `CLOSE_KEYWORD_PATTERN` = `/^(?:Fixes|Closes|Resolves) (THR-\d+)[ \t]*$/gim`, closing only when a full line reads exactly `Fixes THR-NNN` (trailing whitespace/CRLF ok). Keyword-in-prose, markdown bullets, branch names in merge-commit subjects, and bare `THR-XX` title tokens are now inert (verified against the exact recurrence strings). `extractNativeCloseableIssueIds` (flush-guard, models the native integration) decoupled onto a separate broad `NATIVE_CLOSE_KEYWORD_PATTERN` so the guard stays permissive; `flush-close-guard.yml` was retired (THR-654), leaving those helpers test-only. CLAUDE.md §Merge=Done + DoD and the pull-work skill (closeout + checkpoint guidance) now require the keyword to stand alone and forbid quoting it in prose for a non-closing reference. **Christian-owned half:** vectors 2/3 (branch name, bare title token) provably never came from our workflow — they are Linear's **native** GitHub integration's auto-move-on-merge, disabled via a Linear settings change recorded as `Design/user-actions.md` item 3 (with the live scratch-issue verification, THR-738's remaining Done-when). 29 unit tests (7 new regressions) + 271 scripts tests green; typecheck ratchet 3524 unchanged; `vite build` 9.35s; wiki-freshness blocking green. Browser-verify exempt: CI/docs/scripts only, no UI pillar.

✅ **THR-74 — Company layer: Draw Together** (2026-07-25, Social Systems Expansion, PR 2b part 5) — The second company UAT and the *formation nudge*: the player reaches through one bond (a threaded *anchor* mortal) and every scattered threaded mortal nearby feels the tug to gather. New `draw_together` graph-op stamps a per-mortal convergence field (`convergePullHexCol/Row/UntilTick`) on the anchor + every living, ungrouped, threaded mortal within `DRAW_TOGETHER_RADIUS_HEXES 8` of the anchor's hex; fail-soft on a missing/non-mortal/unthreaded anchor, fires (no refund) with no companions in range. **Verify-the-noun finding:** the prior checkpoint pointed the pull at `movementCandidates.ts`, but that module is *not* the live mover — free mortals choose destinations encounter-driven through `encounterScoring.scoreAndSelect`, so a pull wired only there would have been inert for idle mortals (the Substrate-Honesty trap). The honest read-site is `computeConvergenceBonus` in `encounterScoring.ts` — a per-agent hex-proximity additive score term (up to `DRAW_TOGETHER_PULL_WEIGHT 2.0` at the convergence hex, decaying with distance), sibling of the `hex.mark_ground` attraction term, so each mortal's *own* choices bend toward the anchor (a tilt, never a command). `runFormationScan` attributes a company gathered under an active pull to `cause: 'draw_together'` — the writer for a cause the trace/name generator already accepted but nothing set. `company.draw_together` UAT (Heart/Spirit, essence 4, targets a threaded individual) granted by a new gathering-bonds milestone beat that fires the first time the ascendant threads `DRAW_TOGETHER_MIN_THREADED_FOR_UNLOCK 2` living mortals — unlocking the formation tool *before* any company exists, so the first band can be gathered deliberately; absent from `listUnreachableActions()`. The added `GraphNode` import fixed a pre-existing un-imported-type ref, so the ratchet went **3525 → 3524** (DOWN 1, zero net-new; baseline refreshed). Full suite **13101 green (878 files)**, `vite build` 9.27s, generated + wiki freshness green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events). Browser-verify exempt (engine + content + data + docs, no `src/components`). Remaining THR-74: Seeking Companions firing-path + PR 3 (UI, carries the closing keyword).

✅ **THR-721 — Surface agent ambitions** (2026-07-24, Attention Tier Model) — Ambitions are assigned at worldgen/birth/re-eval, bias encounter choice, and carry motive-receipt provenance, yet the player experienced them as "disappeared from the UI" — the `JourneyTab` gate (`interactionDepth ≥ 2` **or** knowledge ≥ `known`) rarely tripped in normal play, and the Chronicle's Completed Ambitions section was a "will appear here" placeholder. **Engine:** `AMBITION_PRIMARY_INTERACTIONS` 2→1; the hardcoded `'known'` gate string promoted to a named `AMBITION_PRIMARY_KNOWLEDGE = 'recognised'`; new `COMPLETED_AMBITIONS_MAX_DISPLAY = 10`; new pure `getCompletedAmbitions(graph, agentId)` walks completed `pursues` edges (newest-first by `resolvedTick`, display-capped, abandoned/failed excluded — biography not a failure ledger), fail-soft when a template or `resolvedTick` is missing; optional `AgentInfoCardData.completedAmbitions` populated in the same `getAgentInfoCard` pass that builds `intents`. Secondary-ambition gates untouched (verdict 3). **UI:** `JourneyTab` gate now reads the two constants (one meaningful exposure — a dilemma, an observed/social encounter, a divine action, or ~20 colocated ticks — or recognition-level knowledge reveals the primary drive); `ChronicleTab` §Completed Ambitions renders the real list in the tab's existing event idiom (`t{resolvedTick} — {name}`, gold tick accent, tokens only, same gate as primary ambitions), empty state preserved. `AgentDetailPanel.tsx` left untouched (orphaned dead code). **Wiring:** interface rows `ambition-player-visibility` (🟠 PARTIAL → 🟢 LIVE) and `ambition-completed-history` (🔴 LEAKED → 🟢 LIVE) re-badged with dated `verifiedLive` and both-side symbol hits (`getCompletedAmbitions`, `AMBITION_PRIMARY_KNOWLEDGE`); the interface-map projection page updated in the same PR. Full suite **13058 green (873 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green (9.29s), generated-freshness + wiki-freshness green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events). Browser-verify (real Chrome, 1920×1080): live `getAgentInfoCard` on the seeded First (Kael Thornweaver) returns `completedAmbitions` sorted newest-first (t24 Master the Blade → t18 Found a Dynasty) with the active primary "Spread the Faith" still visible; both production tab components rendered against the live graph show the surfaced primary ambition (JourneyTab) and the Completed Ambitions list (ChronicleTab); 0 console errors. Closes via `Fixes THR-721`.

✅ **THR-74 — Company layer: The Parting** (2026-07-24, Social Systems Expansion, PR 2b part 2) — A threaded company's dissolution stops being a silent line in the ledger and becomes an authored moment, told in one of two registers chosen by *how* it ended. New pure `groupParting.ts`: `selectPartingVariant(reason, finalCohesion)` → **bittersweet** when the company parted whole (`goal_complete`, or `undersize`/`leader_death` at cohesion ≥ the fray line) or **bitter** when the bond itself failed (`cohesion_floor`/`betrayal`, or an ambiguous end below the fray line); `composePartingMoment` draws a seeded line from `group-parting-content.ts` (5 bittersweet + 5 bitter, `{company}` token resolved at fire time, "party" never used). New `isGroupThreaded(graph, groupId, ascendantId)` gate — true when the ascendant threads any live member or the leader; read in `runGroupUpkeep` **before** `dissolveGroup` closes the `member_of` edges, surfaced on `DissolutionOutcome.threaded`. `phaseGroups` composes the moment for threaded dissolutions (authored message + `band: variant` + `GROUP_PARTING_EVENT_SIGNIFICANCE 0.72`, from a dedicated `partingRng` stream); untethered companies keep the systemic "goes its separate ways" line. **No new phase, no new GameState field, no trace-union change** (the variant is derivable from `reason`+`finalCohesion`, already on `GroupDissolvedTrace`, sidestepping the emitTrace Omit-collapse quirk). Full suite **13048 green (872 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green (9.23s), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events). Browser-verify exempt (engine + content only — the event feed renders existing TickEvents unchanged). Remaining PR 2b: Seeking Companions (formation-spotlight seam), Shared Spoils / Old Wounds (cohesion-fray drama pool), the two UATs, the rulebook subsection; UI is PR 3. `Fixes THR-74` still rides the final PR.

✅ **THR-718 — Items move capability tiers again** (2026-07-24, Content Architecture) — Every possession-catalog entry wrote `domainContributions: {}`, so `computeRawScore`'s `possesses`/`bonded_to` walk found nothing — a legendary blade made its bearer no mightier, and the `attachment-domain-contributions` contract sat 🔴 LEAKED as a half-finished 2026-04-06 `effects[]` migration. **Engine:** new `StatContributionEffect` (`type:'stat_contribution'`, per-reach `contributions`) in the `AttachmentEffect` union; pure `collectStatContributions(node)` in `effectQueries.ts` (fail-soft — `{}` on none/malformed, skips non-numeric/NaN); an additive term inside `computeRawScore`'s artifact walk feeding the same sigmoid as traits, with the **legacy node-prop read preserved** (traits/resources/old artifacts, NFP #6). One stat substrate — no bare `domainContributions` fills (user verdict). **Content:** `item-stat-bands.ts` bands (`MINOR 0.5`/`NOTABLE 1.0`/`LEGENDARY 2.0` + `MAGNITUDE_DOTS_TOTAL 5`) enforced by a build-failing ceiling + double-dip content test; **9 genuine capability items migrated, each magnitude justified inline** (no mass default — the THR-736 anti-pattern): artifact-templates ×3 legendary, starter ×4, anomaly ×2; `mechanicalSummary` prose updated to name the tier contribution. Mounts/cloaks/consumables/blessings deliberately excluded (movement/one-shot intent, not capability) — remaining reward-catalog sweep tracked as THR-745. **UI:** `StepDots` gains `variant:'magnitude'` (level not progress — filled/dim, no glow; default unchanged for its three consumers); `DomainCard` renders a 5-dot magnitude row (tier 0–4 → 1–5 filled) beside the tier word, reveal-gated, tokens only, aria-labelled. **Wiring:** `attachment-domain-contributions` 🔴 LEAKED → 🟢 LIVE (dated `verifiedLive`, both-side symbols `stat_contribution` + `collectStatContributions`); rulebook + systemic-wiring-guide one-liners. Full suite **13022 green (870 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green, generated-freshness green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 48 events). Browser (real Chrome, 1920×1080): Ascendant Sheet DomainCards render magnitude dots (Iron/Gold 1-filled, Shadow/Veil 2-filled, Eye 3-filled + 2 dim, no glow); 0 console errors; live-graph proof — 3 migrated items spawned onto an agent each carry their authored `stat_contribution` on the `possesses` edges `computeRawScore` walks. Closes via `Fixes THR-718`.
✅ **THR-74 — Company layer: party-exclusive delves** (2026-07-24, Social Systems Expansion, PR 2b part 1) — With the reachability gate on main (#790), the signature party-exclusive content ships: three group-EXCLUSIVE delve templates (`encounter.sunken_vault`, `encounter.broken_span`, `encounter.hollow_watch`) authored `actorAffinities: ['group']` + `minGroupMembers: 2` — no `'individual'`, so a solo agent can never draw them; only a company of ≥2 living members does, through the THR-74 gate. Each is a three-step expedition shaped so different Reaches shine (force/read/haul; anchor/cross/hold; breach/hold/collapse) — best-member substitution puts a different companion in `{actor}` each step while the others assist. A small additive converter override lets `encounter-content.ts` author group-exclusive entries without changing the ~76 individual archetypes. Interface contract `company-gates-exclusive-content-reachability` un-pinned PARTIAL → 🟢 **LIVE**, exercised end-to-end against the real delves (a company at a dungeon draws `encounter.sunken_vault`; a solo agent draws nothing). Full suite **13024 green (869 files)** (one pre-existing "all encounters include individual" invariant generalized to admit group-exclusive content), typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents, 40 events). Remaining PR 2b: the four authored moments (which need firing-path wiring that does not yet exist), the two UATs, and the rulebook subsection; UI is PR 3. `Fixes THR-74` still rides the final PR.

✅ **THR-726 — The world mints ambitions** (2026-07-24, Thematic Pressure & Living World) — Ambition assignment read only a mortal's own snapshot: a sacked hometown, a blighted harvest, a witnessed wonder minted nothing, so the world's best-coupled system never let *your deeds* become *their desires*. **Engine:** `mintAmbitionsFromEvents` rides `ambitionTick`'s existing re-eval cadence (no new phase) — for each free-slot agent it gathers graph-native `encounter_outcome` event nodes within `MINT_LOOKBACK_TICKS` that they `participated_in` (victim/participant by edge role) or witnessed via `occurred_at` at their location, classifies each by tested Reach (`classifyMintEvent`), and offers the `AMBITION_MINTING_RULES` candidates through the **existing `selectAmbitions` personality funnel** so identity still decides (craven flees, proud avenges the same ruin); `MINT_MAX_PER_EVENT` caps avengers per event, `MINT_BASE_CHANCE` gates, one mint per agent per re-eval into free slots only (v1 never displaces a held want). One aggregate `ambition_minted` trace per tick (never per-agent). **Content:** `src/data/ambition-minting-rules.ts` (event-class × relation → template+weight, plus class prose stems) and six new funnel-gated `EVENT_MINTED_AMBITION_TEMPLATES` (Avenge the Wrong / Protect the Home / Flee the Ravaged Land / Rebuild from Ashes / Found Something New / Chase the Wonder) — a separate pool so spontaneous re-eval never assigns them without an event; milestones use agent-self predicates only, because `target_agent_eliminated` treats a missing `$`-ref node as *eliminated* and would auto-complete. **UI/wiring:** provenance rides the `pursues` edge (`mintedByEventId`/`mintedByLabel`); `resolveMintedAmbitionProvenance` (motiveReceipt.ts) overrides the ambition contribution's receipt detail so foreshadowing names the origin — *"vengeance for the bloodshed at Ardenmor Keep."* `findAmbitionTemplateById` resolves the new pool everywhere milestone-eval and agent-detail display look ambitions up. **Substrate finding:** the dormant `REACTIVE_AMBITION_TEMPLATES` were never dispatched — the `triggerEvent`/`skipFilters` fields have no consumer — confirming minting was genuinely absent. Both pre-seeded interface rows (`world-events-mint-ambitions`, `minted-ambition-provenance`) 🟢 LIVE with run evidence (read-site of the first audit-corrected from `ambitionSelection.ts` to the rules module, where the symbols actually live). Full suite **13002 green (868 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green, 30-tick + tick-80 CLI smoke clean (434 agents, 4 organic mints by t80). Live browser (`?view=game&seeded`, tick 155): 7 organic mints with themed templates + provenance labels + priority; 2 stored motive receipts naming their origin event; console clean; screenshot blocked by uncomposited pane, panel-visibility of ambitions is knowledge-gated (THR-721 scope, plan-acknowledged). Closes via `Fixes THR-726`.

✅ **THR-74 - Company layer: engine core** (2026-07-24, Social Systems Expansion, PR 1 of 3) - Threadbare had two social scales, lone agents and faction-scale armies, and nothing in between - so taverns never produced fellowships and the drama engine had no stage for betrayal or bittersweet farewell between companions. `src/engine/groups/` (queries, formation, movement, cohesion, dissolution, names, resolution) + `phaseGroups` at Phase 2.34, deliberately between agent decision and movement execution so a company's agreed heading overwrites members' personal destinations before any of them run - which is what removes the need for any guard inside the individual movement path. Companies form from colocated compatible agents (relationship sentiment incl. `sworn_ally`, shared ambition category, courage complementarity), take a seeded proper name, aggregate members' existing movement candidates into one destination with leader-weighted consensus and dissent detection, move cohesion only on **named events** (never passive drift), and disband into persistent history (`groupStatus:'disbanded'`, every `member_of` edge stamped `leftAtTick` - nothing deleted). **Zero new node types, edge types or GameState fields**, and the company node carries **no `located_at`** - position derives from the leader, so members' edges stay the single spatial truth. Army files untouched. **Three findings the plan did not anticipate:** armies already occupy `actorType:'group'` (discriminated by `groupType` vs `armyState`); a hard death cascades away the membership edge so the death delta was never charged (companies keep a `roster` of ids to keep the loss detectable); and companies form in taverns, which are sublocations outside the adjacency graph, so the first companies sat in the inn forever until travel resolved up through `parentLocationId`. **Two gate-caught regressions:** an unregistered event-id counter broke same-seed id determinism (sim output was byte-identical), and per-tick destination re-decision - a pathfinding sweep per member - timed out two real-tick suites, now gated on `DECISION_REEVALUATION_TICKS`. `GROUP_FORMATION_MIN_COLOCATED` retuned 3->2 against the smoke (only ~17 spotlight agents hold a position across 650+ locations). `member_of` consumer sweep: 14 sites guarded via `getFactionMembershipEdges`, which excludes companies rather than requiring `actorType==='faction'` - the positive form silently dropped real faction memberships. Full suite **12963 green (864 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525**, `vite build` green, generated + wiki freshness green. 72-tick CLI smoke: first company forms t31 ("The Watch of the Nameless Road"), three travelling by t72 with members colocated. Content (PR 2) and UI (PR 3) follow; `Fixes THR-74` rides the final PR.

✅ **THR-727 — Divine Receipt: resolution-time outcome feedback for player action cards** (2026-07-23, Action System & Unlocks) — Playing an action card was the core player verb with no consequence surface: the engine assembled a full `aftermathSummary` for every resolved player cast in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` and discarded it — the resolution events carried no `notification`, player successes scored ~0.6 against the 0.8 chronicle threshold, and the encounter-aftermath modal path only fired for threaded agents. Closes canon rule 6's missing link (player action → visible consequence → next decision). **Engine:** new `src/engine/playerReceipts.ts` + `phasePlayerReceipts` (`post-resolution` slot, disjoint from `phaseAutonomousAftermath` which filters to non-player actors) scans resolved `source==='player'` actions not yet receipted, excludes Ascendant Beat templates (id-set membership in `ASCENDANT_POOL_BEAT_TEMPLATES` — beats keep their own modal), builds a `PlayerActionReceipt`, tiers it (modal when steps ≥ `RECEIPT_MODAL_MIN_STEPS`, rarity ≥ Mythic, a change kind in `RECEIPT_MODAL_CHANGE_KINDS`, or authored reactions present; else toast), caps the queue at `RECEIPT_QUEUE_MAX` drop-oldest (headless safety), marks `playerReceiptEmitted` for idempotency (mirrors `autonomousAftermathApplied`), and emits a `player_action_receipt` TickEvent (+ optional `TickEvent.band`) and `player_receipt` traces. **Content:** `src/data/receipt-content.ts` — six named constants + `RECEIPT_FRAME_LINES` (band-keyed framing pools, player-as-god register, deterministic `actionId`-hash selection, no PRNG). Dispatch copy on both paths switched `consequenceMessage.success` → `narrativeTemplates.initiation` and the non-agent path in GameView gained the toast it lacked. **UI:** new `DivineReceiptModal.tsx` (art header via a shared `getActionArt` extracted from `ActionCard` — no third art map; band-styled outcome word, framing line + enriched overview, polarity-coloured changes, woven technical sentence — no key:value chips; reaction buttons reusing `applyAftermathReactionForAgent(ascendantId,…)`; Acknowledge). Registered in GameView `interruptModalOpen` **and** `getDebugOpenModals` (THR-668), toast click-through via a new `{kind:'receipt'}` NavigationTarget, `notificationRouter` band passthrough + `player_action_receipt→'divine'` category, `__DEBUG.listPlayerReceipts()`. Three new interface-contract rows (🔵 UNVERIFIED-OK). 15 new tests; phaseRegistry equivalence baseline updated for the new phase. Full suite **12903 green (861 files)**, typecheck ratchet **3525 = 3525** (zero net-new), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (387 agents), browser-verified (Claude-in-Chrome): fired `divine.afflict_bless` as a player cast → modal auto-opened + sim auto-paused, rendered art/band-word/framing/changes/technical sentence, Acknowledge closed it + marked acknowledged, and the modal-tier event landed in the Chronicle. Outcome variance for player casts deferred to THR-728 (blocked by this). Closes via `Fixes THR-727`.

✅ **THR-725 — Economy answers the god: economic context in encounter scoring/seeding** (2026-07-23, M3 Dynamic Economy) — Closed player-loop link 4: four granted verbs (`bless_harvest`/`blight`/`open_markets`/`reveal_vein`) moved prosperity and the world said nothing back, because `encounterScoring.ts` held zero economic terms.
