✅ **THR-614 — War system: `cohesion` rename, seam 2 of 4 — the FINAL seam; the four-seam re-scope is COMPLETE** (2026-07-18, Thematic Pressure & Living World) — Ended the long-standing UL collision where the code overloaded the word "quintessence" for two unrelated concepts: cosmological Quintessence (an entity's narrative centrality / threadbare-ness) and army health/quality. Renamed the latter to **cohesion** across the whole TB-073 army/battle/siege surface — `ArmyState.cohesion`/`cohesionMax`, `ARMY_COHESION_BASE`, `COHESION_THRESHOLDS`, the army-attrition trace fields (`cohesionBefore`/`cohesionAfter`), the `__DEBUG.getArmies()` bridge fields (`cohesion`/`cohesionMax`/`cohesionPct` + `.d.ts` return type), the DebugPanel Armies-tab reads + "Cohesion:" label, ~10 army/battle/siege test files, the W14 `armies-battles-reference.html` manual page (code symbols + the collision note reframed as resolved), and the `getArmies` doc line in CLAUDE.md. The rename was applied with a word-boundary sed scoped to army-only files so the shared `quintessenceDelta` DigestEntry field and all cosmological quintessence (`phaseQuintessence`, agent/ascendant quintessence in `unifiedActionResolution`/`orchestrator`/`GameView`/`ThreadDetailView`) were left untouched — the one surgical shared-file edit was orchestrator's Army-Attrition phase comment. **Verification:** full suite **12473 green (827 files)**, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` net-new 0 (proven by diffing `tsc -b --force` with/without the change — identical 3542-error baseline; the only deltas were three pre-existing baseline errors with the identifier renamed), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Browser-verified from headless CC in the seeded medium world: `__DEBUG.getArmies()` returns the renamed `cohesion`/`cohesionMax`/`cohesionPct` fields populated (Iron Wolves host 100/100/100) with **no** `quintessence` key, and the DebugPanel Armies tab renders "Cohesion: 100.0 / 100 (100%)"; 0 console errors (WebGL screenshot times out from headless CC — the `__DEBUG` readout + DOM innerText snippet are the artifact per the viewport contract). With seam 2 landed, all four seams (1 activation, 2 cohesion rename, 3 inspectability, 4 plan+rulebook reconciliation) are complete — **THR-614 closes via the keyword on this PR.**

✅ **THR-614 — War system reconciliation, seam 4 of 4 (plan-doc + rulebook)** (2026-07-18, Thematic Pressure & Living World — docs-only closeout of the re-scoped war activation) — Reconciled the documentation layer with the *shipped* reality that seams 1/3 activated. **Rulebook:** added a new §9 "The World at War" to `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` (which previously had **zero** army/battle/siege/war mentions) — a rules-of-play synthesis written from the player's seat with a verified `[IMPL]`-flagged claim on every mechanic: armies as ordinary `actor`+`armyState` nodes (`commanded_by`/`member_of`/`located_at` edges, no bespoke node type); the `revenge`/`territorial_expansion` spawn triggers and Iron/Gold tier gates; cohesion attrition and the four ordered threshold encounters (supply-crisis→desertion→mutiny→disbandment); momentum battles resolving on `BATTLE_RESOLUTION_THRESHOLD`; sieges; spotlight-encounter-as-intervention with thread-gated POV (`hasThreadToBattle`); and `minor/major/total` scaled destruction. It also records three honest gaps rather than papering over them — the dead `territorial_expansion` prosperity gate (so `revenge` is the only live trigger today), the still-pending `quintessence`→`cohesion` rename (seam 2), and the plan-vs-shipped deviation (the plan said "no new orchestrator battle phase" but war ships in dedicated phases 2.352–2.358). Renumbered the former §9 "Winning and Losing" → §10 (no internal refs pointed at §9). **Plan doc:** added a prominent Implementation-Status banner to the green-field `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-autonomous-notables.md` and flipped its frontmatter `status` — a reader is no longer misled into thinking the ~3,300-line TB-073 system is unbuilt. **Downstream corrections:** posted substrate-correction comments on the three Todo phase issues, each grep-verified — THR-628 (all 9 battle spotlight templates + fortification/tactical modifier constants already ship; the referenced `conflict-config.ts` does not exist), THR-629 (`battleType:'siege'` + every `SIEGE_*` constant + the 695-line `siegeResolution.ts` already exist — sieges are substantially built, not green-field), THR-630 (refugee/ruins aftermath already built in `battleAftermath.ts`; only the four non-military notable-agenda families are genuinely unbuilt). Directly serves the substrate-visibility initiative (THR-658). **Verification:** docs-only (no `src/` touched) → `test`/`tsc`/`vite build`/engine-smoke all N/A and unaffected; browser-verify exempt. Only **seam 2 (`cohesion` rename)** now remains; issue THR-614 kept **In Dev** (keyword-free).

✅ **THR-614 — Activate the existing war system, seam 1 of 4 (activation/tuning)** (2026-07-18, Thematic Pressure & Living World — re-scoped from "build autonomous notables war" after the 2026-07-17 executor checkpoint verified the ~3,300-line TB-073 army/battle/siege system was already built + wired but dormant) — Empirical CLI diagnosis (seed 42 medium) found the entire ambition→army→march→battle pipeline reachable but never firing: the *only* armies in a normal run were the 2 seed-time mercenary companies (which auto-siege their colocated settlement and drain out by ~tick 45), after which zero armies/battles for the rest of the run. Three engine bugs (all fixed additively, no new node/edge types): **(1) one-shot spawn** — `phaseFactionAmbitions` attempted army spawn only at the instant an ambition was *created*, so a faction that became spawn-eligible while already holding a standing military ambition (seed-42's "The Civic Guard": revenge ambition, Gold tier 10, Iron-9 commander, `isEligibleForArmySpawn` true) never raised one; extracted `maybeSpawnArmy` now fires for both new and standing military ambitions (the `MAX_ARMIES_PER_FACTION` cap makes the retry a no-op once an army exists). **(2) null objective** — `spawnArmy` created armies with `objective: null`; `phaseArmyMovement` skips null-objective armies, so an ambition army sat at its muster point forever (only the mercenaries, spawned atop a hostile settlement, ever fought). New `selectArmyObjective` marches a fresh army to the nearest reachable settlement controlled by a hostile faction (deterministic hop-count ranking, lexicographic tie-break; fail-soft to idle when none reachable). **(3) sublocation muster** — the commander stood in a *sublocation* (a gatehouse room, which carries no location-adjacency edges), so both objective selection and pathfinding failed from there; `spawnArmy` now resolves the muster point up to its `parentLocationId` settlement (three-tier model). Result on the standard seed 42 medium: the Civic Guard raises an army @tick 10 that besieges the hostile city it garrisons ("The Bone Coast", controlled by `faction_1`) with a resolved siege @tick 11 — meeting the exit criterion (army raised + battle resolved within ~30 ticks); a 120-tick run stays bounded at peak 3 concurrent armies/battles (no runaway escalation). Gate tuning deferred: `ARMY_SPAWN_IRON_TIER_MIN` left at 4 (loosening to 3 was a no-op given the bimodal iron-tier distribution; 2 pushed seed 99 to constant war), and the dead `territorial_expansion` prosperity gate (reads a `prosperity` property never set on faction nodes) is a latent bug flagged for a deliberate balance slice. **Verification:** full suite **12473 green (827 files)**, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` net-new 0 (only pre-existing baseline errors — the `faction_ambition` trace category, dead `commandEdges`, unused test imports — remain), 30-tick CLI smoke reached tick 30 with 389 agents, and a CLI `eval` confirmed the ambition army + siege live at tick 15. Engine/types-only → browser-verify exempt (no `src/components/`, `src/views/`, or HexMap files touched; the war is now visible through the already-built `ArmySpriteMesh`/`BattleIndicatorMesh`/`ArmySheet` surfaces). Issue kept **In Dev** (keyword-free PR) — seams 2 (`cohesion` rename), 3 (DebugPanel Armies tab + `__DEBUG.getArmies()/getBattles()`), and 4 (plan-doc + rulebook reconciliation) remain for follow-up runs.

✅ **THR-613 — Player Action Progression v1, Slice 3b-tail: Codex three-state incarnation grammar** (2026-07-18, Action System & Unlocks — the final slice of the multi-session core) — Made the *permanence* of the two-domain identity legible without touching the flood-prone live drawer. The §5.B design gate (2026-07-18, human-reviewed via chat) resolved *against* "acquirable this run" ghost cards in the ActionDrawer (flood-control under the empty THR-501 starter floor + the blast radius of the `getTargetActionSlots` filter cascade) and routed the full three-state catalog into the **Codex** instead, reachable pre-filtered from the character sheet. **Engine/logic:** new pure `codexRunState.ts` — `buildCodexRunContext(gameState)` (domains from `getAscendantProgress`, the same read the Reaches/Signatures readouts use; unlock set from `unlockedActionIds`) + `codexEntryRunState(entry, ctx)` returning the shipped `SignaturePathState` (`available`/`acquirable`/`locked_incarnation`) with `SIGNATURE_STATE_COPY` reused verbatim (one source of truth). The lock keys on `requiresReach` (the real THR-503 reach gate), **never** the `reach` classification tag — so a universal (`reach:'star'`, no-gate) card is never wrongly greyed out, matching exactly what the drawer hides. **Registry:** added `requiresReach`/`isAscendantAction` to `CodexEntry`; catalogued the eight `invest.*` reach signatures (the only reach-locked cards) into the Codex divine category — they were absent before, so "Another life" was previously un-showable. **UI:** `Codex` gained optional `runContext`/`initialStateFilter`/`embedded`/`onClose` props → per-card incarnation badges (Yours / Within reach / Another life, dimmed for non-held) + a state-filter chip row; the standalone `?view=codex` route is byte-unchanged (no runContext → plain catalog). In-game overlay mounted in GameView (lazy import → own 23 kB chunk); entry points on the AscendantSheet ("Browse the path codex →") and the SignaturesBlock ("What you could still become →"), both deep-linking pre-filtered to `acquirable`. `getTargetActionSlots` untouched (blast radius dissolved). Manual page `divine-actions-reference.html` gained a player section + Designer Note. **Verification:** full suite **12473 green (827 files)**; `tsc -b` net-new 0 (3542 with changes vs 3557 stashed — a decrease); `vite build` green; browser-verified @1920×1080 on `?view=game&seeded&size=medium` (3-domain god): entry link opens the overlay pre-filtered to acquirable (aria-pressed), the "all" filter partitions the divine category 22 acquirable + 5 locked (the off-domain signatures), badges render with the shipped copy, dimming at opacity 0.62, overlay fills viewport with no page scroll, **0 console errors**. Screenshot substituted with a computed-style + layout + DOM-state capture (impediment #185 recurrence — WebGL Browser-pane screenshot hang; Claude-in-Chrome unavailable in the headless run). Issue kept **In Dev** (keyword-free PR) for Cowork to close — this completes the plan's §7 phasing (Slices 1–4 + rulebook + this tail); the optional card-choice/hold picker is out of scope and would need its own design pass. Plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-player-action-progression-v1.md`.

✅ **THR-640 — Motive-Receipt Foreshadowing Phase C: object-case pronouns + Reach-flavored motive clauses** (2026-07-18, Thematic Pressure & Living World, deferral of THR-631) — Content-depth pass on the receipt-driven encounter-motivation prose. **Item 1 (object-pronoun fix):** the two clauses that placed a pronoun in object position (`{matter} moves {subject} closer` and `This is {subject} all over` in `MOTIVE_CLAUSES`) rendered "moves they closer" / "this is they all over"; added a realizer `objectPronoun` helper (they→them/he→him/she→her) + `{object}`/`{Object}` typed slots, wired the object forms into both `composeReceipt` and `composeGeneric` slot maps, and switched the two clauses to `{object}`. **Item 2 (Reach-flavor sub-tables):** new `MOTIVE_CLAUSES_BY_REACH` gives the four most common contribution kinds (ambition, personality, intel, divine) Reach-specific S2 variants (3 relevant Reaches each × 3 variants — e.g. ambition/gold "coin and standing", personality/iron "straight on, the only way she knows how", divine/star "the powers above"); `composeReceipt` prefers the reach sub-pool when the receipt's `dominantReach` has one, falling back to the base kind pool, and keys the composition `pull:kind/reach`. **Tests:** the receipt agreement sweep now fills the object slots and asserts they resolve, plus a new static object-case lint that fails a subject slot sitting after a copula / transitive verb / object preposition (catches the bug class at the source). Full suite **12463 green (826 files)**, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` clean in the four touched files (repo baseline red is pre-existing/THR-489), 30-tick CLI smoke reached tick 30 with 389 agents + trace lines. **Items 3 (`intentPhrase` backfill) and 4 (authored marquee overrides) deferred to THR-657** — both turned out to depend on Phase-B (THR-631) wiring that never landed: `intentPhrase` doesn't exist as a field and `{matter}` has no consumption path (would need a new field on a 278-importer type + resolver/composer wiring — engine work the issue declared "N/A"), and authored-override selection still runs on `computeBaselineSignals` stubs while rendering through the deprecated `resolveForeshadowingPlaceholders` that bypasses the typed realizer's agreement guarantee. Browser-verify exempt (content-only; render surfaces unchanged, correctness by the agreement/object-case sweep). Plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-thr-631-motive-receipt-foreshadowing.md` (orphaned on commit cd1ee374, never merged to main).

🚧 **THR-613 Slice 4 — Player Action Progression v1: Covenants (sustained-control surface + `release_control`)** (2026-07-17, Action System & Unlocks; issue remains **In Dev**) — Delivered Axis C's UI + the release op (plan §5.A + §3.4): the god now sees what it *holds*, not just what it *does*. New folded-by-default **Covenants** section in the AscendantBar (`CovenantsBlock` + `selectCovenantRows` + `COVENANT_*` copy) lists each active sustained control — the ongoing prose as title, the target resolved to its node name (fail-soft to the hex), a plain-register upkeep phrase (cost/income/self-sustaining — never a raw float), and a contested badge — each with a **Release** control, the bar's only player-mutating control. Release enqueues a new `GameState.pendingControlReleases` field (mirrors `pendingChoiceCommits`), consumed by `phaseControlEffects` next tick: it lapses the effect `voluntarily_released` (a contested covenant is abandoned — its contestation encounter resolves in the rival's favour next tick), emits the groundwork-declared `ControlReleaseTrace`, and clears the queue; a released row leaves the panel immediately (optimistic — `selectCovenantRows` hides queued ids). `CONTROL_RELEASE_EVENT_SIGNIFICANCE` (0.5) tunable; `__DEBUG.listControlEffects()` / `.releaseControl(id)` bridge. 16 new tests (5 engine release cases in `phaseControlEffects.test.ts` + 11 selector cases in `covenantRows.test.ts`). 🚧 **THR-613 Slice 3b — Player Action Progression v1: signature-path legibility (UI)** (2026-07-17, Action System & Unlocks; issue remains **In Dev**) — Delivered §5.B's three-state permanence grammar for the eight reach signatures (the identity-defining headline powers). New folded-by-default **Signatures** section in the AscendantBar (`SignaturesBlock` + `selectSignaturePaths` + `SIGNATURE_STATE_COPY`/`SIGNATURE_GROUP_COPY` copy) partitions all eight into **Available** (a signature in one of your reaches you have earned), **Acquirable** (in your reaches, not yet earned — "not *yet*"), and **Locked-this-incarnation** (a reach outside your permanent domains — "not *this run*"), the last group rendered dimmed under a "Not This Incarnation" header. The god's domains come from the same `getAscendantProgress` read the Reaches readout (Slice 3a) and the Deepening beat use — one source of truth, the three surfaces cannot drift on what counts as "your reach" — and earned-ness from `unlockedActionIds` (a signature is granted by its reach's acquisition beat → its id lands in the unlock set). This makes legible the *permanence* the reach gate silently hides: without it the player cannot tell "I can't do this yet (earn it)" from "I can't do this at all this run (another incarnation's path)". Prose-first, no numbers; the selector reads `REACH_SIGNATURE_CONTENT_TEMPLATES` (all 8 reaches, one signature each) as the authoritative card set. 8 new selector contract tests (`signaturePaths.test.ts`): all-eight-covered completeness, the available/acquirable/locked partition, primary-★ flagging, yours-first ordering, three-domain handling, fail-soft empty case. 🚧 **THR-613 Slice 3a — Player Action Progression v1: progression legibility (UI)** (2026-07-17, Action System & Unlocks; issue remains **In Dev**) — Made the shipped Slices 1–2b progression legible in the UI. (1) `AscendantBeatModal` now merges `SPINE`/`DEEPENING`/`MILESTONE` presentation into one beat-id-keyed lookup (`AUTHORED_BEAT_PRESENTATION`), so a Deepening/Milestone beat renders its **authored reach-flavored vignette** (eye → "The Clarity of Your Sight") rather than the generic kind-placeholder every reach previously shared — the per-reach prose Slices 2/2b shipped was dead data until this wiring; the offer banner's eyebrow follows the same precedence. (2) New **Reaches** section in the AscendantBar (`ReachesBlock` + `selectReachRows` + `REACH_COPY`/`REACH_RANK_LABEL` copy) shows the god's permanent domains and current depth as prose tier words (Eye · Blind), reading the same `getAscendantProgress` the Deepening beat trips on (one source of truth, no drift) with a per-reach "Attend the beat" hint when a Deepening is pending. **Browser-verify surfaced and fixed a shipped substrate bug** (Slices 1–2b were engine/content, browser-exempt, so it had never rendered): dev-seed condition/clue `has_trait` edges omit `level`, and `computeRawScore` did `base * assignment.level` → `0 * undefined = NaN`, poisoning every reach's capability and silently defeating Deepening tier-crossing detection for the seeded god. Fixed fail-soft (NFP #4): `base * (assignment.level ?? 1)` — a no-op for every production trait (worldgen/culture/growth all set `level`) — plus a defence-in-depth finite-guard in `selectReachRows`. 22 new tests (`ascendantBeatPresentation.test.tsx` render + namespace-disjoint contract, `reachRows.test.ts` selector, `domainCapability.test.ts` NaN regression). Plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-player-action-progression-v1.md`. **Remaining (THR-613 stays In Dev):** Slice 3b (ActionDrawer three-state locked grammar + codex "other paths" + card-choice/hold picker + `run-lifecycle-reference.html`), Slice 4 (Covenants panel + `release_control`).

🚧 **THR-613 Slice 2b — Player Action Progression v1: milestone/breadth beats (Axis B)** (2026-07-17, Action System & Unlocks; issue remains **In Dev**) — Slices 1–2 built **Axis A** (reach *depth*): the god accrues `reachPractice`, crosses a Domain-Capability tier, and gets a prose-first Deepening vignette. Slice 2b builds **Axis B** (palette *breadth*), the axis where a new verb is the honest reward. New additive `'milestone'` `BeatKind` + `src/data/ascendant-milestone-beats.ts` (`ASCENDANT_MILESTONE_BEATS` + `MILESTONE_BEAT_PRESENTATION` + `milestoneChronicleProse`), catalogued through `findBeatDefinition`/`forceOfferBeatById` so an enqueued milestone **resolves** (records a `BeatRecord`) rather than being skipped as `missing_template` — the same bug class Slice 2 fixed for Deepening. `phaseAscendantProgression` gained the Axis-B branch: it counts the god's controlled essence sources via a new pure `countControlledSources` (reading THR-611's shipped source model — hooking the existing tiers, **not** a new trigger system) and enqueues `beat.milestone.the_wellspring_flows` at `MILESTONE_SOURCES_FOR_BEAT` (3) controlled sources **or** the first `flowering` source (`MILESTONE_FLOWERING_FOR_BEAT`), whichever lands first. Dedup is recorded at **enqueue** time in a new `milestoneBeatsFired` property bag — not inferred from beat history — so a beat that is offered but dismissed can never re-fire every tick, and a milestone can't re-fire if the source count dips and recovers. A Deepening **wins the tick** when both are eligible (the rarer, more personal moment); the milestone is threshold-based rather than edge-based, so it simply re-detects next tick and nothing is lost. **The grant — a real card, not a fake reveal.** The milestone grants `loc.open_markets` ("Awning Unfurled"): a shipped, fully-implemented, ascendant-facing Gold economy card (prosperity up, unrest down; real prose, real essence cost) that **no beat granted and no starter floor revealed** — under the empty THR-501 floor it was literally unreachable, a capability no player could ever fire. Slice 2's Deepening beats grant nothing precisely because every reach-gated signature is already handed out by the acquisition beats (re-offering one would lie about a card the god holds); the milestone has no such problem, so breadth gets a genuine new verb. A contract test now enforces the rule: a milestone grant must never duplicate another beat's. `collectGrantedActionIds` extended to include the milestone catalogue so the existing bucket drift-guards (`granted → bucket`, `bucket → real template`) cover it. New `ascendant.progression.milestone_enqueued` trace carries the counts that fired it. 16 contract tests (`phaseAscendantMilestone.test.ts`): both thresholds, below-threshold silence, once-per-run dedup across ticks, Deepening priority, spine/pending gating, catalogue resolution, and the no-fake-reveal guard. Discovery beats (the shipped Ruins/Delve Clue → Delve pattern) named as the third Axis-B path in the systemic wiring guide — no new system, just named so the three breadth paths read as one spine. Full suite **12396 green (821 files)**, vite build green, **0 net-new `tsc -b`** (3542=3542 stash-diff), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). **Browser-verified @1920×1080** (`?view=game&seeded&size=medium`, Playwright DOM): `__DEBUG.fireBeat('beat.milestone.the_wellspring_flows')` → the modal renders the "A Wellspring" vignette + the granted "Awning Unfurled" card face; Receive → `runUnlockedActionIds: ['loc.open_markets']` (its *only* entry — confirming the card was previously unreachable) and a resolved milestone `BeatRecord`; **0 console errors** (38 warnings all pre-existing worldgen/Three.js noise). Deferred: **THR-656** (art for `loc.open_markets` — it renders a placeholder icon, cosmetic only). Plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-player-action-progression-v1.md`. **Remaining (THR-613 stays In Dev):** Slice 3 (ActionDrawer three-state locked grammar + ascendant-bar tier readout + surface `DEEPENING_BEAT_PRESENTATION`/`MILESTONE_BEAT_PRESENTATION` in the modal + card-choice/hold picker + `run-lifecycle-reference.html` manual page), Slice 4 (Covenants panel + `release_control` op).

🚧 **THR-613 Slice 1 — Player Action Progression v1: engine substrate** (2026-07-05, Action System & Unlocks; issue remains **In Dev**) — God-side capability growth, the substrate that makes the player's action palette visibly deepen over a run. The ascendant now accrues `reachPractice` in its two permanent reaches whenever it resolves an in-domain action (`accruePlayerReachPractice`, hooked in the `unifiedActionResolution` uncontested-completion loop): `PLAYER_PRACTICE_PER_ACTION × difficultyScaling × diminishing`, with the secondary reach lagging via `SECONDARY_REACH_PRACTICE_MULT` and diminishing returns mirroring the agent `DIMINISHING_RETURNS_FACTOR`. `computeRawScore` folds `reachPractice[reach]` into the raw score as one more additive term, so it feeds Domain Capability through the **same sigmoid the agents use** — one source of truth, no parallel XP number (honours "ascendants use the same prerequisite system as agents"). A new leaf tick phase `phaseAscendantProgression` runs immediately **before** the Beat Director: it derives the live tier per permanent reach (`computeTier`), compares against a persisted `reachTierSnapshot` on the ascendant node, and on an upward crossing sets `ascendantBeats.pending` to a **Deepening beat** (new additive `'deepening'` `BeatKind`, id `beat.deepening.<reach>`). Because the Director skips when `pending` is already set, a Deepening beat preempts the cadence draw with **zero change to the Director itself** — the mutex-safe seam with THR-611's beat-enqueue work. Snapshot seeds silently from the current tier on the first phase run (no spurious beat); at most one Deepening per tick (`DEEPENING_BEAT_MAX_PER_TICK`); a crossing that can't enqueue (spine still active, slot occupied) leaves the snapshot unchanged and is re-detected next tick — no beat lost, none double-fired. All tunables in the new `src/data/player-progression.ts` (surfaced in the CMS constants registry). Three progression traces (`ascendant.progression.practice`/`tier_up`/`deepening_enqueued`; `control_release` declared for Slice 4) + `__DEBUG.getAscendantProgression()` (per-reach practice/tier/snapshot/pending readout). 11 contract tests (`phaseAscendantProgression.test.ts`) including the plan's Done-when: a run crossing a tier fires exactly one enqueue, and the primary-only cap when both reaches cross. Full suite **12373 green (819 files)**, vite build green, **0 net-new `tsc -b`** (3542=3542 stash-diff), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Engine + data + debug + one `AscendantBeatModal` presentation entry (required by the new `BeatKind`) → browser-verify exempt (Slice 1 is engine-only; the ActionDrawer three-state grammar / Covenants panel / Deepening modal are Slices 3–4). Plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-05-player-action-progression-v1.md`. **Remaining (THR-613 stays In Dev):** Slice 2 (8 Deepening beat content templates + milestone/discovery breadth beats, coordinate THR-611), Slice 3 (ActionDrawer three-state locked grammar + ascendant-bar tier readout + Deepening modal wiring), Slice 4 (Covenants panel + `release_control` op).

✅ **THR-636 — Encounter context UX** (2026-07-05, Encounter Experience) — Encounters' two delivery surfaces stopped undercutting the payoff. Right-rail toasts now render a **structured card** (`EncounterNotificationCard.tsx`) — agent name (prominent) · *encounter name* · `step N of M · band-word` meta · one-line prose tease — instead of dumping the full prose (`useEncounterNotifications` builds the card model; `ENCOUNTER_TOAST_DURATION_MULT`/`NOTIF_TEASER_MAX_CHARS` name the former magic numbers). Inside `EncounterVeil`, a new **context strip** promotes the character from a ghost subtitle to a clickable **portrait + name** (→ agent detail via `onSelectAgent`), renders the previously-dropped **location** with a **"Show on map"** camera-focus link (`onShowOnMap` → `hexMapRef.centerOn`), and a **reach chip**; the passive 6px step dots become an **outcome-coloured interactive `StepNavigator`** (≥`STEP_NAV_MIN_HIT_PX` hit areas) where clicking a resolved dot enters a read-only **replay** (`StepReplayView`) of that step's frozen prose, outcome word, reach, god-action taken, and complication, with "Return to the present". Engine substrate: additive `StepProseRecord` (`src/types/stepProseRecord.ts`, own file for small blast radius, fields aligned with THR-603 `ChapterStepRecord`) + `stepProseHistory?` (`readonly unknown[]`, cast at consumers) on `UnifiedAction`, captured at the `unifiedActionResolution` step-resolution site with the **seeded** resolution `rng` threaded into `enrichProse` (NFP #3 — capture-at-resolution freezes the truth; the legacy `encounter.ts` path uses `Math.random` so it is intentionally left summary-only via the fail-soft re-render path rather than storing nondeterministic prose); additive notification context fields (`totalSteps/outcomeBand/hexCol/hexRow/locationLabel`) populated at all 3 emission sites in `phaseEncounterVisibility` via `resolveLocationToHex`; `encounter_step_prose_recorded` trace registered in `TRACE_CATEGORIES`; `__DEBUG.getStepProse(agentRef)` bridge. Content: one exported `OUTCOME_BAND_WORDS` lexicon + `outcomeBandWord`/`stepOutcomeWord` in `outcome-band-content.ts` (reuses the existing 6-band vocabulary — no second lexicon). All five plan tunables named; seven-row fail-soft table honoured. Full suite **12362 green (818 files)**, vite build green, **0 net-new `tsc -b`** (3542=3542 stash-diff), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents, 18 unified actions resolving → records captured). Browser @1920×1080 (`?view=game&seeded&size=medium`): app loads with **0 console errors**, the new code (`StepReplayView`/`Show on map`/card) is served + executing, and `__DEBUG.getStepProse` is wired and returns correct shapes. **Browser-verify note:** the toast-card / veil / replayed-step *live* screenshots were not captured this run — the seeded game gates normal mortal encounters behind a scripted multi-beat opening that was non-deterministic to drive to completion within the run's budget; the component rendering is covered by 22 `EncounterVeil` + 36 `buildUnifiedEncounterStageModel` unit tests, and the load/console/`__DEBUG` browser pass was completed. ✅ **THR-639 — Action Unlock Reveal + Artifact Representation pattern** (2026-07-05, UI Visual Overhaul — Design System v1) — When the game teaches a new action (the Ascendant Beat modals), the modal showed only a `You will learn: <names>` text strip — no card, no art, no effects. Now every granted action is revealed as the **real focused `ActionCard` face** (art, name, cost, description) via a display-only `templateToPreviewSlot` adapter in `AscendantBeatModal.tsx` — the bare template id resolves art (`ACTION_ART`) and the type line exactly as the Action Drawer does, so the reveal card and the drawer card are pixel-identical. A new plain-prose **effects line** (`src/data/actionEffectsProse.ts` — `actionEffectsProse(template)`: 17 authored overrides for every `grantsActionIds` action + a composed crud×target fallback that degrades to a reach-generic; essence cost interpolated from the template so it is never hardcoded; THR-609 plain register, second person addressing the god) renders on the focused card face, plumbed as an additive `WheelSlot.effectsLine` populated once in the single `getTargetActionSlots` builder — so the Action Drawer's focused card and the modal share one line, no divergence. Selection beats render the same cards as clickable 1-of-N choices; non-selection beats render them as a pure reveal via a new additive `ActionCard.interactive` prop (default true — drops the click handler + "Click to activate" hint) with the footer button resolving. The modal `maxWidth` widens to fit up to `UNLOCK_CARD_ROW_MAX` (3) cards at `UNLOCK_CARD_SCALE` (0.8), wrapping + internally scrolling beyond. Fail-soft per the plan: unresolvable grant id → text line + `console.warn`; missing art → gradient placeholder. New binding pattern doc `Docs/design-system/artifact-representation.md` (art + information obligation + core-artifact registry table; generalizes THR-637 from image-only to image+info). Engine pillar N/A (pure presentation; templates resolved synchronously at render). Full suite **12362 green (818 files)**, vite build green, 0 net-new `tsc -b` (3542=3542 by stash-diff), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Browser-verified @1920×1080 (`?view=game&seeded&size=medium` + `__DEBUG.fireBeat`): `beat.spine.opening` → 2 cards (bind_thread_agent / observe_agent) each with its effects line ("…Costs 10 essence." / "…Costs 5 essence."); `beat.spine.a_path_opens` → 3 interactive choice cards (dream/omen/inspire, Costs 1/2/2) all above the fold; console 0 errors/warnings; DOM assertions confirm card count == `grantsActionIds.length` on both.

🚧 **THR-631 Phase B (emission + consumption) — Motive-Receipt Foreshadowing: the receipt drives the prose** (2026-07-05, Thematic Pressure & Living World; issue remains **In Dev**) — Phase A shipped the typed-slot realizer + composed-generic floor; Phase B **emission** (prior run) kept the scorer's normally-discarded per-candidate contribution terms as a `MotiveReceipt` on the agent node at the `phaseAgentDecision` selection commit. This run ships Phase B **consumption**: the receipt now renders as prose. New `composeReceiptForeshadowing` (`src/engine/foreshadowing/composeReceipt.ts`) assembles S1 knowledge (real intel tier from the receipt) → S2 pull (top contribution **kind**) → S3 expectation (real completionProb forecast tier, with an em-dash hedge tail below the `briefed` tier — dramatic irony: the prose commits to the agent's read, not the truth) → optional S4 stake (second contribution kind, only when its weight clears `STAKE_CLAUSE_MIN_WEIGHT`). Tooltip render = the S2 sentence only. `readMotiveReceipt` (`src/engine/foreshadowing/receiptRead.ts`; matches templateId + optional locationId, rejects stale/mismatched receipts) wires **both** foreshadowing resolvers (`getEncounterForeshadowing.ts` tooltip path + `encounterForeshadowing.ts` panel path) to read the same receipt off the same agent node — one causality source, two surfaces — falling soft to the composed-generic path when no receipt matches. New content in `foreshadowing-content.ts`: `MOTIVE_CLAUSES` (all 14 contribution kinds), `EXPECTATION_BY_FORECAST` (5 forecast tiers), `LOW_INTEL_HEDGE_TAILS`, `STAKE_CLAUSES` (curated high-drama kinds) + `DEFAULT_STAKE_CLAUSES` fallback — ≥3–4 variants each, plainspoken-Malazan (THR-609), agreement-safe (`{v:}` only after the pronoun subject; no object/possessive pronouns). Additive: `ForeshadowingResult.tooltipProse`, trace `receipt`/`compositionKeys`; `__DEBUG.getForeshadowing` returns `tooltipProse`+`receipt`. Trace emit payloads typed as `Omit<ForeshadowingResolutionTrace,…>` to sidestep the emitTrace Omit-over-union field collapse — net `tsc -b` went **down** (3544→3542, removing two pre-existing excess-property errors at the foreshadowing call sites). 14 new tests (`composeReceipt.test.ts`) including a **self-maintaining** clause × pronoun agreement sweep that derives each verb's singular/plural forms from the real `conjugate` function (adding a clause with a new verb needs no test edit). Full suite **12362 green (818 files)**, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents; 16 live receipts on real agent nodes by tick 40; receipt-driven prose rendered end-to-end for he/she/they via a bundled resolver check). Engine + content + dev-bridge only (no `src/components/` → browser-verify exempt). **Remaining (THR-631 stays In Dev):** the full resolver module-merge + the tooltip-component S2 render + a DebugPanel receipt table (a UI slice); the scorer-sourced `divine`/`bond`/`reputation`/`proximity` contribution kinds (need the scorer to surface terms it folds into `baseScore`); Phase C content depth (reach flavoring, `intentPhrase` backfill, authored overrides for top marquee encounters); Phase D wiring docs.

Root cause: `createHexFillMesh`/`updateHexColors` wrote instance colors with unflagged `setRGB` (linear interpretation) while fog-culling writes flagged `THREE.SRGBColorSpace`; with sRGB renderer output the unflagged path double-gamma-encodes → pale base map at game start. All four `setRGB` calls now flagged. Per user directive the sepia remembered-hex tint is removed entirely — remembered hexes keep full terrain color (agents/events still hidden, unexplored still parchment); orphaned `toSepia` + `SEPIA_*` constants + tests deleted. 🚧 **THR-631 Phase A (core) — Motive-Receipt Foreshadowing: the composed-generic prose floor** (2026-07-05, Thematic Pressure & Living World; issue remains **In Dev**) — The encounter-motivation foreshadowing surface failed in two visible ways for the ~99% of encounters with no authored variant: the generic fallback jammed the encounter *title* into a place slot ("trouble in Weave a Political Alliance") and broke subject–verb agreement ("They believes"). Both bugs lived in the two resolvers' single-string fallbacks (`GENERIC_FORESHADOWING_FALLBACK`, `GLOBAL_FORESHADOWING_FALLBACK_TEMPLATE`). This slice ships the two load-bearing, permanent Phase-A deliverables that fix them at the root — the **typed-slot agreement realizer** (`src/engine/foreshadowing/realizer.ts`: `conjugate`/`pronounNumber`/`realize` — verb slots `{v:lemma}` conjugate to the subject's grammatical number, noun slots are typed so a title can never land in a `{place}` slot; fail-soft, never throws) and the **composed-generic clause pool** (`src/data/foreshadowing-content.ts` + `src/engine/foreshadowing/composeGeneric.ts`: knowledge→pull→expectation, reach-flavored across the 8 canonical Reaches, intel-tiered with mandatory hedges below `briefed`, plainspoken-Malazan/THR-609 voice, ≥3 variants/key, deterministic on (agentId, encounterId)). Both resolvers' *no-authored-variant* branch now composes instead of emitting the buggy string; authored variants and per-template fallbacks are untouched; the old strings are marked `@deprecated` (kept only for an existing token-shape test). The encounter title is deliberately never surfaced — it is an action label, not a noun phrase; the matter is grounded at the candidate location ("what stirs at Ashmarket") when known, neutral otherwise. New tests: `realizer.test.ts` (conjugation + the historical "They believes" bug proven impossible) and `composeGeneric.test.ts` including the **full clause × pronoun agreement sweep** (every clause rendered for he/she/they, asserting no singular-subject-base-verb, no plural-subject-singular-verb, no unresolved slots — the plan's Done-when). Full suite **12343 green (816 files)**, `tsc --noEmit` clean + 0 net-new `tsc -b` errors (2 flagged errors pre-exist on baseline), vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Engine + data only (no `src/components/` → browser-verify exempt; the UI renders the same `ForeshadowingResult.prose` field, and prose correctness is proven by the agreement sweep + CLI). **Remaining (THR-631 stays In Dev):** rest of Phase A (collapse the two divergent resolvers into one module; thread-card tooltip overlap fix), and Phases B (Motive Receipt emission from `ScoredCandidate` at decision commit + real intel tier), C (fill clause tables to the marquee floor + authored overrides), D (wiring docs).

🚧 **THR-611 Slice 4 — Divine Economy: the Find → Claim front half** (2026-07-05, Divine Economy — Essence Sources & Income; issue remains In Dev) — Slices 1–3 grew income only from hosts the player already controlled; the directive was "find *and build* sources of essence," so Slice 4 delivers the discovery half. Worldgen now seeds a small number of **latent** `placeOfPower` sources (`src/engine/essenceSourceSeeding.ts`, `seedLatentEssenceSources`, `LATENT_SOURCE_SEED_COUNT=6` capped by eligible hosts, deterministic per-seed) onto natural wild-interest locations (grove/cavern/hot_spring/monument/ancient_road) — undiscovered (`discoveredBy` undefined, fog-hidden) and typed by their own **locale** dominant sphere, wired into `gameInit` after location sphere-affinity seeding. A key ground-truth correction surfaced during CLI verification: nearly all wild interest points already carry a *mundane* `controls` edge (faction/culture territory), so the seeding filter deliberately does **not** exclude controlled hosts — a divine source is orthogonal to mortal control (income only ever walks the *ascendant's* own controls edges, so no leak). Two new graph-op kinds (`src/types/graphOp.ts` + fail-soft executor cases): `find_source` (reveal latent sources within `SOURCE_DISCOVERY_RANGE_HOPS=3` hexes of the target via new `findLatentSourcesInRange`, sets `discoveredBy`) and `claim_source` (establish the ascendant→host `controls` edge so the worldgen-typed source's income flows; enforces the Find→Claim prerequisite — refuses undiscovered sources; idempotent). Two Star/Eye ascendant templates (`loc.find_source` Eye/read, `loc.claim_source` Star/update, real step ops, Threadbare plain-register prose), both added to the `the_wellspring` beat grant (now unlocks the whole find→claim→build→defend loop) and `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` (`unlockable-generic`). A claimed ley-nexus pours *its country's* sphere — distinct from a consecrated shrine, dedicated to the *god's* sphere. 14 new tests (`essenceSourceSeeding.test.ts` + `graphOpExecutor.findClaimSources.test.ts`; `ascendantBeatPool.test.ts` grant assertion updated to the five-verb loop). Full suite **12319 green (813 files)**, vite build green, 0 net-new tsc errors (3544=3544 baseline), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents; CLI-verified 6 latent sources seeded, all hidden + locale-typed). Engine + data only (no `src/components/` → browser-verify exempt). **Remaining slices (THR-611 stays In Dev):** Player-UI (portfolio income-by-source panel, WebGL hex source signifiers, IPK/codex/chronicle-tier-transition, DebugPanel essence-sources tab, `source_*` traces), and discovery/consecration/defend encounters + rulebook Divine Economy section + Game-Manual-Wiki essence page.

🚧 **THR-611 Slice 3 — Divine Economy: verb surfacing** (2026-07-05, Divine Economy — Essence Sources & Income; issue remains In Dev) — Slice 2 shipped the source-loop verbs (`loc.consecrate_source`/`loc.sanctify_source`/`loc.defend_source`) but nothing granted them, so with the empty THR-501 starter floor they were unreachable by the player. Slice 3 is the surfacing: one investment **Ascendant Beat** `beat.pool.invest.the_wellspring` ("The Wellspring", `src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts` + a Threadbare plain-register content template in `ascendant-pool-beat-templates.ts`) grants all three verbs via the shipped `resolvePendingBeat` grant path. One beat grants the whole loop because the three are useless apart (consecrate a place, THEN deepen it, THEN ward the *same* source) — mirroring `the_hallowed_place` granting both consecrate variants together. Three matching `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` entries are `unlockable-generic` — universal and sphere-flavored (`consecrate_source` types the host to the ascendant's primary sphere), NOT two-domain reach-locked: the verbs' `reach: 'star'` is their cosmic-energy axis, not a `requiresReach` gate, so they surface for every run once unlocked. `starter: true` again deliberately NOT used — the empty THR-501 floor is invariant-enforced; surfacing is the earned-beat grant. +1 test (`ascendantBeatPool.test.ts`) drives the real resolve path: resolving the beat unlocks all three verbs and flips `isActionRevealed` true. Cumulative: full suite **12306 green (811 files)**, vite build green, 0 net-new tsc errors in touched files, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Data-only content (no `src/components/` → browser-verify exempt; the surfacing is proven at the engine level via the real resolve+reveal path). **Remaining slices (THR-611 stays In Dev):** Find/Claim of uncontrolled sources (needs latent-source worldgen seeding + discovery encounters), and Player-UI (portfolio panel + WebGL hex signifiers + IPK/codex/chronicle + rulebook/manual-wiki).

🚧 **THR-611 Slice 2 — Divine Economy: the Build → Defend verb loop** (2026-07-05, Divine Economy — Essence Sources & Income; issue remains In Dev) — Slice 1 shipped the income substrate but nothing created *typed* sources (migration only makes untyped dormant places of power), so the interesting per-sphere income was inert. Slice 2 adds the player verbs the user directive asked for ("actions that progress essence income"). Three new **graph-op kinds** (`src/types/graphOp.ts` + dedicated fail-soft executor cases in `graphOpExecutor.ts`, routed through the resolution `graphOnlyOps`→`executeGraphOps` path — no `unifiedActionResolution.ts` change): `consecrate_source` (turns a controlled host into a typed source feeding the ascendant's PRIMARY sphere — read from `sphereAlignment.primary`, override via `op.sourceSphere`/`sourceKind` — and ensures a `controls` edge so `computeSourceIncome` sees it; idempotent, upgrades a migrated untyped place of power in place), `sanctify_source` (raises sanctity by `SANCTITY_BUILD_PER_ACTION` toward flowering ≈2× income; refuses an untyped host), `defend_source` (clears `contestedBy`/`desecrated` + restores sanctity by the new `SANCTITY_DEFEND_RESTORE` 0.25, re-derives the uncontested tier). All tier derivation reuses the Slice-1 pure helpers — the ops never hardcode a tier; no `touchWorld` from the executor (matches the `phaseEssenceSources` precedent — tiers/income recompute every tick, `worldVersion` bumps per tick). Three **ascendant action templates** (`loc.consecrate_source`/`loc.sanctify_source`/`loc.defend_source`, `src/data/unified-action-templates.ts`) — Star reach, settlement/shrine targets, real step ops (not no-op cards, cf. THR-605), Threadbare plain-register prose + `technicalEffect` blocks. `starter: true` deliberately NOT used (empty THR-501 starter floor left intact — the invariant enforces it); verb surfacing is deferred to the UI slice. 13 new op tests (`graphOpExecutor.essenceSources.test.ts`) incl. the full consecrate→sanctify(dormant→flowering)→defend loop and income assertions. Full suite **12305 green (811 files)**, vite build green, 0 net-new tsc errors, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (389 agents). Engine + content only (no `src/components/` → browser-verify exempt). **Remaining slices (THR-611 stays In Dev):** verb surfacing (beat unlock grant / starter-floor decision), Find/Claim of uncontrolled sources (needs latent-source worldgen seeding + discovery encounters), and Player-UI (portfolio panel + WebGL hex signifiers + IPK/codex/chronicle + rulebook/manual-wiki).

🚧 **THR-611 Slice 1 — Divine Economy essence-source engine substrate** (2026-07-05, Divine Economy — Essence Sources & Income; issue remains In Dev) — Shipped the additive, fully-verifiable income substrate: an `essenceSource` property bag on existing host nodes (no new node/edge type — a generalization of the place of power), tunable constants + pure tier/DR helpers (`essence-sources.ts`), graph logic (`computeSourceIncome` typed per-sphere income with portfolio diminishing returns; `migrateControlledPlacesOfPower` idempotent income-neutral migration; `recomputeControlledSourceTiers` + contested drain), and a `phaseEssenceSources` tick phase (before `phaseEssence`, O(controlled hosts), one aggregate trace/tick). Typed source income folded into both `computeEssenceGeneration` (pool) and `computeEssenceIncome` (HUD); untyped/dormant migrated places of power stay on the legacy alignment-distributed term, so a save with no built sources yields provably-identical income (contract test asserts bit-for-bit per-sphere). `__DEBUG.getEssenceSources()` inspection. 15 new tests; full suite 12292 green (810 files), vite build green, 30-tick smoke clean. Engine + dev-bridge only. **Follow-on slices (THR-611 remains In Dev):** Content (find/claim/build/defend templates, discovery/consecration/defend encounters, IPK/codex/chronicle) and Player-UI (portfolio income-by-source panel, WebGL hex source signifiers, rulebook + Game-Manual-Wiki page).

✅ **THR-615 — Mortal Economy P1: resource activation** (2026-07-05, M3: Dynamic Economy) — Activated the decorative resource layer. **Step 0** confirmed resources are location-property `Record<string, ResourceInstance>` (not the schema's `resource` node type), seeded by terrain — superseding the plan's node/edge assumption. New `phaseResourceStockTiers` (declarative registry, `pre-economy` slot, ordered after `reputation_decay` and before inline `phaseProsperity`) derives a coarse **scarce/adequate/surplus** tier per resource per location from supply (`quantity/100`) minus a population-proxy demand (prosperity + sublocation density), via the pure `resourceEconomy.ts`; spotlight-capped at `ECON_PHASE_SPOTLIGHT_CAP` 40 (coarse tier-only beyond), first-derivation silent (avoids a tick-1 ring-buffer burst), `touchWorld` on change. 18-resource **class table** (`resource-classes.ts`: category · primarySphere mirrors `RESOURCE_DEFINITIONS[id].sphereAffinities[0]` · baseValue · scarcitySensitivity). Aggregate base-value-weighted `resourceBalance` (∈[-1,1]) stored per location and read by `phaseProsperity` as an additive equilibrium term (`RESOURCE_BALANCE_PROSPERITY_WEIGHT` 0.15 → ±15), attributable in the `prosperity_tick` breakdown. **Famine/Glut**: `resource_scarcity`/`resource_glut` chronicle triggers (baseline-register templates), staple/strategic + baseValue≥0.6, on genuine crossings only. **Livelihood thread tug** (scope amendment): a bonded non-dormant mortal whose home settlement holds a staple in scarce (hard/Famine) or surplus (moderate/Glut) pulls a Gold-reach `ThreadTug` on the existing attention machinery — state-based, deduped by `agentId::resourceId`, residents resolved sublocation→parent; the attend path (`useAgentInteraction`) resolves by agentId so no backing encounter is needed. **UI**: prose-only **Livelihood** line on `LocationView` (Famine/Glut as economic in-prose keywords via extended `renderProseWithIPK`) + DebugPanel **Economy** tab (`EconomyDebugTab`) + `resource_stock_tier_change` trace. **Wiki**: new W18 Game Manual page `livelihoods-reference.html` (dual-layer) registered in the manifest. ✅ **THR-581** (2026-07-04, Engine Observability & Performance) — **Large-map stall fix: `agent_decision` super-linear cost.** Follow-on to the THR-580 diagnosis. Extended the THR-580 phase timing to sub-phases (temporary `ad_social`/`ad_faction`/`ad_filter`/`ad_score`/`ad_strategic` brackets) and empirically **disproved the handoff's root-cause hypothesis**: the `getEntriesNearHex` awareness pre-filter profiled at ~1.5ms, not the ~300ms bottleneck. The true super-linear driver was `computeCapability` (walks trait / artifact / controls graph edges per call) recomputed **per encounter-entry** with a **fixed agentId** inside three filter stages — `filterByAwareness` (`src/engine/encounterAwareness.ts`), `filterByOutgrowth`, and `filterByThreat` (`src/engine/encounterFilterPipeline.ts`). Memoizing capability per reach (≤9 distinct) within each call collapses hundreds of redundant graph traversals per agent into ≤9. Identical values → no behavior change (determinism parity, NFP #3). Also delivered the plan's specified O(1)-in-map-size **bounded neighborhood-probe** in `getEntriesNearHex` (`src/engine/encounterCache.ts`): enumerates the cube-coordinate disk within `maxRange` and looks each hex up directly instead of scanning every occupied hex; self-tuning crossover at `hexDiskArea(maxRange)` (probe used only once occupied-hex count exceeds the fixed candidate count); matches re-ordered by a new per-hex insertion `seq` so output is **byte-identical** to the old scan-all order (preserves the reroute-max and `scoreAndSelect` stable-sort tie-breaks). Permanent `agent_decision_awareness` timing sub-row emitted **once per tick** (never per-agent — trace-ring flood guard), gated on profiling (zero cost when off). **Large seed-42 `profile 80`:** `agent_decision` **392→81ms avg**, `ad_filter` **301→26ms**, worst tick 641→234ms. **Determinism parity:** seed-42 state hashes byte-identical before/after on medium (`-1560496860`, scan-all branch) and large (`1560457581`, probe branch engaged). 4 new equivalence unit tests (`encounterCache.test.ts`) assert probe==scan-all byte-for-byte across centers/ranges/both branches. Full suite **12205 green (804 files)**, tsc `--noEmit` exit 0, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (391 agents). Browser-verify exempt: engine perf, no render change. **Honest scope note:** the secondary DM-rebuild-tick spike (now the residual outlier) is left to THR-570's `structuralCacheVersion` split per the handoff's explicit gating ("Do NOT bundle a structuralCacheVersion split"). Engine = the fix · Content N/A · UI N/A (dev-verify via THR-580 Phases tab + CLI `profile`). Parent: THR-570.

✅ **THR-604** (2026-07-04, Action System & Unlocks) — **Technical game-effect descriptions in the Action Catalog.** Built plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-04-action-catalog-gameplay-effects.md`. Added an additive optional `technicalEffect?: string` to `UnifiedActionTemplate` (278 importers, zero forced ripple — `tsc -b` net-zero) as a wiki-facing technical statement distinct from the atmospheric `description`. Authored all **165** ascendant-facing catalog entries in one reviewable map, `src/data/action-technical-effects.ts`, **sourced from the resolving code** (each written by reading the template's step GraphOps, its `controlSpec`, or the engine bridge that implements its id — not paraphrased from flavor prose), magnitudes kept **symbolic** (named constants like `HEX_BLESS_INFLUENCE_DELTA` / "per-tick Spirit cost", never literals — NFP #1); the map is overlaid at `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` assembly (additive `.map`; inline `technicalEffect` wins; absent id untouched). New metadata-only `src/engine/engineEffectRegistry.ts` aggregates the id-keyed engine bridges that resolve effects **outside** step ops — `HEX_BRIDGE_TEMPLATE_IDS` (new additive export from `hexActionBridge.ts`, union of tile-mutation / static-GraphOp / dynamic-generator maps) + `TEMPLATE_REVELATION_MAP` (revelationResolver — the observation hexes) + `PERCEIVE_RELAY_TEMPLATE_IDS` + `SELF_ACTION_TEMPLATE_IDS` (new additive export from `unifiedActionResolution.ts`) + `ENCHANT_TEMPLATE_ID`. The generator (`scripts/generate-action-catalog.ts`) derives `effectSource` per template (precedence template-ops → control-spec → engine-bridge → aftermath-only → none, first-match, branch-variant-aware) and emits `technicalEffect` + `effectSource` + `totals.byEffectSource` + `unauthoredEffects`; **SCHEMA_VERSION 1→2**. `public/action-catalog.html`: per-entry wiring badge (`wired·template` / `wired·engine` / `sustained` / `aftermath` / `not wired`; missing text → `—` + `unauthored`), a drawer Effect block, header wiring counts, an effect-wiring filter, and a `schemaVersion≥2` guard that hides effect UI on legacy JSON (fail-soft). **Honest finding (verify-the-noun, plan §Kill-criteria):** the derivation surfaces **8 genuine no-ops, not the plan's 6** — `sub.sanctify` and `sub.sanctify_tavern` are also unwired (empty step ops, no controlSpec, no engine bridge; the only engine reference is a `placeGating` sphere-alignment *availability* check), so the plan's "sub.sanctify sustained" claim was stale. These get intended-effect text + the truthful `none` badge; wiring them is tracked in THR-605. **Verified:** full suite **12201 green (804 files)** incl. new `src/engine/__tests__/engineEffectRegistry.test.ts` + `scripts/__tests__/generate-action-catalog.test.ts` (12 new tests); `tsc -b` **net-zero new errors** (3539 with changes = 3539 baseline, via stash diff); `vite build` green (prebuild regenerates the JSON in-sync). Browser-verified @1920×1080 (Playwright DOM — static page, no live GameState, DOM-structure assertion per the W9 precedent): 165 cards each with a wiring badge; stats "effect wiring: 92 wired·template · 17 sustained · 44 wired·engine · 8 not wired · 4 aftermath"; drawer Effect block code-true text + badge (`hex.claim_dominion`→sustained); 5-option effect filter; sole console entry an environmental `favicon.ico` 404. Wiki-freshness `sources` extended to the effect files + generator. Engine = registry (metadata-only) · Content = the 165 authored effect strings · UI = catalog Effect line/badge/filter. Non-goals (explicit): implementing the no-op actions (THR-605), in-game Codex display, auto-deriving effect text. Mutex with THR-605. Parent epic: Action System & Unlocks.

✅ **THR-580** (2026-07-03, Engine Observability & Performance) — **Tick-loop observability instrumentation (E1–E5 + U1–U3) + large-map stall diagnosis.** Built the THR-570 plan. E4 dedicated timing ring in `traceBuffer.ts` (`emitTiming`/`emitPhaseTiming`/`getTimingTraces`, `TIMING_BUFFER_SIZE=4000`, profiling off by default) so high-volume phase-profile traces stop evicting semantic traces; re-routed all `tick_phase_profile` emitters to it. E1 `runInlinePhase()` times the 4 heavy inline phases; E2 `distance_matrix_rebuild` trace + `SimulationRuntime.distanceMatrixRebuildCount`; E3 per-tick `tick_profile` rollup; E5 `aggregatePhaseTimings()` (avg/max/p95). U1 PhasesDebugTab timing columns + profiling toggle; U2 CLI `profile`; U3 `__DEBUG.getPhaseTimings/enable/disableProfiling`. Phase-2 diagnosis (THR-581): seed 42 large map — `agent_decision` dominates (291ms avg @ ~1026 agents), slowest tick had no DM-rebuild ⇒ super-linear `phaseAgentDecision`, not a rebuild storm; distance matrix capped at 1200 while large maps reach ~1777 locations mid-run. Full suite 12183 green (801 files), vite build green, `tsc -b` 0 net-new, engine smoke clean. Wiring-checklist updated. Deferrable inline-phase tail → THR-582; the perf fix → THR-581.

✅ **THR-599** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W14) — **`public/armies-battles-reference.html` — "Armies, Battles & Lairs".** Fourteenth Manual-section page (Tier 2, the living world), authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W14 (systems-spec authority `Docs/plans/2026-03-29-conflict-and-destruction-design.md` TB-073 + the M2.5 monster-encounters plan for lairs; no dedicated UL military shard yet — flagged honestly). Player-manual "How it plays" (4 sections): **armies raise themselves, march, and wear down** (factions muster hosts only for `territorial_expansion`/`revenge`; warband/regiment/host trading speed for hitting-power; terrain punishes columns and roads help more; strength bleeds every tick and the consequences surface as encounters — supply crisis→desertion→mutiny→dissolution — never a raw number); **battles resolve in the world, not on a board** (a momentum struggle over several turns, turning points you can shape *only* when a thread ties you to a participant, aftermath leaving destruction/captured-or-killed commanders/refugees); **sieges — when the walls become the battle** (walls favour the defender, the attacker wins by patience + starvation, the whole region gets pulled in); **lairs escalate if you leave them alone** (minor→major named-elite→legendary monster-faction, spreading + poisoning the land + reinfesting cleared-but-untended ground). Kept the honest scope note as a callout: **war is a story generator, not a wargame** (no tactics screen, no unit micro). Designer Notes carry code-true flags. **Status grep-verified precisely, not parroted:** the whole army→battle→siege→lair chain is **IMPL & wired** in `orchestrator.ts` Act 2.35x (`phaseArmyMovement`→`phaseArmyAttrition`→`phaseBattleDetection`→`phaseBattleTick`→`phaseLairEscalation`→`phaseArmyNotifications`) — correcting the plan's "broad but shallow" implication. Armies raised by `phaseFactionAmbitions`→`armySpawning.spawnArmy` (commander Iron≥4, faction Gold≥3, size from Gold tier) + worldgen + reach-signature warhost; attrition (`phaseArmyAttrition`, deterministic) fires four one-way `QUINTESSENCE_THRESHOLDS` encounters and force-disbands at 0; battles (`battleResolution`) momentum ±`BATTLE_RESOLUTION_THRESHOLD 8`, thread-gated spotlights (`battleSpotlights`, 55% seeded) vs chronicle-only; aftermath (`battleAftermath`) severity/commander-fate/prosperity-loss/refugees; sieges (`siegeResolution`) accelerating pacing + `FORTIFICATION_*` + `SIEGE_STARVATION_TICK 15` + regional pull range 5; lairs (`lairSeeding`+`lairEscalation`, every 25t) upgrade at 30/60t + adjacent spread + reinfestation. **Two honest DESIGN simplifications flagged:** hostility is faction-difference only ("no alliance system yet", `battleResolution.areHostile`) and "underfunded" is a `prosperity<0.2` proxy not a treasury (`armyAttrition.checkUnderfunded`); plus the deliberate `MAX_ARMIES_PER_FACTION=1` cap and the **term collision** between army-`quintessence` (cohesion) and cosmological Quintessence (narrative centrality). 19-row constants table + systems-spec/UL-gap/adjacent-Manual links (no open questions minted). Registered `section:"Manual"` as the 14th entry (after W13, preserving W1→W14 reading order; Manual heads the hub) with **15 verified `sources` globs — all 4 plan globs resolved, enriched with `armySpawning`/`armyNotifications`/`battleAftermath`/`battleSpotlights`/`siegeResolution`/`lairSeeding` + `types/army.ts`/`types/battle.ts` + 3 content files**; `npm run generate-design-wiki` regenerated the hub + re-injected nav into all 17 pages. Style matches the parchment/Cinzel W1–W13 pages verbatim (retained the Google-Fonts `@import`; `.tier` ladder + `.callout`/`.tunables` reused). Full suite **12189 green (802 files)**, `vite build` green (page in `dist/`), `check:design-wiki` OK (17 pages), `check:wiki-freshness` OK (public/ HTML + manifest JSON only — no `src/` runtime, engine-smoke N/A). ✅ **THR-598** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W13) — **`public/settlements-economy-reference.html` — "Settlements, Prosperity & Trade".** Thirteenth Manual-section page (Tier 2, the living world), authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W13 (systems-spec authority `Docs/plans/2026-03-17-gold-reach-economic-systems-design.md`; no dedicated UL economic shard yet — flagged honestly). Player-manual "How it plays" (4 sections): **settlements grow and shrink on their own** (the four sizes hamlet→town→city with capital fixed; a place must *sustain* its fortune to change; sick settlements cannot grow); **prosperity and unrest — a settlement's pulse** (prosperity as an equilibrium value drifting toward its conditions; unrest as instability that colours everything at a place); **trade routes — the arteries between places** (routes carrying goods with a volume that bleeds away when un-travelled until the road falls silent); **the land sets the ceiling** (terrain-seeded timber/stone/ore/water as carrying-capacity ceiling not income, plus rare anomalies holding uncommon goods that draw the perceptive). Kept the honest scope note as a callout: **the economy is texture serving story, not a trading sim.** Designer Notes carry code-true flags. **Status grep-verified precisely, not parroted:** settlement promotion/demotion (`phaseSettlementPromotion`: `SETTLEMENT_PROMOTION_PROSPERITY 70`/`_DEMOTION 20`/`_SUSTAIN_TICKS 20`, capital excluded, THR-401 health gate), prosperity equilibrium (`phaseProsperity`: `PROSPERITY_DRIFT_RATE 0.05`, target inputs, `BASE`/`MAX_CARRYING_CAPACITY 30`/`100`, `UPKEEP_CITY`/`_CAPITAL 5`/`8`, shocks), trade-route decay (`phaseTradeRouteDecay`/`tradeRoute.ts`: `trades_with` edges, `TRADE_ROUTE_MAX_VOLUME 10`/`_DECAY_RATE 1`/`_FRESHNESS_WINDOW 5`), settlement-genome reassessment, 8 economic traits (`phaseEconomicTraits`), 12-trigger economic chronicle (`economicChronicle`), and 4 base + 10 anomaly resources (`resourceSeeding`/`ANOMALY_RESOURCE_MAP`) — all IMPL. **Honest DESIGN callout:** the unrest defection(70)/sack(90) threshold *consequences* are risk markers, NOT auto-fired — `phaseUnrest` is decay-only, emitting threshold-*clear* traces; unrest is driven UP elsewhere (`complicationEffects`/`phaseDoom`/player acts) and genuinely influences resolution/omens/prose (IMPL). Also noted the missing UL economic shard as documentation debt (referenced, not re-opened). 17-row constants table + systems-spec/UL-gap/adjacent-Manual links (no open questions minted). ✅ **THR-597** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W12) — **`public/world-map-reference.html` — "The World: Hexes, Terrain & Sight".** Twelfth Manual-section page (Tier 2, the living world), authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W12 (canon authority `Docs/canon/hex-map.md`; UL Graph shard — HexTile/TerrainType/Three-tier Position Model/located_at Edge/Hex/Location/Sublocation). Player-manual "How it plays" (4 sections): **the world is a grid of hexes** (flat-top tiles each with a terrain + a mutable ledger of divine influence & corruption; enough of either remakes the terrain; map size medium↔large); **where things are — three nested tiers** (hex → location → sublocation, a mortal pinned to exactly one rung, resolution nesting upward to the hex); **the land remembers what you do to it** (divine influence vs corruption, each decaying, each transforming terrain past a threshold with a cooldown; magical saturation as a *place's* charge); **sight, and the fog over the world** (the god's fog of war unexplored→visible→remembered lifted by avatar/retinue/scry, plus the mortals' own hex-granular encounter awareness scaled by Domain Capability). Designer Notes carry 9 IMPL / 2 DESIGN / 3 OPEN code-true flags and lead with the **load-bearing decision — hexes are flat `GameState.tiles[]` array state, not graph nodes** (hex actions produce `HexMutation[]` applied in `phaseHexState`; only locations/sublocations/actors are graph nodes). **Two distinct hex-distance sight systems documented separately, not conflated:** player fog of war (`visibility.ts`/`recalcVisibility`, states unexplored→visible→remembered, LOS from avatar `AVATAR_SIGHT_RANGE 0` / retinue `AGENT_SIGHT_RANGE 1` / scry `SCRY_SIGHT_RANGE 0`) vs agent encounter awareness (`encounterAwareness.ts`/`filterByAwareness`, per-reach hop budget `AWARENESS_THRESHOLD 0.05` / `BASE 1` / `CAPABILITY_PER_HOP 0.15` / `MAX 5`, same-hex always visible; **location-hop awareness is the rejected approach — hex distance is the oracle**). **Accuracy point grepped from code:** magical saturation (`phaseMagicalSaturation`, `MAGICAL_SATURATION_DECAY_RATE 0.02`) decays a per-*location* property, distinct from the per-hex-tile `divineInfluence`/`corruption` (`phaseHexState`: decay 0.02/0.01, transform thresholds 0.7/0.8, cooldown 10) — stated precisely to head off the common conflation. Honest note on the dual position representation: the canonical relationship is the `located_at` edge (used by `visibility.ts`/`encounterAwareness.ts`) while `hexActorIndex.ts` (THR-188) reads the mirror `locationId` property to build a fast per-tick index; both resolve up to the same hex. 14-row constants table + canon (`Docs/canon/hex-map.md`) / UL (Graph shard) / adjacent-Manual / Deep-Reference links (no open questions minted — the large-map tick-loop perf note THR-162/163/164/165 is referenced from canon). Registered `section:"Manual"` as the 12th entry (after W11, preserving W1→W12 reading order; Manual heads the hub) with **9 verified `sources` globs — all 5 plan globs resolved, enriched with `types/visibility.ts`, `types/hexMutation.ts`, `data/terrain-transformation-content.ts`, `lib/hexMath.ts`**; `npm run generate-design-wiki` regenerated the hub + re-injected nav into all 15 pages (each sibling gained exactly one nav link; diffs verified nav-only). Style matches the parchment/Cinzel W1–W11 pages verbatim (retained the Google-Fonts `@import`; new `.tier` ladder grid, reused `.card-grid`/`.callout`/`.tunables`). ✅ **THR-596** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W11) — **`public/factions-cultures-reference.html` — "Factions, Cultures & Secrets".** Eleventh Manual-section page (Tier 2, the living world), authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W11 (source spine `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` §2 "What the World Is" + the UL Agents/Graph shards; factions & cultures are `actor` graph nodes). Player-manual "How it plays" (4 sections): **the world organizes itself** (three social layers — factions, cultures, groups — over the individual mortals, each bending its members' choices; a god earns standing, anoints, or sows a crisis, never commands); **factions — organizations with their own designs** (12 seeded factions across 5 `factionType`s; reputation on `member_of` edges climbing per-faction rank ladders; alignment favours members who fit; factions act autonomously — six ambitions, six action types shaded by the leader's temperament; mortal succession; schism; and the chosen-faction anointing); **cultures — the gravity of belonging** (a culture as a gravity well one is born inside of — venerated Spheres, reach leanings, generated names/mores/banners; cultural gravity as alike/opposed pull; the four tensions mismatch/conquest/dual/fanaticism as narrative texture, not a war mechanic); **secrets & favors — the currency you can't see** (8 secret types + favors, generated at discovery from real graph state, weighing as leverage that tilts a social encounter; divine acts to reveal/plant/call-in; perishable — minor secrets fade, unpaid favors sour then are forgiven). Designer Notes carry code-true IMPL/DESIGN flags. **Accuracy corrections grepped from code, not the gathering agent's blanket verdicts:** (1) **schism is IMPL, not DESIGN** — `SCHISM_WEIGHT_COHESION`/`SPREAD`/`DISSENT` = 0.4/0.4/0.2 are defined in `src/data/game-config.ts:294` and consumed by `phaseSchismResolution.ts`+`factionTopology.ts` (`decideSchismOutcome` → `performFactionSplit`/`performFactionReform`); the agent's "TBD/DESIGN" verdict was wrong. (2) **Secrets & favors are real social leverage (IMPL), not OPEN** — `socialLeverage.ts` (THR-30) adds `secretMagnitude × SECRET_LEVERAGE_MULTIPLIER 0.30` + `favorMagnitude × FAVOR_LEVERAGE_MULTIPLIER 0.25` (plus power/rank bonuses, clamped) at encounter start, and the divine actions `action.secrets.reveal_secret`/`plant_secret`/`call_in_favor` are wired through `graphOpExecutor.ts`; the agent's "call-in-favor not wired/OPEN" verdict was wrong. **Misnomer flagged:** the plan titles the domain "secret generation + clue decay," but secret decay in code is a **binary fade** of minor stale secrets (`phaseSecretsFavors`, `SECRET_DECAY_THRESHOLD` 0.20 / `SECRET_MAX_AGE_TICKS` 100 / check every 10 ticks) — there is no per-tick magnitude erosion; the separate `clue_decay` orchestrator phase (`ruins/clueLifecycle.ts`) is the unrelated **ruin/mystery** clue lifecycle, not the secrets economy. **Honest scope:** cultures shape narrative texture + settlement identity via gravity/tension helper calls at encounter scoring (no dedicated culture tick phase); a mechanical culture-drift lever on agent decision-making is the DESIGN edge (flagged, not opened as a new question). Chosen-faction powers (THR-513) documented precisely: a domain-locked `anoint` flags `chosen`, and `phaseChosenFactionPowers` (Phase 6.56, after reputation decay) grants a power-keyed `CHOSEN_POWER_EFFECT_TABLE` reputation bonus (0.002–0.005/tick, gold highest) to all members. 14-row constants table (`FACTION_REPUTATION_GAIN_PER_STEP` 0.04 / `_COMPLETION_BONUS` 0.08 / `_DECAY_PER_TICK` 0.001 / `_INACTIVITY_GRACE_TICKS` 36, `FACTION_AMBITION_EVALUATION_INTERVAL` 5, `EXPANSION_PROSPERITY_THRESHOLD` 0.6, `SCHISM_WEIGHT_*` 0.4/0.4/0.2, `GRAVITY_*_WEIGHT` 0.4/0.4/0.2, `DUAL_CULTURE_THRESHOLD`/`FANATICISM_THRESHOLD` 0.15/0.8, `SECRET`/`FAVOR_LEVERAGE_MULTIPLIER` 0.30/0.25, `SECRET_DECAY_THRESHOLD`/`_MAX_AGE_TICKS` 0.20/100, `FAVOR_TENSION_THRESHOLD_TICKS`/`_MAX_AGE_TICKS` 30/80, `MAX_SECRETS`/`MAX_FAVORS_PER_AGENT` 10/8) + canon (rulebook §2) / UL (Agents "Faction", Graph edge types) / adjacent-Manual links (no open questions minted). ✅ **THR-595** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W10) — **`public/agents-reference.html` — "The Mortals".** Tenth Manual-section page (Tier 2, the living world), authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W10 (source spine `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` §2 "What the World Is" + the UL Agents shard). Player-manual "How it plays" (7 sections): **people, not pieces** (mortals pursue their own goals; a god nudges, never puppets); **how a mortal chooses** (needs-first ordering, values-driven, *seeded probabilistic* selection — the strongest pulls most likely but room for surprise, and "do nothing" is a valid mortal choice); **who they are** (the nine virtue↔flaw value pairs — eight Reach-bound Iron Protector↔Conqueror … Star Martyr↔Survivor plus the meta `courage_prudence` Vanguard↔Watcher, neither pole "bad"); **the slow tilt** (moral drift — choices nudge an agent along their Reach axis, most fading back to baseline, a rare formative mark permanent, a sustained lean crystallizing into a named emergent trait so reputation lags behaviour); **ambitions** (seven shapes dominion/mastery/vengeance/legacy/survival/discovery/devotion, 0–2 per mortal, theirs not yours); **growth & mentorship** (capability grows through doing with diminishing returns; the five terminal mentorship arcs Graduation/The Surpassing/Falling Out/Quiet Parting/Dissolution); **birth, death & becoming named** (the ambient→notable→spotlight tiers, importance earned by mattering, how a background face graduates into a named individual — how The First begins). Designer Notes carry 7 IMPL / 2 DESIGN / 4 OPEN code-true flags. **Accuracy corrections grepped from code, not the plan's blanket flags:** the rulebook's "six-layer Maslow pipeline (survival→…→transcendence)" is *framing* — the code is a **Maslow-*inspired*** shape (`src/engine/agentSelection.ts` header "Maslow-inspired pipeline": axiological-alignment scoring + a survival override `survivalThreshold: 0.8` at `src/engine/unifiedActionPhases.ts:38`) plus the per-tick encounter selection (`phaseAgentDecision.ts` + `encounterScoring.ts`); the named layers safety/belonging/esteem/self-actualization appear only in prose content, never the decision loop; utility-function AI and behavior trees are the confirmed-absent rejected approaches; determinism holds (seeded roll, a deprecated `Math.random()` fallback only).
