✅ **THR-584** (2026-07-04, Agent Success Redesign) — **Critical afterimages for the top-20 hot encounter templates (outcome-ladder §C2).** Built `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-encounter-balance-outcome-ladder.md` §C2. Authored both critical bands (`criticalSuccessAfterimage` / `criticalFailureAfterimage`) on the climactic step of the 20 templates with the highest selection share — derived from the committed `Docs/playtests/kpi/2026-07-03-kpi-report.json` `templateConcentration.byTemplate` funnel aggregated across seeds 42/99/7 (top-20: confront_the_unknown 119, grand_tournament 80, arcane_resonance_study 79, master_local_craft 68, mason_lord_wall 66, comet_omen 60, weave_political_alliance 60, plague_outbreak 57, master_craftsman_challenge 49, guild_initiation_trial 46, barter_with_travelers 41, brink_rescue 39, verdict_that_burns 38, flawed_steel 37, shadow_in_the_night 34, tavern_brawl 32, dragons_challenge 30, pilgrims_offering 28, the_oracle_consulted 27, mend_equipment 27). 13 live in `src/data/encounter-content.ts` (linear, legacy `EncounterEntry` raw format) and 7 in `src/data/encounters/*.ts` (branching `ActionStep`). The `ActionStep` fields already existed and are consumed at `src/engine/encounter.ts:100-103` (`selectBandAfterimage`); this issue fills the rare tails so a critical outcome no longer falls back to generic prose once THR-571 revives them (the sparse-authoring flag raised in the W7 manual page, THR-592). **Small additive wiring:** exposed the two optional fields on the `EncounterEntry` step type and mapped them through `toUnifiedTemplate` — the only way the 13 legacy-format linear templates could carry them (the `ActionStep` runtime schema was untouched). Voice per `Docs/canon/prose.md`: crit-success = rare brilliance (the champion kneels, the reading opens a door, the wall rises flawless); crit-failure = **cool failure with a hook** (a doubt planted that travels home, a rival who now knows what you reached for, a mistranslation that seeds a future dispute, a masterwork whose fatal flaw becomes an apprentice's cautionary tale). 56 afterimages authored across the 20 templates; branching templates carry both bands on every outcome variant (spread `fallback` copies included → 70 total strings scored). **Verified:** every authored afterimage scored against the real prose-quality scorer (`scoreProseEntry`, `src/engine/content-eval/proseQualityScore.ts`, contentType `encounter`) via a throwaway bundled harness — **70 strings (35 success + 35 failure), all band PASS, 0 FAIL / 0 WARN**, 20/20 templates covered; full suite **12189 green (802 files)** incl. `lint-encounter-content` (the encounter-contract prose linter) + `unified-action-templates` (70 tests); vite build green; `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; 30-tick CLI engine smoke (seed 42, medium) clean — tick 30, 391 agents, 52 events, no exceptions. **Browser-verify exempt:** content-only, no file under `src/components`/`src/views` touched — the prose-QA scorer report is the verification artifact (stated in commit body). **Known gap (noted, not fixed):** `collectAuthoredProse` does not sweep step-level afterimage fields, so these never appear in the DebugPanel Prose QA tab / `__DEBUG.proseQualityReport()` corpus — verified via the scoped scorer instead; extending the collector risks surfacing pre-existing base-afterimage FAILs and is out of scope for this content ticket. Parallel-safe with THR-571 (touches template content only; THR-571 touches the resolution engine). Engine N/A (fields pre-existing) · Content = the 56 authored afterimages · UI N/A (consumed by the existing encounter stage). Parent project: Agent Success Redesign.

✅ **THR-603** (2026-07-04, Attention Tier Model) — **Encounter density vision & Chapter Ledger.** Built plan `Docs/plans/2026-07-04-encounter-density-and-chapter-ledger.md` (intent-judge Allow, forked audit PASS/PASS/PASS-with-notes). Settles the encounter-density open question — density is **player-authored** (more threads/interventions → more chapters, never rationed) with a gentle world-pressure lean as doom climbs — and builds the surface that makes many concurrent chapters manageable: a persistent, always-readable **Chapter Ledger**. **Engine:** new `src/types/chapterRecord.ts` (`ChapterRecord`/`ChapterStepRecord`/`ChapterParticipant`, deliberately off `unifiedAction.ts`'s 278-importer blast radius) + `src/engine/chapterArchive.ts` — `buildChapterRecord` distils a resolving (or live active) encounter into a compact record, snapshotting per-step prose **post-`enrichProse()`** (via `gatherNarrativeContext`/`resolveStepDefinition`/`stepOutcomeToOutcomeBand`, mirroring the live encounter stage adapter) so the ledger never re-enriches on render; `appendChapters` caps at `CHAPTER_ARCHIVE_CAP`=2000 / `CHAPTER_ARCHIVE_EVICT_BATCH`=100 evicting oldest **non-threaded** first (keeps player-invested chapters); `isEncounterAction` (`getAnyEncounterById ‖ isBranchingTemplate`) / `getChapterTemplateName` / `emitChapterArchivedTrace`. Wired at the orchestrator resolved-action cleanup **before** the prune — `RESOLVED_ACTION_RETENTION_TICKS`=20 **untouched**, decoupling "always readable" from retention. Additive `chapterArchive?: readonly ChapterRecord[]` on `GameState`. **Gentle doom ramp:** `CURATION_PHASE_MULTIPLIERS` (`branchingConstants.ts`, `{call .9, road_of_trials 1, crisis 1.15, ordeal 1.3, return 1.5}`, set all 1.0 to disable) × `BRANCHING_CURATOR_BIAS_WEIGHT` via new `runtime.curationPhaseMultiplier`, set once/tick in `runTick` from `getJourneyPhase(doomClock.progress)`, read in `computeBranchingCuratorMultiplier` (only affects branching-quest surfacing). **UI:** `ChapterLedger.tsx` (one list merging active `unifiedActions` + archived `chapterArchive`, newest-first, threaded/all filter, `CHAPTER_LEDGER_PAGE_SIZE`=25 pagination; reused **embedded** in the AgentProfileModal **Chapters** tab filtered to that entity as actor-or-participant) + `ChapterView.tsx` (full reading surface — per-step narrative + afterimage in the narrative lexicon never raw numbers, "you whispered…" choices, complication prose, aftermath prose + polarity-coloured changes, every cast member clickable → profile) mounted in `GameView` with a right-sidebar "📖 Chapter Ledger" open button; `game.chapter-ledger`/`game.chapter-view` IA surfaces (mount `drillin`) + `game.agent-profile-modal` reads[] updated. **Trace:** `encounter.chapter_archived` in `TraceCategory`+`TRACE_CATEGORIES`+typed `ChapterArchivedTrace`; `curationPhaseMultiplier?` added to `BranchingCuratorNudgeTrace`. **Debug/CLI:** `__DEBUG.getChapterArchive(filter?)` (`debug-bridge.ts`/`.d.ts`); CLI `chapters [agent|@hero]`. **Content/canon:** "three to six per session" retired as doctrine in `public/encounters-manual-reference.html`, `public/turn-structure-reference.html`, `public/run-lifecycle-reference.html`, `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` §3 + open-Q #4 (density half **RESOLVED**, stopping-point signal stays open), and a new **Density** section in `Vision/01-core-loop.md` (distinguishes player-provoked density from scan-over-surfacing; "always readable ≠ always surfaced — the bookshelf is full, the stage holds one scene"). **Verified:** full suite **12189 green (802 files)** incl. new `src/engine/__tests__/chapterArchive.test.ts` (6 tests — eviction policy, encounter-identity, and the integration proving a resolved encounter is archived and stays readable past retention while its `UnifiedAction` is pruned); vite build green; `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (3539=3539 baseline, all new files clean); 30-tick CLI engine smoke (seed 42, medium) clean — 30 chapters archived from real play + `chapters` command renders archived+active. **Browser-verified @1920×1080** (Claude-in-Chrome — `preview_screenshot` times out on the WebGL game view): screenshots of the Chapter Ledger (8 rows merging active + resolved) and Chapter View (steps/outcomes/aftermath/clickable cast); `__DEBUG.getChapterArchive()` grew 0→8 with rich records; console clean (no errors/exceptions). Consciously deferred: the DebugPanel inline archive list (redundant with the wired `__DEBUG` bridge + CLI; not worth threading a prop through GameView's large tree). Noted pre-existing (not this ticket): a few encounter templates use non-standard `{actor}`/`{adj}`/`{verb}` placeholders `enrichProse` doesn't substitute — render literally in the live encounter stage too (identical path); content-authoring follow-up. Filed UL-proposal for the **Chapter Ledger** / **Chapter Record** terms. Engine · Content · UI all covered. Parent project: Attention Tier Model.

✅ **THR-594** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W9) — **`public/interface-reference.html` — "Reading the Interface".** Ninth Manual-section page, authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W9 — the interface tour whose **synthesis authority is the IA manifest, not the rulebook** (`src/data/ia-manifest.ts`, `IA_SURFACES` = 62 committed surfaces, browsable in-game at `?view=cms#ia-surfaces`; viewport contract from CLAUDE.md § Viewport Contract, enforced at `src/index.css:201-202`). ✅ **THR-593** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W8) — **`public/clocks-reference.html` — "Doom, the Mandate & Omens".** Eighth Manual-section page, authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W8 (source spine `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` **§8 "The Clocks"** + §9 "Winning and Losing"). Player-manual "How it plays": **two clocks, opposite directions** (the Doom Clock counting down to an Unmaking that always lands vs the Victory Mandate counting up toward your declared win; one essence pool, two demands — "that is the point"); **the Doom Clock — seven shapes of an ending** (the seven `DOOM_CLOCK_ARCHETYPES` breach/convergence/changing/sundering/failing/ascension/reckoning as archetype cards, each named for its catastrophe and each renaming the five generic stages Whispers→Signs→Tremors→Crisis→Culmination in its own voice; escalations as world-changing events); **the Victory Mandate** (declared graph-state win, three stages setup→escalation→culmination, drawn from the god you remembered being); **one pool, two clocks** (the tension made concrete — mandate checkpoints pinned to doom progress bank counter-omens that soften the next escalation, or owe severity that bites harder); **omens — the pressure made visible** (short atmospheric tracks, max two slots, doom-echo/seasonal primary + sphere-surge/cultural secondary) as the softest of three registers alongside doom escalation cards and the First's doom-scheduled journey beats; **both endings make the next world** (neither clock is a fail state). Designer Notes carry 16 IMPL/DESIGN/OPEN flags, code-true: `DOOM_CLOCK_ARCHETYPES` (7, `src/types/doomClock.ts:13`) + `DOOM_STAGE_NAMES` (5, per-archetype thematic renames in `src/data/doom/*.json`, uniform 0.20/0.40/0.60/0.80/1.0 thresholds) + `DoomCardEffectType` (6) + severity `max(DOOM_MIN_SEVERITY 0.5, 1 + penalty − counterOmens × DOOM_COUNTER_OMEN_SOFTENING 0.5)` + `tickModifier` (player decel / rival accel, floor 0.1); `doomIdentityMilestones.ts` trigger IMPL / matrix DESIGN; Mandate 3-stage / 4-type (`src/types/mandate.ts`), sphere_growth evaluator live (`MANDATE_PRIMARY_TARGET_DELTA`=0.18, `MANDATE_CHECKPOINT_THRESHOLDS`=[.4,.6,.8,1], `MANDATE_COUNTER_OMEN_MARGIN`=0.02), narrative type unwired (DESIGN), 12 templates; the Omen Agenda (`OmenState` 2 slots / 4 categories, `OMEN_ENCOUNTER_BIAS_CAP`=0.4, `OMEN_SPHERE_PRESSURE_CAP`=0.05, `OMEN_MAX_HISTORY`=20). **Honest-status nuance grepped from code, not the plan's blanket flags:** the doom↔mandate exchange *mechanism* is **IMPL** — `phaseMandate.ts:334–339` writes `counterOmens`/`nextEscalationSeverityModifier` straight into `state.doomClock` — and only the exchange-rate *tuning* is the open question (rulebook Open Q #5, deferred to KPI 2026-06-23); the `narrative` mandate type has no evaluator (`custom` always false, DESIGN); Ascension doom-echo omens are deferred (THR-79) and 6/12 sphere-surge tracks are stubs; `phaseOmenAgenda` runs **inline** in `orchestrator.ts:2006` while `doomPhase`/`mandatePhase`/`emittedOmenDecayPhase` are registry-wired (`pre-doom`/`post-narrative`/`post-doom`). 13-row constants table + canon (rulebook §8–9, cosmology.md for sphere flavour) / UL (Cosmology, Encounters shards) / adjacent-Manual links (no open questions minted — Open Q #5 & #6 referenced only). ✅ **THR-592** (2026-07-04, Game Manual Wiki — W7) — **`public/encounters-manual-reference.html` — "Encounters & Aftermath".** Seventh Manual-section page, authored to the dual-layer template in `Docs/plans/2026-07-03-game-manual-wiki.md` §5 W7 — the **player-voice layer over the deep `encounters-agents-reference.html`** page (source spine `Docs/canon/rulebook.md` **§7 "Encounters and Aftermath"** + `Docs/canon/encounters.md` as content-spec authority + `Docs/ubiquitous-language/Encounters.md` for terms; Step-0 canon page loaded first per the encounter-content protocol). Player-manual "How it plays": the encounter as a **framed chapter** (the curated middle beat of scan→moment→aftermath, rationed at 3–6/session not per-tick); **two shapes, one format** (branching vs recurring template, both `UnifiedActionTemplate` — the distinction matters to authors, not the god who lives them); **hex-granular awareness** as two cards ("see the hex, see everything on it" + the honest note that awareness is *reach on the map* and never hides content as a design lever, THR-138); **one curve for peasants and gods** — the single resolution machine, four **outcome bands** + a *success-at-a-cost* middle + doubles-driven critical swings; **aftermath reshapes the mortal not you** (the cool-failure rule — failure is plot, not punishment); and the shape of a good choice (the four design rules as cards + the player-as-god framing callout). Designer Notes carry 17 code-true flags (IMPL/DESIGN/OPEN): the **sigmoid vs d100 split represented precisely** — the sigmoid lives in `src/engine/domainCapability.ts` (`SIGMOID_MIDPOINT`=10 / `SIGMOID_K`=0.4, producing the 0–1 Domain Capability) while the d100 resolves against a **linear threshold** `Math.floor(probability × 100)` clamped `[PROBABILITY_FLOOR 0.05, PROBABILITY_CEILING 0.95]` with **Phase-2 doubles crits** (matched digits ≤/> threshold → critical success/failure) in `src/engine/resolutionService.ts`, plus scale tempering (`SCALE_DIFFICULTY_OFFSETS`, `MIN_PROBABILITY_BY_SCALE`, `CRIT_FAILURE_PERMITTED_BY_SCALE` — personal/local can't crit-fail) in `src/engine/resolutionScaleAdjust.ts`; the staged candidate filter cascade with `MAX_SCORED_CANDIDATES`=40 + `BRANCHING_CAP_RESERVE`=1 (`encounterFilterPipeline.ts`/`encounterScoring.ts`); hex awareness `filterByAwareness` (`AWARENESS_THRESHOLD`=0.05 / `BASE_AWARENESS_HOPS`=1 / `CAPABILITY_PER_HOP`=0.15 / `MAX_AWARENESS_HOPS`=5) in `encounterAwareness.ts`; encounter seeds (`PendingEncounterSeed` → `evaluateEncounterSeeds`, `caused_by`/`spawnedFromSeedId`) + hidden marks (`src/engine/hiddenMarks.ts`, held in `GameState.hiddenMarks[]`, `MARK_REVEAL_SCORING_BONUS` reveal + `MARK_DECAY_GRACE_TICKS`=20 / `MARK_DECAY_PER_TICK`=0.02 / `MARK_DECAY_FLOOR`=0.05 / `REVEAL_PROBABILITY_MULT`=0.9 decay in `phaseHiddenMarkDecay.ts`); aftermath reactions via `GraphOp` + `criticalSuccessAfterimage`/`criticalFailureAfterimage` consumed by `selectBandAfterimage` (`encounter.ts:100–103`, sparsely authored → THR-584); the four design rules verbatim + the binding player-as-god framing. **Two disambiguation notes surfaced (not minted as open questions):** (a) "dilemma" is used as the plain word — it is *not* the named Dilemma system, which is the separate divine-**meeting** mechanism (`src/engine/dilemmaSelection.ts`, `EnrichedDilemmaTemplate`, distinct from `UnifiedActionTemplate`); (b) the encounter-role "Court Position" name-collides with the influence `CourtPosition` — both flagged UL-disambiguation candidates. **Honest KPI reality check:** the THR-457 harness June-11 baseline of ~89% failure / 0 branching-fires-per-30t improved (June-22, `Docs/audits/2026-06-22-encounter-content-and-delivery-assessment.md`) to 29–33% failure (target ≤35% met) but branching ~0.5/30t (target ≥1 still red) and template concentration ~37% top-share (target ≤8% still red) — flagged as the delivery gap with a re-measure advised (June-22 tree was 32 commits behind main). 13-row constants table + canon/UL/Deep-Reference links (no open questions minted). Type-clean new code — `TimingTraceInput` distributes `Omit` per-interface so member-specific fields discriminate. **Phase 2 diagnosis (→ THR-581):** seed 42 `--map large`, `profile 80` — `agent_decision` dominates (291ms avg, 459ms max @ ~1026 agents) and the slowest tick (40, 1499ms) had **no** DM-rebuild, so the stall is super-linear `phaseAgentDecision`, **not** the plan's #1 rebuild-storm hypothesis; separately the distance matrix is capped at 1200 while large maps reach ~1777 locations mid-run (577+ unindexed → `getDistance→Infinity`). Deferrable ~46-inline-phase migration tail → THR-582. ✅ **THR-577** (2026-07-03, Architecture Decomposition — THR-572 Phase 1) — **Last two module-scope engine caches migrated to `SimulationRuntime`.** The detail-page render cache (`detailPageGenerator._detailCache`) and entity-prose cache (`proseGenerator._proseCache`) — the residual module-global singletons that persisted across game sessions — now live on `SimulationRuntime` as `detailPageCache`/`proseCache` (+ `detailPageCacheTick`/`proseCacheTick` guards, initialised in `createSimulationRuntime`, cleared in `resetRuntimeCaches`), following the shipped `foreshadowingCache` pattern and satisfying the load-bearing "engine caches must be owned per session, not stored at module scope" decision (kills cross-session bleed). Both accessors gained an optional trailing `runtime?: SimulationRuntime | null` — present → cache used, absent → prose/page composed fresh (matching the `foreshadowingCache`/`threadStoryCache` graceful degradation); the module `_lastCachedTick`/`clear*Cache()` exports were removed. `runtime` threaded from `GameView` into `LocationView` (+ its `SublocationDetailView` child), `HexChronicle`, and `AgentInfoCard`. KPI's `BRANCHING_TEMPLATE_CACHE`/`RICH_TEMPLATE_CACHE` **intentionally** kept at module scope with a justification comment — provably static across sessions (a pure function of the compile-time template registry: a given templateId always classifies identically), so per-session ownership would add threading cost with zero correctness benefit. Detail-page opener path (`useOpenDetailPage`) is currently production-dormant (no `DetailPageOpenerProvider` mounted), so the migrated detail cache is exercised by tests + ready for when the opener is wired; the live prose cache is exercised via `LocationView`/`HexChronicle`. Tests updated to construct a runtime and assert cache behaviour on it (`resetRuntimeCaches` clears; no-runtime → uncached-but-deterministic). Full suite **12183 green (801 files)**, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 391 agents, 53 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt: additive optional-prop threading; deterministic prose output byte-identical; covered by 72+ passing component tests. Engine pillar = the migration; Content pillar N/A; UI pillar = prop threading only. Parent epic: Architecture Decomposition (THR-572).

✅ **THR-567** (2026-07-02, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — wave-2 #8, final integration) — **Character-sheet personality section: layered born→marked→becoming→now.** Extends the shipped `OverviewTab` Personality section (intimate+) beyond the THR-562 "becoming" chips to tell the full moral story. **(a) Live per-axis position:** new local `MoralAxisRow` compact bar (mirrors the Core section's `CoreContinuumRow`; virtue=gold / vice=rose marker) rendered per moral axis with signal, reading `card.axiologicalProfile` (signed ±1 → 0–1 via `signedToCanonical01`). **(b) Contributing rows grouped under their axis:** the enumerable "born as → marked by" provenance. New `card.personalityContributors` on `AgentInfoCardData` fed by an additive read-only `getPersonalityContributorDisplays` (`agentDetail.ts`, same intimate+ gate): origin vignettes resolved from `node.originVignettes` via a new `getOriginVignetteById` (`origin-vignettes.ts`, THR-561), **and** permanent traits carrying `axisContributions` (the slot a formative-mark trait uses, THR-529). Fail-soft: unknown vignette id / unknown axis id skipped; born-neutral agent → section shows whatever emergent chips exist. **Landed-code correction:** the THR-529 `axiological_mark_apply` effect moves the baseline *in place* with no trait/provenance — its contribution is not separately enumerable, only the live axis position reflects it (documented in the builder). The emergent `personalityTraits` path + becoming beat are untouched; near-neutral axes are filtered by `AXIS_SIGNAL_EPSILON` so a bland agent isn't padded with eight flat tracks. 4 new/extended tests (`agentDetail.test.ts` builder + gate, `AgentProfileModal.test.tsx` render); full suite **12181 green (801 files)**, vite build green, `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (3547=3547, stash-diff proven). Engine pillar N/A (one additive UI-read builder, no simulation change); Content pillar N/A (vignettes/marks already authored); UI pillar is the whole issue. ✅ **THR-562** (2026-07-02, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — wave-2 #3) — **Threshold → emergent personality traits + hysteresis: notification + UI completion.** The engine (registered `personalityTraitEmergePhase`, hysteresis grant/release, 16 `subcategory:'personality'` trait defs, `personality_trait_emerged` trace + "becoming" TickEvent) shipped under THR-527 and was re-based onto THR-559's unified live position — so #3's real delta was the two pillars THR-527 (engine-only) left. **Notification:** the becoming TickEvent carried no `notification` directive so `routeNotifications` silently dropped it — added `notification:{channel:'toast'}` in `becomingEvent()` (toast, not alert, so distinct same-tick becomings survive the alert icon+tick dedup; `actorId`→agent nav) + a `personality_trait_emerged → 'lifecycle'` case in `eventTypeToCategory`. **UI:** new `card.personalityTraits` on `AgentInfoCardData` (`agentDetail.getPersonalityTraitDisplays`, intimate+, from `subcategory:'personality'` has_trait edges, pole/reach parsed from `trait.personality.<reach>.<pole>`), pulled out of the generic `allTraits` chips; the live sheet `OverviewTab` renders a distinguished **Personality** section (virtue=gold / vice=muted-rose chips, flavor-on-hover). Targets the live `AgentProfileModal`/`OverviewTab` — the issue's literal `AgentDetailPanel` is orphaned dead code; `personality` added to the category color/label maps there + the CMS registry for completeness. 4 new/extended tests; full suite **12178 green (801 files)**, vite build green, `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (3547=3547, stash-diff proven), 30-tick CLI smoke clean (+40-tick: 83 personality trait edges, becoming events carry the toast directive). Browser-verified @1920×1080 (production `AgentProfileModal` from a live-state transparent card — Personality section shows Disloyal/Misleading/Perceptive, excluded from Traits; Chronicle shows the becoming beats). Parent epic: Agent Personality & Moral Drift.

✅ **THR-470** (2026-06-30, Encounter Format Migration) — **Seed-99 branching fire rate: root cause was a broken KPI metric, not awareness.** `firesPerChunk` counted resolved branching actions from the `RESOLVED_ACTION_RETENTION_TICKS=20`-pruned `state.unifiedActions` but divided by the full tick count (~9× undercount on long runs). Added `SimulationRuntime.branchingFiresTotal` (incremented per newly-resolved branching action before pruning); `firesPerChunk` reads the lifetime counter (windowed fallback for pure-state tests). Seed 99 now 3.33/30t ✓, 42 4.67 ✓, 7 4.17 ✓. The handoff's awareness-floor lever was investigated and rejected (never under-firing). 2 KPI tests; full suite 11975 green; engine/CLI only, browser-verify exempt. ✅ **THR-561** (2026-07-02, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — wave-2 #2) — **`axisContributions` field + origin-vignette birth seeding.** Wired the origin-vignette content library's (`origin-vignettes.ts`, 144 entries, THR-539) long-missing consumer. New pure `personality/originBaseline.ts` (`drawOriginVignettes`, `vignetteAxisContribution`, `sumAxisContributions`, `computeOriginBaseline`) + idempotent tick phase `personalityOriginSeed.ts` (slot `post-economy`, `beforePhase:['personality_trait_emerge']`) that draws `ORIGIN_VIGNETTES_PER_AGENT`=5 distinct vignettes per mortal via `mulberry32(hash(seed,id))` and lays their signed canonical-0–1 axis contributions — plus any permanent-trait `axisContributions` — **onto** the reach-axis baseline (`AxiologicalProfile`), additive over the generated spread (mirroring Core's `seedCoreProfileWithVignettes`), recording provenance on `node.properties.originVignettes`. New optional `axisContributions?: Partial<Record<string,number>>` on `TraitDefinitionProperties` (per canonical axis id, kept separate from `domainContributions`; consumed only by the baseline computation — forward-carrier for formative-mark traits). New `personality_origin_seeded` trace, emitted as ONE aggregate per tick (`details.kind`: `seeded`|`unknown_axis`) — never per-agent (ring-buffer discipline), auto-surfacing as a DebugPanel chip. Fail-soft: empty pool→unchanged, unknown axis→skip+count. 21 new tests + `phaseRegistry.equivalence` baseline updated; full suite **12175 green (801 files)**, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 391 agents, 53 events) clean + seeding verified firing. Browser-verify exempt: types/data + engine only. Blocked-by THR-559 (Done). Parent epic: Agent Personality & Moral Drift.

✅ **THR-530** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — wave-2 #6) — **Autonomous in-encounter choice (the one genuinely new subsystem).** Non-hero agents previously resolved encounters mechanically and their authored aftermath reactions were never applied. New pure `reactionChooser.ts` (`computeAxisLeans` from `axiologicalProfile` baseline+drift; `inferReactionLean` from each reaction's reach-pole effects; `chooseAlignedReaction` deterministic argmax with lowest-index tie-break → `reactions[0]` when unaligned) + new `autonomous_aftermath` tick phase (slot `post-resolution`) applying the chosen reaction, nudging drift toward the chosen pole, emitting `reaction_selected`; idempotent via `UnifiedAction.autonomousAftermathApplied`. Constants `PERSONALITY_REACTION_WEIGHT` (kill lever) etc. 29 tests; full suite 11966 green; 300-tick run fired the chooser 51× with non-index-0 variety. Engine-only, browser-verify exempt. Parent epic: Agent Personality & Moral Drift.

✅ **THR-528** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — wave-2 #4) — **Choice → drift: authored poles + apply + decay-to-baseline.** `AuthoredChoiceCard` gains authored `moralAxis`/`pole`/`magnitude` (retiring the inferred heuristic as the primary path); 4 branching encounters migrated as proof (incl. a `withdrawn`→vice divergence). Shared resolvers `resolveEncounterArchetypePole`/`resolveChoiceDrift`; new `liveAxisPosition(baseline, drift)` + `driftDeltaFor` — live = clamp(baseline+drift) on ±1, drift a temporary delta decaying to zero so the live position rests at the (mutable) baseline, not neutral. `phaseChoiceResolution` reads the baseline + emits `livePosition`; `phaseDriftDecay` uses `PERSONALITY_DRIFT_DECAY_PER_TICK`. 14 tests; full suite 11945 green. Engine/content only, browser-verify exempt. Parent epic: Agent Personality & Moral Drift.

✅ **THR-559** (2026-07-02, Agent Personality & Moral Drift — issue #1) — **Canonical axis registry: keying + scalar-unification foundation.** Made the axis registry the single source of truth for the moral-axis **key** and **canonical scale**, and unified the two divergent live-position computations onto one accessor. **Registry (`axisRegistry.ts`):** `reachToAxisId`/`axisIdToReach` (canonical `${reach}_axis`; non-moral reaches pass through), `getAxisByValuePair`, and `signedToCanonical01`/`canonical01ToSigned` — the one bridge between signed ±1 internal storage and the 0–1 author/UI scale. **Drift keying reconciled:** `ArchetypeDrift.axisId` keyed by the canonical id at all production sites (`phaseChoiceResolution`, `phaseAutonomousAftermath`, `reactionChooser.computeAxisLeans`) — writers+readers move together so behavior is unchanged; `archetype_drift_register` handler canonicalizes the authored `axisId` (bare reach or canonical id both match; no content authors it). **Unified live position:** `personalityTraitEmerge` now reads `clamp(baseline+drift)` via the shared accessor (retiring the parallel baseline-only read + the duplicated `toLivePosition`) → `signedToCanonical01`; `state.archetypeDrift ?? []` fail-soft added. **Naming sites reconciled:** contract-test guards assert `REACH_VALUE_PAIR` == registry `valuePair` bridge and `MORAL_AXIS_POLES_BY_REACH` covers exactly the 8 registry reaches (2 distinct poles each), plus the Seer(Veil→Eye)/Manipulator(Veil→Shadow) relocation. **Grey-zone honored:** storage stays signed ±1; ±1→0–1 storage migration + EncounterArchetypePole/ARCHETYPE_NAMES content-vocab rename are optional destructive follow-ups. Full suite **12154 green** (1 pre-existing intermittent `doomIdentityMilestones` flake — reproduced on a clean stashed baseline with 0 changes, so not from this change), `tsc --noEmit` exit 0 + `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (source files clean), vite build green (31s), 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt: engine/types + tests only (character-sheet is THR-532). **Mis-sequencing noted:** shipped *after* its dependents THR-527/528/539 — a retroactive consolidation, not an unblocker. Parent epic: Agent Personality & Moral Drift.

✅ **THR-490** (2026-07-01, Content Architecture — deferral of THR-472) — **DebugPanel "Prose QA" tab: real-time audit of the authored-content library against the prose-quality scorer.** New pure `collectAuthoredProse()` sweeps the static authored tables (encounters, spells, omens, possessions, conditions) into a deduped 864-entry `EvalInput[]` corpus (per-table fail-soft, globally-unique ids, `mechanicalSummary` excluded), fed to the shipped THR-472 `scoreProseBatch`. New dev-only `prose-quality`/`Prose QA` tab (`ProseQualityView.tsx`) renders a summary band (864: 496 pass / 157 warn / 211 fail / 0 error), band-filter chips, type/id filters, worst-first sortable table (capped 200), and expandable per-entry flag breakdown with quoted evidence + worst-tail badges. `__DEBUG.proseQualityReport()` / `scoreProseEntry(id)` mirror the report for scripting. Constants named, deterministic, fail-soft, additive (NFP 1/2/3/4/6). 7 collector unit tests; full suite **12142 green (799 files)**, `tsc -b` 0 net-new, `vite build` green. Browser-verified @1920×1080 (Playwright): tab open with summary + table + expanded flags, `summary.total=864`, console clean (0 errors). Made the offline-only THR-472 scorer legible in-session — directors see how the real content library scores against the bar, worst entries first, without leaving the game. Parent THR-472 (Done). Project: Content Architecture.

✅ **THR-535** (2026-07-01, Agent Coordination Protocol) — **CI backstop: block flush PRs that carry issue-closing keywords (defense-in-depth for the flush → false auto-close bug, recurred 4×).** The plan-doc flush → false auto-close bug (THR-501/519/525 + the 2026-06-26 batch) kept regressing because the only protection was the *absence* of a `Closes/Fixes/Resolves` keyword in the flush PR body — a text convention in `flush-plan-docs/SKILL.md` that drifts, and GitHub's **native** Linear integration closes on the keyword regardless of the custom `linear-autoclose.yml` guard. **Infrastructure:** new **blocking** GitHub Actions workflow `.github/workflows/flush-close-guard.yml` (`pull_request` opened/edited/reopened/synchronize) fails any flush-pattern PR (branch `docs/plan-flush-*` **or** title `docs(plan): batch flush`) whose title/body/commit messages carry `Closes|Fixes|Resolves THR-NNN` **or** the `Closes <linear-issue-url>` form — the exact URL vector that closed THR-525, which the custom guard's `extractCloseableIssueIds` deliberately ignores but the native integration acts on. Because the check blocks the merge, the native integration never sees the keyword, covering **all** flush paths (primary batch flush + the SKILL.md Step 2f.4 fallback template + any future template regression), not just the one THR-534 patched at source. Match logic single-sourced in `scripts/linearAutoclose.mjs`: new `extractNativeCloseableIssueIds` (bare-id + URL forms) + `evaluateFlushCloseGuard` (blocked iff docs-flush context AND a native-closeable ref present), imported by the workflow's inline github-script so it stays consistent with `isDocsFlushContext`. 13 new unit tests (all 4 Done-when cases: `docs(plan): batch flush` + `Closes THR-NNN` → fail, `docs/plan-flush-*` branch + `Closes <url>` → fail, bare `THR-NNN` → pass, `docs: closeout THR-NN` → pass; plus commit-message, plain-URL, and empty-context edges). `scripts/__tests__/linearAutoclose.test.ts` **24 tests green**; full suite green, `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0, `vite build` green. `flush-plan-docs/SKILL.md` cross-references the guard (mirrored to `.agents/`); `Docs/impediments.md` #162 resolution logged. **Browser-verify N/A:** CI/workflow tooling, no runtime UI. **Manual follow-up (needs admin):** add `Block flush PRs carrying close keywords` to `main` branch-protection required statuses — CC lacks branch-protection rights; advisory until then. Depends-on THR-534 (Done). Parent: Agent Coordination Protocol.

✅ **THR-541** (2026-07-01, Encounter Format Migration) — **THR-470 follow-up: `beatsPerChunk` windowed-numerator / full-tick-denominator KPI fix.** The `threaded_beats_per_10t` KPI shared the exact artifact THR-470 fixed for branching fires: `buildThreadedBeats` (`src/engine/kpi/gameplayKpi.ts`) counted rich resolved actions from `state.unifiedActions` — pruned after `RESOLVED_ACTION_RETENTION_TICKS=20` — but divided by the **full** tick count, undercounting runs longer than the window. **Fix (engine, same shape as THR-470):** added `SimulationRuntime.threadedBeatsTotal`, incremented once per newly-resolved "rich" action (multi-step ≥3 OR branching) at the orchestrator's resolved-action cleanup chokepoint (right beside the `branchingFiresTotal` increment, before pruning); `buildThreadedBeats` now prefers this lifetime counter, falling back to the windowed snapshot when no runtime is supplied (pure-state unit tests). Extracted the rich-template predicate into an exported cached `isRichTemplate(templateId)` (mirrors `isBranchingTemplate`), used by **both** the windowed fallback and the orchestrator increment so they classify identically; preserves the historical `getAnyEncounterById`-only resolution (branching templates in the unified set were already uncounted — behavior unchanged, only the window removed). `KpiRuntimeView` gains `threadedBeatsTotal?`. 2 new tests mirroring THR-470 (`prefers the runtime lifetime counter` @ tick 120 → 24 total / 2.0 per-10t; windowed fallback → 2 rich `encounter.deep_descent` beats, non-rich id excluded). Full suite **12124 green (798 files)**, `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. **Acceptance evidence:** on seed 42 at tick 60 the pruned snapshot held only 21 actions / 17 resolved yet the KPI reported `threadedBeats.total=14` (2.33 per-10t) — the cumulative counter tallying across all 60 ticks, surviving pruning. Browser-verify exempt: engine-only, `GameplayKpiReport` is a dev/diagnostic metric with no player-facing render path. Parent THR-470 (Done). Parent epic: Encounter Format Migration.

✅ **THR-523** (2026-07-01, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Reach-gated bucket catalogue + signature acquisition beats — the reach-signature epic's catalogue-consumer leaf.** **Content/data:** the 8 `invest.<reach>.<name>` signatures (THR-553/555) catalogued in `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` as `{ bucket:'reach-gated', requiresReach }` (drift-guarded against `REACH_SIGNATURE_CONTENT_TEMPLATES` + `REACH_SIGNATURE_ID_BY_REACH`, a new derived reach→id map), so the shipped `getTargetActionSlots` reach gate (THR-503) hides out-of-domain signatures; new Threadbare-voice pool-beat content template `beat.pool.invest.reach_signature` ("A Second Mastery"). **Engine:** the plan's "primary at Beat 4 / secondary at pool" doesn't map onto static data — the reach model is an affinity-bag (2–3 domains, no static primary/secondary) and a `selection` beat grants exactly one chosen id (Beat 4 can't grant both a god-path *and* a signature via selection). Solution: new `BeatDefinition.grantsReachSignature: 'primary'|'secondary'`, resolved per-run in `resolvePendingBeat` from the ascendant's affinity-ranked reaches (`resolveReachSignatureGrant` — affinity desc, `REACH_DOMAINS` tie-break, deterministic NFP #3) → the run's signature id, applied orthogonally to `grantsActionIds` (fail-soft no-op when a slot has no ranked reach). **Beat 4 "A Path Opens"** keeps its dreamer/prophet/patron `selection` *and* unconditionally grants the primary signature; new `beat.pool.invest.reach_signature` investment pool beat grants the secondary, gated by the new `unacquired_reach_signature` eligibility predicate (retires once all in-domain signatures learned). THR-505 coverage tests stay green (8 new ids bucketed + resolvable + `requiresReach` declared). 10 new tests (ranking + tie-break + fail-soft, dynamic grant on Beat 4 & pool beat, eligibility on/off, bucket↔template drift guard). Full suite **12122 green (798 files)**, `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 + `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (changed files clean; lone pre-existing `ascendantBeatSeeding.ts` unused-param error unchanged), vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. Browser-verify N/A: engine/content/data only, no `src/components/`|`src/views/` file touched (plan §5 marks UI N/A — beats render through the shipped `AscendantBeatModal`). Design deviation from the plan's literal "both selection beats" noted in the Linear closeout. Plan `Docs/plans/2026-06-30-ascendant-reach-signatures.md` §4.3–4.4. ✅ **THR-555** (2026-07-01, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **The 3 engine-backed reach signatures (Iron/Warhost, Veil/Rend the Gate, Stone/Great Work) — `UnifiedActionTemplate`s consuming the shipped effect trio.** The signatures that DO need a new engine effect (siblings THR-550/551/552 shipped the effects; THR-553 shipped the 5 content-only ones). **Content:** 3 templates authored in `src/data/reach-signature-content.ts`, spread into `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES`. `invest.iron.warhost` — aftermath `signature_warhost` on the target faction (mobilize + raise a force, sphere-scaled) **plus** a step `onSuccess` `anoint_faction` op → the shipped `chosen_status_grant` primitive (the "chosen banner"). `invest.veil.rend_the_gate` — a sustained `sphere_influence_amplify` rift at the target location. `invest.stone.great_work` — `spawn_unique_location` `master_forge` (one per run via `GREAT_WORK_UNIQUE_TAG`) + a legendary relic via `artifactForgeTier`. **Engine (wiring pillar):** the three shipped resolvers read a **literal** target id, so static templates declare sentinels bound at fire time by the new exported `bindReachSignatureTargets` pass (`encounterAftermath.ts`, called once at the aftermath dispatch loop head; no-op for every pre-THR-555 effect kind) — mirroring the `$target` idiom the step-level imbue/anoint ops already use: `$target`→`action.targetId` (warhost faction / rift location), `$primary`→the caster's primary Creation Sphere (the rift amplifies the ascendant's *own* sphere — a static template can't know it), Stone's `nearAgentId:'$target'`→the target location's hex (a location id is not an agent id). Sentinels live in the content module (one-way engine→content import, no cycle). **Content (individualization):** 3 bespoke `SIGNATURE_MATRIX` cells at each signature's reach × its primary Creation Sphere (REACH_TO_SPHERE: iron×force / veil×mind / stone×matter — the "bespoke veneer"), each an authored trigger + name + `SIGNATURE_BESPOKE_BASE_VALUE`=5 reach base layered with the sphere-flavored passive; the existing default-floor test now exempts bespoke cells. Reach-gated via the shipped `requiresReach` field (THR-503); **not** touching `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` (that catalogue + acquisition beats are THR-523). Constants NFP #1 (`GREAT_WORK_UNIQUE_TAG`, `SIGNATURE_BESPOKE_BASE_VALUE`, sentinels). 12 new/updated tests (5 template+matrix structure, 4 runtime target-binding integration proving each template resolves to its effect on the played-on target incl. `$primary`→caster sphere, +3 existing-suite touch-ups). ✅ **THR-551** (2026-07-01, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **New aftermath effect `sphere_influence_amplify` — Veil's "Rend the Gate" reach signature (sustained sphere rift, engine effect).** Veil's headline reach signature as a real *sustained* aftermath effect. **Engine:** `{ kind:'sphere_influence_amplify'; locationId; sphere:CreationSphereName; perTick?; durationMode:'sustained'; when? }` added to `EncounterAftermathReactionEffect` (`src/types/unifiedAction.ts`) with an exhaustive resolver case in `encounterAftermath.ts`. Unlike one-shot `signature_warhost`, the resolver **builds a `ControlEffect`** and appends it to `GameState.controlEffects[]` (new `nextControlEffects` accumulator), so the rift is ticked by `phaseControlEffects` and lives in the existing two-object control lifecycle (contestable, relic-buyable, lapses on essence depletion). Two **additive** `ControlSpec`/`ControlEffect` fields (`src/types/controlEffect.ts`) — `perTickSphereInfluence: { sphere; magnitude; cap }` (pushes scaled sphere pressure onto `targetNodeId` via the canonical pressure system while score < cap, additive to the generic `CONTROL_PRESSURE_PER_TICK`; deterministic, no PRNG) and `perTickLeak: { chance; corruption; entropyPressure }` (seeded per-tick chaos pulse → hex `corruption` mutation + `entropy` sphere pressure + a `narrative` tick event; PRNG `mulberry32(seed + tick·RIFT_LEAK_SEED_OFFSET=53 + hashString(effectId))` — deterministic/replayable; trace + event fire **only on a hit**, no per-tick no-op flood). Both fields carried through `spawnControlEffect` (`controlEffectSpawn.ts`) for future sustained templates. Magnitude `scaledEffect(perTick ?? RIFT_INFLUENCE_PER_TICK=2, mult)`, cost `scaledCost(RIFT_PERTICK_COST=0.4, mult)`, leak chance `min(1, RIFT_LEAK_CHANCE=0.08 × mult)` — all from `spherePowerMultiplier(actor primary-sphere score)` (THR-548), so the downside scales with the upside (individualization, not a flat tax). Relic-buyout of upkeep via the shipped `upkeepArtifactId` path (THR-509). **Content:** constants `RIFT_INFLUENCE_PER_TICK`/`RIFT_INFLUENCE_CAP`/`RIFT_PERTICK_COST`/`RIFT_LEAK_CHANCE`/`RIFT_LEAK_CORRUPTION`/`RIFT_LEAK_ENTROPY_PRESSURE`/`RIFT_LEAK_SIGNIFICANCE`/`RIFT_ESTABLISHED_SIGNIFICANCE` (`game-config.ts`) + `RIFT_LEAK_SEED_OFFSET` (`phaseControlEffects.ts`), all NFP #1; `sphere_influence_amplify` added to `KNOWN_AFTERMATH_EFFECT_KINDS`; systemic-wiring-guide updated (faction-effects code block + Part 5 table, 21→22 kinds). **UI:** N/A (existing surfaces) — rift establishment + leak emit `narrative` TickEvents → chronicle/event log; both new trace categories appear in the DebugPanel; sustained controls already surface in ThreadsPanel (THR-418). Player-facing card is the future Veil signature card (separate content issue). **Tracing:** `ascendant.signature.rift` (establishment) + `ascendant.signature.rift_leak` (per-tick leak) registered in `TRACE_CATEGORIES` + `sphere_influence_amplify` added to `EncounterAftermathEffectTrace.effectKind`; dual trace (specific + `encounter_aftermath_effect`) on the establishment path. **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** no-op + trace on missing/non-location target, location-without-hex, or no acting ascendant; resolver-boundary try/catch. 11 new tests (6 resolver: establishment + floor/ceil scaling + perTick override + 3 fail-soft no-ops; 5 phase: per-tick influence + cap + leak fire/no-fire/determinism). Verification: full suite **12080 green (794 files)**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean; `tsc -b` source files clean (test files follow the shipped warhost baseline pattern, THR-489 known-red). Browser-verify exempt: engine/types/content + docs, no UI pillar file. Plan `Docs/plans/2026-06-30-ascendant-reach-signatures.md` §3.5/§3.9/§3.10 (never committed — built from the detailed issue description + the THR-548 helper + the THR-550 sibling + the ControlEffect lifecycle). Blocked-by THR-548 (shipped). Parent epic THR-499.

✅ **THR-550** (2026-06-30, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **New aftermath effect `signature_warhost` — Iron's "Call to Arms" reach signature (engine effect).** Iron's headline reach signature as a real aftermath effect. **Engine:** `{ kind: 'signature_warhost'; factionId; baseStrength?; leaderAgentId?; when? }` added to `EncounterAftermathReactionEffect` (`src/types/unifiedAction.ts`) with an exhaustive resolver case in `encounterAftermath.ts`. Resolution marks the target faction `mobilized` (faction property: `mobilized`/`mobilizedTick`/`mobilizedStrength`) and raises a force on the **existing army node form** via new `armySpawning.raiseWarhostForce` — reuses the `actor`/`group` node + `armyState` bag + `commanded_by`/`member_of`(role:army)/`located_at` edges (**NOT a new node type** — load-bearing rule cited), differing from `spawnArmy` only in being divinely commanded (no faction-ambition coupling / no `pursues` edge, no eligibility/Gold gate, quintessence from the sphere-scaled warhost strength rather than Gold tier; carries `objective:null`, a supported idle state). Force strength = `scaledEffect(baseStrength ?? WARHOST_BASE_STRENGTH=30, spherePowerMultiplier(actor primary-sphere score))` (THR-548) — leader is `effect.leaderAgentId` (any individual) else the faction's strongest Iron member via `selectCommander`. **Fallback** (no valid leader/location): the faction property plus a belligerent shift (`WARHOST_FALLBACK_SENTIMENT_SHIFT=-0.15`, clamped at `FACTION_WAR_SENTIMENT_FLOOR`) on the faction's negative-sentiment rival `relates_to` edges. **Content:** constants `WARHOST_BASE_STRENGTH`/`WARHOST_FALLBACK_SENTIMENT_SHIFT` (game-config, NFP #1); systemic-wiring-guide updated (faction-effects code block + Part 5 effect-types table, 20→21 kinds). **UI:** N/A — engine effect; player-facing surface is the future Iron signature card. **Tracing:** `ascendant.signature.warhost` registered in `TRACE_CATEGORIES` + `signature_warhost` added to `EncounterAftermathEffectTrace.effectKind`; dual trace (specific + `encounter_aftermath_effect`) on every path. **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** no-op + warn trace on missing/dissolved/non-faction target; resolver-boundary try/catch. `baseStrength` made optional vs. the issue's `: number` so the constant is the load-bearing default; cost-via-`scaledCost` is card-level (out of scope for the resolver). 6 new tests (army path + strength scaling floor/ceil, authored override, sentiment fallback, dissolved + missing no-ops). Verification: full suite **12069 green (792 files)**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt: engine/types/data + wiring-guide doc, no UI pillar file. ✅ **THR-549** (2026-06-30, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Reach-signature individualization scaffold — the sphere layer every signature composes against (epic foundation).** The (reach × primary-sphere) layer that makes a reach signature read differently per Creation Sphere: same Reach, different Sphere → distinct name, prose, and a unique twist. **Engine/Content:** `src/data/reach-signature-content.ts` (the THR-548 sphere-power-scaling file) gains the individualization layer — `SphereTwistSpec` (the individualization bar: a `trigger` condition + a generic-effect `payload` delta, i.e. *named effect with numbers AND a unique trigger*, not just bigger numbers), `SignatureIndividualization { twist; nameFragment; proseKey }`, and `SignatureMatrix = Record<ReachDomain, Partial<Record<CreationSphereName, …>>>`. `resolveSignatureIndividualization(reach, primarySphere)` returns the bespoke authored cell when present, else `composeDefaultSignature` — which **composes against the shipped `sphere_flavored_effect` primitive** (THR-509 `SPHERE_EFFECT_TABLE`): the reach base passive plus the sphere's flavored passive when the table has one. Creation spheres absent from the primitive table (`time`, `entropy`) still yield a real payload from the reach base alone. The **correctness property under the two-domain lock** (every ascendant's reach × sphere pair is fixed for the run): every (ReachDomain × CreationSphereName) pair resolves to a non-null individualization — **never an empty card**. Matrix ships as an empty skeleton (all 8 reaches present, no bespoke cells); per-cell content + the signature effects land in the sibling issues (THR-550/551/552 + content issues). `REACH_SIGNATURE_DEFAULTS` (8 reaches) seeds name/prose/trigger stems echoing `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` + the authored signatures (Iron/Warhost, Veil/Rend-the-Gate, Stone/Great-Work). Pure + deterministic (no PRNG, no graph). **UI:** N/A — types + data + pure resolver, no runtime UI; consumers (signature cards) are the sibling issues. 8 new tests (default seed per reach, the 64-pair never-empty guarantee, payload floor, sphere-flavored layering, time/entropy non-empty, determinism, bespoke-over-default). Verification: full suite **12063 green (791 files)**, `tsc --noEmit` exit 0, `vite build` green. Browser-verify exempt: types + data + pure resolver, no UI pillar file. Plan doc `Docs/plans/2026-06-30-ascendant-reach-signatures.md` §3.3/§2 (pending flush at implementation time — built from the detailed issue description + the THR-509 primitive). ✅ **THR-542 slice 3 / THR-545** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **The Core — Star reach-axis re-scope (Beacon/Guiding ↔ Wrecker/Misleading), a careful canon migration.** With the Core's `core_hope` (Hopeful↔Bitter) now owning *inner outlook* (slice 1), Star's reach axis — which had been an inner disposition (Inspiring↔Discouraging) masquerading as a reach behavior — re-points to its real domain, **wayfinding/fate**. **Engine:** the Star entry in `src/types/axisRegistry.ts` moves from `virtue Beacon/Inspiring · vice Anchor/Discouraging` to **`virtue Beacon/Guiding · vice Wrecker/Misleading`**; because UI/prose read pole labels through `getPoleLabel`, the re-scope surfaces everywhere automatically with no UI edit. The independent oracle in `axisRegistry.test.ts` (the deliberate double-entry guard against a registry regression silently matching a source regression) was updated **in lockstep**. **Content:** the 18 Star `origin-vignettes.ts` entries were re-themed from morale/hope — which, after slice 1 shipped `core_hope`, had become a *duplicate* of the Core's Hopeful↔Bitter vignettes — to wayfinding (guiding others' journeys home ↔ leading them astray / the wrecker's false lantern), preserving the same 9-virtue/9-vice counts, magnitude distribution, and `origin.<reach>.<pole>.<slug>` id convention; the two `the-star-pilgrim.ts` comment references were updated (the encounter — *"Give a direction. Point to the sister shrine"* — already fits Beacon/Guiding, so its `moralAxis:'star'` tag stayed correct). **Canon:** the Star rows in `Docs/canon/cosmology.md` and `Docs/canon/encounters.md` (`Wanderer↔Anchor`→`Beacon↔Wrecker`) and the THR-524 canon table in `Docs/plans/2026-06-29-archetype-virtue-vice-layer.md` were reconciled, with a dated supersession note on the THR-524 Star bullet. **Careful migration, not find/replace:** the unrelated `heart`-tier capability word `Inspiring` (`traits.ts`), the `Beacon Market` location name, and the `RoadMesh`/`meeting-content` `Anchor`/`Anchorite` literals were deliberately left untouched; the comet encounter (`the-comet-at-the-turning.ts`) frames its premise on the *pre-THR-524* `Wanderer↔Anchor` vocabulary (a different, older drift, with no mechanical `moralAxis`/`pole` tags — purely thematic prose) and was **deferred as THR-547** rather than damaged by a hasty rewrite of a cosmic fate encounter. ✅ **THR-542 slice 2 / THR-544** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **The Core — origin-vignette library + emergent-trait defs (Content slice 2 of 4).** Authored the content half of the Core layer atop slice 1's shipped engine foundation. **Content:** `src/data/core-origin-vignettes.ts` — **60 pre-history vignettes** keyed `(continuumId, pole, magnitude)`, 6 per (continuum × pole) across all 5 Core continuums, magnitudes small-delta-skewed for a central-limit baseline, Threadbare voice ("show the soil, not the flower"), generic/reusable — the Core sibling of the reach `ORIGIN_VIGNETTES` (THR-539); `src/data/core-trait-content.ts` — **10 emergent-trait defs** (5 continuums × virtue/vice), new `subcategory:'core'`, built from `CORE_CONTINUA` so pole labels can't drift, each carrying **empty `domainContributions` and no `scoringModifiers`** — the load-bearing Core≠reach≠capability invariant: the Core seeds/colours/bends the reaches, never adds a parallel reach-scoring axis; Core traits are pure character descriptors. **Engine wiring:** new `seedCoreProfileWithVignettes` (`coreMechanics.ts`) draws the unchanged central-limit PRNG baseline first (so slice-1 determinism is preserved bit-for-bit) then lays `CORE_ORIGIN_VIGNETTE_DRAW_COUNT=3` authored signed pulls (virtue +, vice −) on top, returning the applied vignette ids; the `core_personality` phase seeds via it, records ids on `node.properties.coreOriginVignettes`, and now **grants/releases the matching Core trait node** on emergence/fade — born-extreme agents granted silently at the seeding tick (trait edges are graph mutations, not traces, so no tick-1 ring-buffer burst, preserving the single aggregate `seeded` trace which gains a `vignettesApplied` count). Added `'core'` to the `TraitCategory` union (fully additive — all consumers are `=== 'specific'` comparisons with graceful fallback, so Core traits are correctly excluded from reputation/personality scoring and mastery decay) + a CMS badge colour. **UI:** N/A v1 — the character-sheet Core section is THR-546. Canon-safe framing held throughout (`coreRegistry.ts` module doc): Core = *which way* you bend, never "Quintessence traits". 33 new tests (vignette shape, trait-def shape + invariants, `seedCoreProfileWithVignettes` determinism + baseline-preservation + pole-direction, phase grant/release/born-extreme-silent/aggregate-trace). Verification: full suite **12044 green (789 files)**, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` 0 net-new (3496 THR-489 baseline; none of the 11 changed files appear in the error set), 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt: content data + engine/types only, no UI pillar file. Plan doc `Docs/plans/2026-06-29-quintessence-core-layer.md`.

✅ **THR-542 slice 1** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **The Core — Quintessence foundation personality layer (Engine foundation, slice 1 of 4).** A second, more fundamental personality layer beneath the 8 reach moral axes: five plain virtue↔vice continuums of *who an agent fundamentally is* — `core_warmth` (Warm↔Cold), `core_hope` (Hopeful↔Bitter), `core_forgiveness` (Forgiving↔Vengeful), `core_humility` (Humble↔Proud), `core_integrity` (True↔False) — on the canonical 0–1 / 0.5-neutral scale, stored at `node.properties.coreProfile`, kept structurally separate from `axiologicalProfile` (reach) and `domainCapabilities` (capability): **Core ≠ reach ≠ capability**, enforced and unit-tested. New `src/types/coreRegistry.ts` (registry + `CoreProfile` type + scale constants + helpers, mirroring `axisRegistry.ts`); `src/engine/core/coreMechanics.ts` + `coreConstants.ts` implement the three spec mechanics as pure functions — **seed** (`seedCoreProfile`: central-limit draw clustered near neutral, deterministic), **colour** (`colourReachExpression`: tints how a reach act *reads* — courage on a True/Humble self, swagger on a False/Proud one — never touches competence), **bend** (`coreBendContributions`: under low normalized Quintessence the Core nudges coupled reach axes toward a pole; a nudge ADDED to drift, **never a cap** — the cold philanthropist stays possible). New `core_personality` tick phase (`src/engine/phases/corePersonality.ts`, registered post-economy) deterministically seeds every mortal agent from `hash(worldSeed, agentId)`, tracks emergence hysteresis on `coreEmergent`, and emits a single `core_personality` trace category (`details.kind`: seeded/emerge/fade/bend). **Load-bearing canon-safe framing preserved:** Core and Quintessence are co-resident but distinct — Quintessence = how bendable, Core = which way they bend; couple directionally not evaluatively; goodness never a Quintessence pole; never labelled "Quintessence traits". Seeding is PRNG today (Core vignette library is slice 2); colour/bend ship as mechanics + traces (consumption lands with their slices). 32 new tests (registry 8, mechanics 17, phase 7) + the phase-ordering equivalence baseline update; full suite **12016 green / 787 files**, 0 failed; `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (3496 = baseline − the single fixed test error); `vite build` green; 30-tick CLI smoke clean (390 agents; 374 Core-seeded by tick 3, sample `{warmth 0.68, hope 0.60, forgiveness 0.49, humility 0.50, integrity 0.52}` — near-neutral as designed). Engine/CLI only — browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file; UI is slice 4). Systemic-wiring-guide updated with the Core seam. Remaining slices decomposed per the handoff's split recommendation: **THR-544** (Content vignettes + emergent-trait defs), **THR-545** (Star re-scope Beacon/Wrecker — careful canon migration), **THR-546** (UI character-sheet Core section on the live `OverviewTab`). The full Core-layer plan doc (`Docs/plans/2026-06-29-quintessence-core-layer.md`) was unavailable at implementation time (pending THR-543 flush); built from the detailed issue description + the parent epic plan. Depends-on THR-525 (Done).

✅ **THR-522** (2026-06-30, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Pool-beat template aftermath + introduction-group binding (the project's last engine tail).** Two pool-side resolve-contract gaps from THR-520 closed. **(1) Template aftermath on resolution (engine):** `resolvePendingBeat` (`src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts`) gained an optional `runtime` + `templateProvider` in `opts`; when supplied it looks up a resolving beat's matched `UnifiedActionTemplate` and runs its `aftermathConfig.fallback.reactions` through the shared `applyEncounterAftermathReaction` resolver — so a beat can express the full typed aftermath vocabulary (`unlock_action`, `encounter_seed`, structural graph ops, faction/reputation) via its template, not only the descriptor `grantsActionIds`. A new `runBeatTemplateAftermath` helper synthesizes a minimal already-resolved `UnifiedAction` whose actor is the beat's **bound subject** (the introduced group) when present, else the ascendant, so actor-relative effects land correctly. Additive + fail-soft: a template with no `aftermathConfig` is the documented grant-only fallback (every shipping beat today); headless callers without a runtime skip aftermath; a thrown resolver never wedges the beat. **(2) Introduction-group binding (engine + content + UI):** the Director now binds the specific un-introduced culture/faction into `PendingBeat.boundNodeIds` at *offer* time (`bindBeatSubject`, picking the first group not in any resolved introduction's `boundNodeIds` — graph order, deterministic), recorded on the new additive `BeatRecord.boundNodeIds` so later draws never re-introduce it (eligibility's count-proxy left untouched — existing tests green). Introduction templates name the group via the new `{group}` enrichment placeholder (`proseEnrichment.ts`, `NarrativeContext.boundGroupName`; fail-soft neutral fallback); `GameView.beatProseOverride` injects the bound node's name. Inspectable: the DebugPanel Beats tab shows a "Bound subject" row and `__DEBUG.beatSchedule().pending` exposes `boundNodeIds` + resolved `boundNames`. Stale `TODO(THR-522)` comments retired. 9 new tests (6 engine binding/aftermath, 3 prose `{group}`); full suite **11984 green (784 files)**, `tsc --noEmit` clean + `tsc -b` 0 net-new in touched files, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke clean (390 agents, 50 events). Browser-verified @1920×1080: DebugPanel "Bound subject: Children of the Shadow-Kept Timberlands" + intro-beat modal prose `"…everything Kael Thornweaver knows, Children of the Shadow-Kept Timberlands lifts its first prayer toward you"` (0 console errors; `__DEBUG` pending.boundNodeIds=`["hist_culture_0"]`). Systemic-wiring-guide updated ({group} placeholder + beat template-aftermath capability).

✅ **THR-470** (2026-06-30, Encounter Format Migration) — **Seed-99 branching fire rate: root cause was a broken KPI metric, not awareness.** The deferral premise (seed 99 "stuck at 0.5–0.75/30t", hypothesised awareness-gating per THR-452/465) proved a measurement artifact. `GameplayKpiReport.branchingFire.firesPerChunk` counted resolved branching actions from `state.unifiedActions` — pruned after `RESOLVED_ACTION_RETENTION_TICKS = 20` (orchestrator) — but divided by the **full** tick count, so any run longer than the window undercounts ~9×. A throwaway funnel harness (real init→tick pipeline) *did* confirm awareness is `the_star_pilgrim`'s single dominant gate (998× of 1998 considerations vs cap 747×) and that the same low-reach pattern hits `pilgrims_offering`/`the_oracle_consulted`/`the_jury_of_the_ruined` — but raising the branching-quest awareness radius to its max lifted the windowed reading only 0.50→0.67, because the **true cumulative rate was already 3.33/30t** (seed 99), 4.67 (42), 4.17 (7) — all ≥3× the ≥1.0 target, simply hidden by the metric. **Fix (engine):** added a lifetime counter `SimulationRuntime.branchingFiresTotal`, incremented once per newly-resolved branching action at the orchestrator's resolved-action cleanup chokepoint (the same pass that stamps `completedAtTick`, before the prune); `buildBranchingFire` now reads this lifetime total via the optional `KpiRuntimeView.branchingFiresTotal`, falling back to the windowed snapshot when no runtime is supplied (pure-state unit tests). After the fix the `kpi` command reads seed 99 **3.33/30t ✓**, 42 **4.67 ✓**, 7 **4.17 ✓** — seed 99 unblocked, no regression. The handoff's recommended awareness-floor lever was implemented, measured, and **reverted as unnecessary** (verify-the-noun: seed 99 was never under-firing). 2 new KPI tests (lifetime-counter preferred; windowed fallback); full suite **11975 green (784 files)**, `tsc --noEmit` clean + `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (59 = 59 baseline in touched files), `vite build` green, seed-99/42 30-tick + 10-tick small-map engine smokes clean. Engine/CLI only — browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file; the rate is a debug/CLI KPI surface). Sibling `beatsPerChunk` carries the identical windowing flaw → tracked as THR-541.

✅ **THR-529** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Formative-mark primitive (rare permanent baseline shift).** Issue #5 of the breakdown — the "what marked them" layer. New author-gated aftermath effect `axiological_mark_apply { reach, signedMagnitude }` (`src/types/unifiedAction.ts` union member + handler in `src/engine/encounterAftermath.ts`) that applies a **permanent** shift to an agent's standing moral baseline on one reach's virtue↔vice axis — moving the baseline itself, where THR-528 choice drift only nudges a temporary layer that decays back. The handler resolves the agent (defaults to the encounter actor), maps reach → legacy ValuePair via `getAxisByReach`, clamps the authored `signedMagnitude` to ±`FORMATIVE_MARK_MAX_MAGNITUDE` (0.15, NFP #1) and the resulting baseline to [−1,+1], and mutates `node.properties.axiologicalProfile[valuePair]` in place (initializing a profile for a born-neutral agent), setting `mutationSummary.touchedWorld`. **Divergence from the handoff, flagged and faithful:** the plan/handoff assumed THR-526 had shipped an `axisContributions` trait field + a baseline-from-trait-sum + a permanent-trait minting path — but the shipped baseline is the existing `axiologicalProfile[valuePair]` (the `AxiologicalProfile` is the baseline store; `driftAccumulator.ts` documents it as "set at birth, moved only by permanent marks"), and `ORIGIN_VIGNETTES` has no birth-seeding consumer wired yet. Rather than build the non-existent trait machinery (a destructive re-architecture, out of scope for #5), the mark shifts the real baseline store directly — faithful to the design intent, additive (NFP #6), no new node type. Inspectability: a typed `AxiologicalMarkAppliedTrace` (`'axiological_mark_applied'` in `TraceCategory` + `TRACE_CATEGORIES` + `TraceEntry`) records reach / valuePair / clamped magnitude / previous + new baseline (NFP #2); a "becoming" `narrative` chronicle beat (`FORMATIVE_MARK_EVENT_SIGNIFICANCE` = 0.75) surfaces the moment. Fail-soft (NFP #4): no actor → skip trace; missing target node → skip trace; born-neutral → profile initialized. **Content (starter set, rare by design):** one mark on the most-committed aftermath reaction per branch of two registered defining-moment encounters — `the-executioners-commission` (Iron: carry-out → vice/Conqueror −0.15, refuse-and-warn → virtue/Protector +0.15) and `the-granaries-in-the-famine-year` (Gold: open granaries → virtue/Patron +0.15, corner the market → vice/Extractor −0.15) — spanning two axes and all four poles. `'axiological_mark_apply'` added to `KNOWN_AFTERMATH_EFFECT_KINDS` (`contentInvariants.ts`). UI: the chronicle beat is the v1 surface; the rich character-sheet "mark rows" are THR-532. 7 new tests (`encounterAftermath-axiological_mark_apply.test.ts`: virtue/vice shift, magnitude clamp, baseline clamp, born-neutral init, explicit target, missing-target fail-soft). Verification: full suite **11973 green** (784 files), `vite build` green (real type gate), 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file — the mark surfaces via the existing event feed). Systemic-wiring-guide + wiring-checklist updated.

✅ **THR-489** (2026-06-30, Repo Health) — **Test-suite stabilization: `npm test` green on main, reconciled & closed.** The months-long red baseline (impediments #22, #30–39, #54, #57) is cleared — the live full run on `main` @ `ab305729` is **783 files / 11966 tests green, 0 failed**, stable across **3 consecutive runs** (no order-dependence). Deliverable = the canonical triage/closure doc `Docs/plans/2026-06-30-test-suite-stabilization-triage.md` (the Cowork-authored version was lost to a stale-branch worktree, impediment #142 shape; reproduced on disk with the actual outcome + keep-it-green guidance).
