✅ **THR-530** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Autonomous in-encounter choice (the one genuinely new subsystem).** Issue #6 of the breakdown. Today only the player branches: non-hero agents resolve encounter steps mechanically and their authored aftermath reactions are **never applied** — the intelligence, artifacts, reputation, and seeds those reactions describe silently never happen. This slice closes that gap. **Engine:** new pure `src/engine/encounters/reactionChooser.ts` — `computeAxisLeans(graph, drift, agentId)` reads an agent's live moral position per reach (`axiologicalProfile` baseline + temporary drift, ±1 scale; null when no profile); `inferReactionLean(reaction, primaryReach)` infers each authored reaction's signed reach-pole from its effects (canonical `${reach}.positive/.negative` `reputation_tally` keys → ±1; actor-self `reputation_score` delta → the encounter's primary reach; off-axis keys and faction/other-agent–targeted effects carry no personal signal and are ignored); `chooseAlignedReaction(reactions, leans, primaryReach, weight)` scores each option `Σ reactionLean·agentLean·weight` and picks the deterministic argmax with a lowest-index tie-break, reporting `aligned:false` (→ caller uses `reactions[0]`) when no option scores strictly positive. New registered tick phase `autonomous_aftermath` (`src/engine/phases/phaseAutonomousAftermath.ts`, slot `post-resolution` — after `phaseUnifiedActionProgress` assembles `aftermathSummary`, before the decay/threshold phases) scans resolved actions of **non-player, non-threaded** agents (excludes thread/avatar holders via `getThreadedAgents`/`getAvatarsOf`), applies the chosen reaction through `applyEncounterAftermathReaction`, nudges drift toward the chosen pole (the THR-528 `applyDriftMagnitude` applicator — "their choices push their leanings"), and emits a `reaction_selected` trace with per-option alignment scores (new `TraceCategory`). Idempotency: a new additive `UnifiedAction.autonomousAftermathApplied` flag (non-hero agents get no notification to mark resolved, so the scan would otherwise re-fire every tick); `PERSONALITY_AUTONOMOUS_AFTERMATH_MAX_PER_TICK` (12) bounds per-tick work. The `reactions[0]` default in `resolveAftermathContextForAgent` is replaced with the profile-aligned chooser, so the CLI auto-aftermath and debug-bridge paths also pick in character. New named constants `PERSONALITY_REACTION_WEIGHT` (2.0 — the kill-criteria lever: → 0 collapses all scores below the floor and reverts to `reactions[0]`) and `PERSONALITY_REACTION_DRIFT_DELTA` (0.05) (NFP #1); deterministic, no PRNG (NFP #3); fail-soft to `reactions[0]` / no-op without a runtime (NFP #4). **Content:** N/A for v1 — consumes the authored `reputation_tally`/`reputation_score` signals already present; broad in-world variety scales with authored reaction poles (more is better, not gating). **UI:** N/A — autonomous behavior surfaces through the existing chronicle/event feed; the layered character sheet is THR-532. 29 new unit tests (`reactionChooser.test.ts` 15: lean inference, scoring, non-index-0 selection, neutral/no-signal/weight-0 fallback, tie-break, determinism; `phaseAutonomousAftermath.test.ts` 6: aligned application, drift, idempotency, profile-less fallback, hero exclusion, no-runtime no-op) + the `phaseRegistry.equivalence` baseline updated for the new `post-resolution` phase. Verification: full suite **11966 green** (783 files), `tsc -b` **0 net-new** (3494 = 3494 baseline, THR-489), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 50 events) clean; a 300-tick traced run fired the chooser **51×** with in-character variety — chosenIndex distribution `[[0,49],[1,2]]` (not all index-0), 8 personality-driven aligned picks. Browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file). **Scope note:** "apply drift" is honored via the reaction-choice nudge using the existing applicator; non-hero agents do not traverse `AuthoredChoiceCard` branches (that is the player path), so there is no branch-drift to apply here.

✅ **THR-528** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Choice → drift: authored poles + apply + decay-to-baseline.** Issue #4 of the breakdown — the "what their recent choices are making them" layer. **Content:** added an authored moral-axis declaration to `AuthoredChoiceCard` (`src/types/unifiedAction.ts`) — optional `moralAxis` (`ReachDomain`) + `pole` (`'virtue'|'vice'`) + `magnitude` (0.05–0.20) — so authors state which axis a choice tilts and how hard, retiring the inferred `toEncounterArchetypePole` heuristic (interventionType → pole) as the *primary* path; the heuristic stays only as the fallback for un-migrated cards. Migrated a handful of branching encounters as proof — `road-ambush` (iron), `the-merchants-favor` (gold, incl. a deliberate **divergence case**: a `withdrawn` "decline advocacy, extract value" choice authored as the Gold *vice*, which the heuristic would have read as virtue), `shadow-court-audience` (shadow), `the-star-pilgrim` (star) — 9 cards across 4 reaches, with a one-line `choice.pole` preference added to each file's local pole heuristic so the declaration takes effect. **Engine:** new shared resolvers in `src/data/encounter-contract-builder.ts` — `resolveEncounterArchetypePole` (authored pole wins over heuristic, used in the canonical `buildLiteEncounterContract`) and `resolveChoiceDrift` (maps an authored card → `{ axisReach, moralAxisPole: 'virtue'|'flaw', driftMagnitude }`, the contract a `PendingChoiceCommit` builder consumes; authored `moralAxis`/`pole`/`magnitude` supersede the cost-tier + interventionType heuristic). New **live-position accessor** `liveAxisPosition(baseline, driftDelta)` + `driftDeltaFor(drift, agentId, axisId)` (`src/engine/encounters/driftAccumulator.ts`): the live position is `clamp(baseline + drift)` on the legacy ±1 axis scale — and because drift is a *temporary delta* that decays toward zero, the live position **rests at the (mutable) baseline, never at neutral**. `phaseChoiceResolution` now reads the agent's baseline (`axiologicalProfile[axis.valuePair]` via the THR-536 registry) and emits the resulting `livePosition` on the `choice_resolved` trace (new optional field, NFP #2). The decay phase (`phaseDriftDecay`) switched to the plan-named constant `PERSONALITY_DRIFT_DECAY_PER_TICK` (with the old `DRIFT_DECAY_RATE_PER_TICK` kept as a `@deprecated` alias) and documents that pulling the delta toward zero is exactly what pulls the live position toward the baseline. New named constants `PERSONALITY_DRIFT_DELTA_SUBTLE/MODERATE/STRONG/DEFINING` (0.05–0.20) + `PERSONALITY_DRIFT_DELTA_DEFAULT` (`src/data/encounter-experience-constants.ts`, NFP #1). Fail-soft (NFP #4): missing profile/axis → no live position (drift still tracked); un-migrated card → heuristic. **UI:** none required — the live position is surfaced via the existing `DriftVisualiser` (full character-sheet personality rows are THR-532, a later wave). Three pillars: Engine (resolvers + accessor + decay) + Content (authored fields + 4 migrated encounters); UI N/A. 8 new resolver tests (`encounter-contract-choice-drift.test.ts`: authored-beats-heuristic incl. the divergence case, authored→drift-sign mapping, `moralAxis` re-routing, default magnitude, heuristic fallback) + 6 new accessor tests (`driftAccumulator.test.ts`: `liveAxisPosition` composition/clamp/rests-at-baseline-after-500-tick-decay, `driftDeltaFor`). Verification: full suite **11945 green** (781 files), `tsc --noEmit` clean, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI engine smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 49 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt — no UI pillar file touched (the live position surfaces through the existing debug DriftVisualiser; the sheet rows are THR-532). **Scope note:** the live `PendingChoiceCommit` construction sites (player-commit UI, autonomous in-encounter chooser) are other items in the breakdown (#6/#8) — this slice ships the authoring + application + decay machinery and its resolvers, verified by unit tests + the contract test, ahead of those construction sites. The 24 per-encounter local copies of `buildLiteEncounterContract` remain legacy duplication (only the migrated handful carry the authored-pole preference); consolidating them onto the shared builder is a separate follow-up.

✅ **THR-527** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Threshold → emergent personality traits (+ hysteresis).** Issue #3 of the breakdown — the layer-4 "who they currently are" slice, unblocked by the THR-525 axis registry + THR-526 birth-seeding. New registered tick phase `personality_trait_emerge` (slot `post-economy`, `src/engine/phases/personalityTraitEmerge.ts`, with `processPersonalityTraitEmergence(state)` extracted for testing): walks every **individual, non-deceased, non-ascendant** actor × the 8 `CANONICAL_AXES`, reads the standing per-axis position from `node.properties.axiologicalProfile[axis.valuePair]` (the legacy ±1 storage that birth-seeding populates and THR-531 selection consumes — the plan's §Scalar-unification directive that "the threshold/announce layer reads the profile"), normalizes ±1 → the canonical 0–1 axis scale via `0.5 + 0.5·v`, and grants the **virtue** trait at position ≥`PERSONALITY_TRAIT_VIRTUE_THRESHOLD` (0.8) / the **vice** trait at ≤`PERSONALITY_TRAIT_VICE_THRESHOLD` (0.2), releasing a held trait only once the position falls back inside the `PERSONALITY_TRAIT_RELEASE_BAND` (0.15) dead-band (virtue release <0.65, vice release >0.35) so traits don't flicker around the boundary. Expressing the thresholds on the 0–1 canonical scale (not the raw ±1 profile) is deliberate — when THR-537/538 migrate the profile storage to 0–1, only the one read line changes, the constants stand. Crystallization fires a "becoming" `tickEvent` (`'personality_trait_emerged'` added to the `TickEvent` `type` union): `"<name> has become <Word>."`. **Content:** 16 personality trait definitions (`src/data/personality-trait-content.ts`) — 8 axes × virtue/vice — generated from `CANONICAL_AXES` so pole labels (Brave/Power-Hungry … Inspiring/Discouraging) and reach bindings can never drift from the THR-524 vocabulary; `subcategory:'personality'` (new `TraitCategory` member, kept distinct from `reputation` = world-perception); each carries a per-reach `scoringModifiers` payload (new optional field on `TraitDefinitionProperties`) and — load-bearing invariant — **empty `domainContributions`**: personality steers which encounters an agent reaches for, never their competence at them (capability lives in a separate field + scoring term). The behavior bias is consumed by generalizing `computeReputationScoringBonus` (`encounterScoring.ts`) to read `personality` traits' top-level `scoringModifiers` alongside `reputation` traits' nested `reputationEffects.scoringModifiers`, scaled by `REPUTATION_SCORING_WEIGHT`. Named constants incl. `PERSONALITY_TRAIT_SCORING_MODIFIER` (0.15) (NFP #1); a typed `PersonalityTraitEmergedTrace` registered in `TraceCategory` + `TRACE_CATEGORIES` + the `TraceEntry` union, emitted on both grant and release (NFP #2); fail-soft per the plan table — missing profile → skip the agent, missing trait-def node → skip the grant + warning trace, position still tracked (NFP #4); deterministic threshold checks, no PRNG (NFP #3). Three pillars: Engine (the phase) + Content (the 16 defs); UI = the "becoming" event surfaces via the existing tick-event feed — the full layered character-sheet personality section is **THR-532** (later wave), explicitly out of this slice. 10 new phase tests (`personalityTraitEmerge.test.ts`: grant/release at thresholds, hysteresis hold-then-release, idempotency/no-duplicate-edges, ascendant exclusion, missing-profile fail-soft, trace emission, opposite-pole release) + the `phaseRegistry.equivalence` baseline updated for the new post-economy phase (and its per-tick capture made eviction-robust by clearing the trace buffer per tick, since the new phase's crystallization burst can otherwise overflow the 2000-entry ring within the 10-tick window). Verification: full suite **11930 green** (780 files), `tsc --noEmit` clean + `tsc -b` **0 net-new** errors (3492 = 3492 baseline, THR-489), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI engine smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents, 49 events) clean with no thrown exceptions. Engine-smoke evidence: a 40-tick traced run had **73 individual agents** crystallize personality traits (e.g. Tam → Disloyal) and **0 non-individual** actors affected (factions/armies excluded by the `actorType:'individual'` filter). Browser-verify exempt: no UI pillar file touched — the becoming beat reaches the player through the existing event feed; character-sheet rows are THR-532.

✅ **THR-531** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Personality → encounter selection weighting (strong & legible).** Issue #7 of the personality breakdown — the most isolated slice; strengthens only the existing `axiologicalProfile → computeDesireScore → desireMultiplier` selection path (`src/engine/encounterScoring.ts`) and adds a labeled signal + trace, colliding with nothing else in flight. New tunable `PERSONALITY_SELECTION_WEIGHT = 2.0` (`src/data/agent-behavior-constants.ts`, NFP #1): the raw signed axiological alignment (`computeDesireScore` over the encounter's motivation pairs) is multiplied by the weight into a new `personalityBias` term that feeds the desire multiplier in place of the unweighted `axiologicalScore`. At weight 1.0 this exactly reproduces the pre-THR-531 baseline; the high default makes positively-aligned agents clearly gravitate toward matching encounters (negatively-aligned candidates still floor at `MINIMUM_DESIRE`, so the amplification is an intentional asymmetry — it strengthens attraction, not aversion). Per the plan's kill-criteria this is the single reversible lever: if selection ever reads as scripted (variety collapse), dial the weight down before any redesign. Inspectability (NFP #2): `personalityBias` (+ the raw `axiologicalScore`) added to `ScoredCandidate` and to `ScoringTrace.topCandidates` — observable in the debug panel's DecisionBreakdown trace. Strictly additive (NFP #6) — the encounterScoring `ScoredCandidate` has a single constructor in `scoreAndSelect`; the same-named types in `curator.ts`/`clueLifecycle.ts` are separate local interfaces, untouched. Three pillars: Engine = entirety; Content N/A; UI N/A (the bias is surfaced via the existing `ScoringTrace`, no new surface). 2 new unit tests (`encounterScoring.test.ts`) assert `personalityBias === axiologicalScore × PERSONALITY_SELECTION_WEIGHT` and that the value is surfaced on the trace. Verification: full suite **11920 green** (779 files), `tsc --noEmit` clean, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 390 agents) clean. Engine-smoke evidence of personality-shaped selection: a 40-tick traced run produced 222 scored candidates with nonzero `personalityBias` (aligned axio 0.902 → bias 1.804 → desire 2.422, selected; misaligned axio −0.953 → bias −1.905 → desire floored 0.011). Browser-verify exempt (no UI pillar file touched). Unblocks nothing downstream — terminal leaf of the selection-weighting concern.

✅ **THR-539** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Origin-vignette content library (content half of THR-526).** Authored `src/data/origin-vignettes.ts`, an engine-free pool of 144 pre-history vignettes that seed an agent's *baseline* personality at birth. Each entry is keyed by `(reach, pole, magnitude)` — all 8 Reaches × both virtue/vice poles × 9 each — with magnitudes skewed to the small deltas (per bucket 4×0.05 / 3×0.10 / 1×0.15 / 1×0.20; 112 entries ≤0.10 vs 32 >0.10) so that drawing several at birth produces a roughly normal baseline (central-limit) rather than extremes. Vignettes are Threadbare-voice one-line *circumstances* ("show the soil, not the flower"), deliberately generic/reusable — no proper nouns, no setting-specific locations, no gendered assumptions — using the THR-524-frozen vocabulary (Iron Brave/Protector↔Power-Hungry/Conqueror … Star Inspiring/Beacon↔Discouraging/Anchor). Load-bearing decision: keyed on `(reach, pole)` ONLY — no `axisId`, `AxiologicalProfile`, or registry import — which is exactly what makes the work parallel-safe with the registry trie (THR-536/537/538); the reach→`axisId` lookup and signed-delta derivation (virtue → +magnitude toward 1.0, vice → −magnitude toward 0.0) are deferred to THR-526's engine half, which consumes this module at birth-seeding time. Typed `OriginVignette` contract (`{ id, text, reach, pole, magnitude }`) plus `OriginVignettePole` / `OriginVignetteMagnitude` unions; a 9-assertion coverage shape-test (`src/data/__tests__/origin-vignettes.test.ts`) asserts ≥130 total, all 8 reaches + both poles present, every (reach,pole) bucket ≥8, only the four allowed magnitudes appear, the small-delta skew holds, ids are unique and follow `origin.<reach>.<pole>.<slug>`, and text is non-empty/trimmed. Three pillars: Content (the entirety of this issue); Engine N/A (consumer is THR-526); UI N/A (character-sheet rendering is THR-532). Verification: `tsc --noEmit` clean, scoped vitest 9 green, vite build green. Engine-smoke exempt (isolated content data module — no tick-loop / orchestrator / agent-decision file); browser-verify exempt (no UI surface rendered in this slice).

✅ **THR-536** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Canonical axis registry module (THR-525 split 525a — additive, zero migration).** Shipped the load-bearing contract that all 8 downstream personality issues (THR-526–533) import, so 525b/525c and the origin-vignette workstream have a stable type to build against. New `src/types/axisRegistry.ts` defines `CanonicalAxis` ({ `reachDomain`, `axisId`, `valuePair`, `virtue:{role,word}`, `vice:{role,word}` }) and `CANONICAL_AXES` — one entry per Reach in `REACH_DOMAINS` order — using the **THR-524-approved** vocabulary exactly (Iron Protector/Brave ↔ Conqueror/Power-Hungry; Gold Patron/Generous ↔ Extractor/Greedy; Shadow Broker/Fair ↔ Manipulator/Scheming; Veil Weaver/Patient ↔ Unraveller/Impatient; Heart Sworn/Loyal ↔ Renegade/Disloyal; Eye Seer/Perceptive ↔ Inquisitor/Judgemental; Stone Keeper/Dependable ↔ Destroyer/Reckless; Star Beacon/Inspiring ↔ Anchor/Discouraging). The two terms that relocate in the new vocabulary are encoded at their **new** reaches — `Seer` Veil→Eye, `Manipulator` Veil→Shadow. The `axisId` follows the stable, derivable convention `${reachDomain}_axis` (distinct from the bare reach value so an axis is never confused with a reach), exported as `CANONICAL_AXIS_IDS`. The `valuePair` legacy-bridge field is sourced directly from the real `REACH_VALUE_PAIR` keys in `agent.ts` (`mercy_ruthlessness`…`sacrifice_survival`) — never invented — so 525b can migrate each reference per-site without a blind find/replace. The canonical **0–1 scale** (0.5 neutral / virtue pole 1.0 / vice pole 0.0) is documented inline and exported as `AXIS_NEUTRAL`/`AXIS_VIRTUE`/`AXIS_VICE`. Helper getters `getAxisByReach` (total over `ReachDomain`), `getAxisById` (undefined for unknown), and `getPoleLabel(axisId, side)` are backed by load-time `Record`/`Map` indexes. **Strictly additive** — the OLD `ARCHETYPE_NAMES` table in `agent.ts` is untouched; no consumer is re-pointed, no scalar retired, no name reconciled (that work is 525b / THR-537). Three pillars: Engine (the registry constants + helpers); Content N/A (no content authored — this is the vocabulary substrate the origin-vignette workstream will consume); UI N/A (no surface rendered). 18 new tests (`src/types/__tests__/axisRegistry.test.ts`): all 8 axes × both poles vs an independently-restated oracle table, the two-relocation check, the `valuePair === REACH_VALUE_PAIR[reach]` bridge check, axis-id uniqueness/convention, and helper round-trips + undefined paths. Verification: types suite **254 green** (28 files), `vite build` green, `tsc -b` **0 net-new** errors (new files clean; only the pre-existing THR-489 strict-null baseline in `__tests__/*.test.ts`). Engine-smoke exempt (additive constants module — no tick-loop / orchestrator / agent-decision file touched); browser-verify exempt (no UI surface). Unblocks THR-537 (525b migration) and THR-538 (525c).

✅ **THR-513** (2026-06-28, Divine Cadence) — **anoint expression card + chosen-faction power consumer (completes the §4.4 toolkit).** Closes the THR-509 dead-content gap: nothing read `node.properties.chosen`, so the chosen status was inert. **Engine:** new `applyAnointFaction(graph, ascendantId, factionId, tick)` (`ascendantExpression.ts`) reads the ascendant's primary reach (`domainAffinities`, THR-503) and stamps a `chosen` status via the shipped THR-509 `applyChosenStatusGrant` (records the actor×domain power from `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE`), dispatched from a new `'anoint_faction'` GraphOp intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` (mirrors `bestow_power`; deterministic). **Consumer:** new per-tick `phaseChosenFactionPowers` (`chosenFactionPowers.ts`), registered orchestrator phase 6.56 (right after faction reputation decay) — reads `faction.chosen.power` and grants the faction's members a power-keyed reputation gain via `applyFactionReputationGain`. Per-power magnitude via `CHOSEN_POWER_EFFECT_TABLE`, default `CHOSEN_FACTION_REPUTATION_PER_TICK`; extended `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` from 4 → all 8 reaches. **Content:** `action.anoint` (`targetCategories:['faction']`, cost `ANOINT_COST`=6). New `'chosen_power'` reputation cause; registered `chosen_faction_power` + `ascendant_primitive` trace categories. 16 new tests; full suite 11768 green, vite build green, 30-tick CLI smoke (398 agents) clean. Browser-verify exempt (engine/content-only; card renders via the existing AscendantHand/ActionDrawer surface — card→drawer wiring is THR-515).

✅ **THR-533** (2026-06-30, Agent Personality & Moral Drift) — **Prose readability pass on the branching encounters + flesh fix.** House-style calibration folding in the creative director's "in-game prose runs too literary" note. Retired the recurring **"the particular X of Y" / "a particular X"** tic across all 24 tic-bearing branching-encounter files in `src/data/encounters/` (~68 instances → 0) plus one stray in `tavern-encounter-content.ts`. The edit was a minimal de-tic — delete the filler word, keep the existing article ("the particular weight of conviction" → "the weight of conviction") — with only two light recasts where bare deletion broke grammar ("a particular kind of failure" → "its own kind of failure"; "had a particular flatness, a particular absence of shadow" → "a flat light, an absence of shadow"). Three legitimate non-tic idioms were deliberately preserved: "nothing particular against", "occupies a particular tier of institutional trust" (the *specific-tier* sense), and "to no one in particular". Text bodies only — **zero structural change**: 65 insertions / 65 deletions, pure 1:1 prose-string line swaps, every `+` concatenation / `\'` escape / `\n\n` sequence intact (the cited 9/10 exemplar `flawed-steel.ts` de-ticked with extra care; structure untouched). Also fixed the stale `reach: 'flesh'` value in `tavern-encounter-content.ts` (4× — Tavern Brawl step 2 + the Drinking Contest template and both steps) → `'iron'`. `'flesh'` was the 9th Reach removed in TB-075 Phase 1 and is not a member of `ReachDomain`; the issue's "→Quintessence" referred to TB-075's *thematic* migration of vitality themes to the Quintessence meta-property, but `reach` is typed `ReachDomain` and `'quintessence'` would be a net-new type error, so the type-safe fix follows the cosmology canon's documented Flesh-content migration rule ("athletics → Iron"), which also matches the Tavern Brawl's own template + step-1 reach. Three pillars: Content (the prose + reach value); Engine N/A (no engine/tick file — `reach` is a content-data literal); UI N/A (prose renders through the existing encounter surface). Verification: full suite **11891 green** (777 files), encounter-structure tests 264 green, `tsc -b` **0 net-new** errors (only the pre-existing THR-489 strict-null baseline in `__tests__/*.test.ts`, none in the edited content files, none on `reach: 'iron'`), vite build green. Content-only ⇒ engine-smoke + browser-verify N/A. Out-of-scope sibling noted for separate tracking: `reward-attachment-catalog.ts:1696` carries the same stale `reach: 'flesh'`. Executed via 4 parallel de-tic subagents over disjoint file partitions; reviewed + verified centrally.

✅ **THR-534** (2026-06-29, Agent Coordination Protocol) — **Harden `flush-plan-docs` against false auto-close of implementation issues.** The authoritative `flush-plan-docs` SKILL.md Step 2f.4 was already scrubbed of closing keywords by THR-510 (2026-06-28), yet PR #428 (2026-06-29) re-closed the unimplemented foundational issue THR-525 — Todo→Done with no In Dev — using the OLD `Closes <linear-url>` template verbatim. Root cause split three ways: (a) the old template survived as a copy-paste hazard in the historical plan doc `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-flush-plan-docs-branch-fallback-fix.md`; (b) GitHub's **native** Linear integration closes on the `Closes <url>` form regardless of the custom `linear-autoclose.yml` guard (whose `isDocsFlushContext` only governs the `Fixes THR-NNN` merge=Done action), so PR-body scrubbing is the *only* effective defense; (c) the flush most likely ran from a checkout predating the SKILL.md fix. Fix: reinforced the SKILL.md prohibition to explain the native-integration vector + cite the #428→THR-525 incident, bumped `last_validated_against`→2026-06-29, mirrored to `.agents/`; neutralized the stale plan-doc template with a SUPERSEDED banner; logged impediment #162. The pre-merge CI guard (defense-in-depth) is tracked separately as THR-535. Docs/skill-only change (zero `src/` files) — engine-smoke + browser-verify N/A; `vite build` green, `check:skill-sync` green.

All `story_beat`/rarity-4 via the shared `withEncounterContract` builder, two god-action branches each (active intervention + the always-valid "let them handle it" restraint pole), full dual aftermath suites with cool failure at every branch, `// TODO(THR-498)` repeatability markers; registered in `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` + `LOCATION_BRANCHING_ENCOUNTER_TEMPLATES`. ✅ **THR-518** (2026-06-29, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Consecrate relic variant: mint a permanent relic + dynamic `upkeepArtifactId` injection.** Closed the THR-511 deferral. THR-511 shipped consecrate as a pure-data sustained-control card and `spawnControlEffect` already carried a *static* `upkeepArtifactId` through to the effect — but the relic variant needs the id to be *dynamic* (a freshly-minted node that doesn't exist until resolution). Added `ControlSpec.mintUpkeepRelic?: MintUpkeepRelicSpec` (`relicName` + optional `tags`); `spawnControlEffect` now, when that field is set and a graph is available, mints a permanent relic artifact (`lossCondition:'permanent'`), binds it to the establishing ascendant via a `possesses` edge, and sets the spawned effect's `upkeepArtifactId` to the **freshly-minted node id** (a minted id always overrides a static one). The existing `relic_upkeep_substitute` consumer (`getUpkeepStatus` / `phaseControlEffects`, THR-509) then waives `perTickCost` while the relic lives and lapses the effect (`upkeep_relic_destroyed`) on its destruction — the rival contestation vector — with zero new consumer wiring. **No new GraphOp, no resolution intercept** — the relic variant stays pure data: a second card `action.consecrate-relic` ("Enshrine Relic", `essenceCost: CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT = 13`, empty `perTickCost`, same faith-spread aura), granted alongside `action.consecrate` by `beat.pool.invest.the_hallowed_place` and bucketed `unlockable-generic`. Mint is deterministic (id + content from owner/target/tick, no PRNG), fail-soft (no graph → no mint, static id stands), and emits a `control_effect_relic_minted` trace. Three pillars: Engine (`MintUpkeepRelicSpec` type + the mint path in `spawnControlEffect`); Content (the new card + unlock-catalog/beat-grant wiring + `CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT`); UI N/A (the card renders through the existing data-driven ActionCard path — no UI-pillar file touched). 6 new tests (`controlEffectSpawn.test.ts`: mint+inject, deterministic id, minted overrides static, no-graph fail-soft, end-to-end waive-while-alive + lapse-on-destroy). Verification: full suite green, `vite build` green, `tsc -b` 0 net-new (3484 = 3484 baseline), 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium, 398 agents, 42 events) clean. Browser-verify exempt (types/engine/data only — existing ActionCard render path). PR pending.

✅ **THR-516** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **Director pool draw: eligibility predicates + identity (reach/sphere) bias.** Closed the THR-505 deferral: the shipped `drawFromPool(pool, rng)` took no world state, so the Director couldn't drop a beat with nothing to say or lean toward the god's identity. Pool beats tagged (introduction → unintroduced_group, investment → unthreaded_target). ✅ **THR-504** (2026-06-28, Onboarding & First-Run Experience) — **Scripted onboarding spine beats 0–4.** Authored the full five-beat opening arc on the Ascendant Beat substrate. **Content:** expanded `ASCENDANT_SPINE` (`src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts`) from 3 turn-gated placeholders to the complete arc with real, shipping-template grants — Beat 0 *The First* (`bind_thread_agent` + `observe_agent`), Beat 1 *The Seat* (`bind_thread_location`), Beat 2 *A Thing Left Behind* (`action.imbue`), Beat 3 *The First Word* (`divine.persuade`), Beat 4 *A Path Opens* — a `kind:'selection'` that forks the opening into one of three god-paths (`divine.dream` dreamer / `divine.omen` prophet / `divine.inspire` patron). Authored per-beat **Threadbare-voice presentation** in a new `SPINE_BEAT_PRESENTATION` map (eyebrow / title / long-form read-and-TTS prose / cta — second-person, player-as-god, indirect intervention; the scripted spine is deterministic so it carries no `enrichProse` placeholders). Extended `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` with the four `divine.*` verbs (`unlockable-generic`). New `isSpineBeatId(beatId)` helper. **UI:** `AscendantBeatModal` now prefers the authored spine copy over the generic kind-derived placeholder (falls back for pool beats); `GameView`'s auto-open + non-dismissable logic re-keyed from `kind==='spine'` to the `beat.spine.` id prefix so the closing **selection** Beat 4 also auto-opens modally and can't be dismissed (onboarding not missable). **Scope decision:** the shipped resolve path (`resolvePendingBeat`, THR-517) is deliberately **catalogue-driven** — a beat *grants action cards*; the threaded actor / throne / artifact the arc narrates are produced when the player *fires* those granted cards (The First's actor comes from the existing MeetingEncounter). The plan §4.1 richer vision — beat resolution itself running `add_node`/`setHomeSeat` aftermath to auto-seed those nodes — is an engine change to the resolve contract with judgment calls the plan doesn't pin down; deferred to THR-520 (created this session, labeled Deferral/Engine). **NFP:** all cadence/grant data are named (#1); the existing five beat traces cover the arc (#2); deterministic turn-gated spine (#3); fail-soft via the unchanged resolve path (#4); narrative-first authored prose (#5); additive — the locked THR-500/505/517 test contract (`ASCENDANT_SPINE[0]`/`[2]` by index, `beat.spine.opening` grants) is preserved unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Content (the 5-beat spine + prose + buckets); UI (modal authored-copy preference + GameView spine-prefix auto-open); Engine N/A (no engine file touched — uses the shipped Director/resolve). 6 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantSpine.test.ts`): arc shape + kinds, grants resolve to real bucketed templates, every spine beat has authored presentation, `isSpineBeatId`, Beat 4 selection (no-choice no-op / valid-choice grants exactly one), and a full Director walk 0→4 proving the player holds the opening cards by ~turn 8 with exactly one chosen path. Verification: **full suite 11774 green** (768 files, zero failures), `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw. Browser-verified via this-worktree vite (5175, `?view=game&seeded&size=medium`; fresh code probed by firing the new `beat.spine.the_first_word` / `beat.spine.a_path_opens` ids that exist only in this branch): all five beats render their authored Threadbare prose modally; Beat 4 renders the choose-1-of-3 picker (Dream/Omen/Inspire with real template descriptions); selecting a path (via UI click and via `__DEBUG.resolveBeat('divine.omen')`) grants **exactly** the chosen path; OBSERVE reveals in the AscendantBar drawer after Beat 0 (unlock→drawer wiring proven); 0 console errors across the full walk. Viewport note: the host display clamps the 1920×1080 window resize to 1528×1226 via OS scaling — nothing renders below the fold; the modal fits. Unblocks the remaining Divine Cadence content (THR-514 pool prose, THR-515 card→beat wiring, THR-516 pool draw bias). PR pending.

✅ **THR-503** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Reach gate in `targetActions` + persisted ascendant reach. Both ascendant-creation paths (`createAscendant`, `createAscendantFromIdentity`) now persist `domainAffinities` on the ascendant node (`AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`); new reader `getAscendantDomainAffinities(graph, ascendantId)`. New optional `UnifiedActionTemplate.requiresReach?: ReachDomain` + tunable `REACH_GATE_MIN_AFFINITY` (0.2, `influence-content.ts`). New filter-9 reach gate in `getTargetActionSlots()`: a reach-gated card is **hidden** (like the unlock gate, never aspiration) unless the ascendant's affinity in that reach ≥ floor — a **permanent two-domain membership filter** per the director's settled premise (fixed primary+secondary reach for the whole run). Fail-soft: omitted affinities → fail-open; unknown reach → ungated + warn. Both drawer hooks (`useTargetActions`, `useAgentInteraction`) read affinities from the node. Trace gains optional `filteredByReach`. 8 new tests (6 gate + 2 persistence); full suite 11685 green, vite build green, engine smoke (seed 42 medium, tick 30, 398 agents) clean, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3483 baseline). ✅ **THR-513** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **anoint** expression card + **chosen-faction power consumer** (completes the four-card §4.4 expression toolkit). Of the §4.4 verbs, anoint is the per-graph-type verb for **factions**, and the issue called out the genuine engine gap: THR-509 shipped `applyChosenStatusGrant` + `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` as an explicit *scaffold* ("the full mechanical payload is the consuming card's concern"), and THR-508 confirmed **nothing read** `node.properties.chosen` — so setting the chosen status alone was dead content. This issue (a) authors the verb and (b) adds the consumer that makes the chosen power mechanically live. **(a) The verb:** new `applyAnointFaction(graph, ascendantId, factionId, tick)` in `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts` validates the target is a faction node (`actor` with `actorType:'faction'`), reads the ascendant's **primary reach** (`getAscendantPrimaryReach` over persisted `domainAffinities`, THR-503 — two-domain lock), and stamps the chosen status by delegating to the shipped THR-509 `applyChosenStatusGrant` (which records the (actor × domain) power from `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE`). Dispatched from a new `'anoint_faction'` `GraphOpType` (`src/types/graphOp.ts`) intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` (sibling to `bestow_power`/`imbue_item`; deterministic — no PRNG). Fail-soft: missing faction / non-faction target / no reach each emit an `ascendant_expression` trace and no-op, never throw. **(b) The consumer:** new per-tick `phaseChosenFactionPowers` (`src/engine/chosenFactionPowers.ts`), registered as orchestrator **phase 6.56** immediately after `phaseFactionReputationDecay` (6.55) so a chosen faction's members **net upward** — it walks `getFactionNodes`, and for each non-dissolved faction carrying a `chosen.power` grants every `member_of` member a **power-keyed reputation gain** through the existing `applyFactionReputationGain` (reputation is the faction's universal lever — rank, access, bonuses, and expulsion all flow from it). v1 channels all four powers through member reputation (the issue's "member reputation/leadership bonus" example); per-power magnitude via `CHOSEN_POWER_EFFECT_TABLE` (gold *Favoured Charter* strongest at 0.005, veil/shadow subtlest at 0.002), default `CHOSEN_FACTION_REPUTATION_PER_TICK` (0.003) for powers with no table entry. **Completed `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` from 4 → all 8 reaches** (added faction powers for veil *Whispered Sanction* / eye *Illumined Charter* / stone *Enduring Foundation* / star *Fated Banner*), finishing the THR-509 partial scaffold. **Content:** authored `action.anoint` (`targetCategories:['faction']`, `essenceCost: ANOINT_COST`=6 — one tier above bestow, a standing grant lifts a whole faction every tick; Threadbare prose; unlockable-generic). New `'chosen_power'` value on the `FactionReputationTrace['cause']` union; registered `chosen_faction_power` trace category (one summary trace per chosen faction per tick) and backfilled the THR-509 `ascendant_primitive` category that anoint's chosen-status evidence depends on. **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); the per-tick consumer emits a structured summary trace + reuses the registered reputation traces (#2); arithmetic-only, no PRNG (#3); fail-soft no-op paths for resolver and consumer (#4); all additive — `applyChosenStatusGrant`, `applyFactionReputationGain`, and the existing intercepts are unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyAnointFaction` + the `phaseChosenFactionPowers` consumer); Content (the `action.anoint` template + completed power table); UI (renders through the existing generic AscendantHand/ActionDrawer once unlocked — no new component; card→beat-grant wiring is THR-515). 16 new tests: 8 resolver (`ascendantExpression.test.ts` — power selection by primary reach, all-8-reaches map, re-anoint latest-patron-wins, three fail-soft) + 8 consumer (`chosenFactionPowers.test.ts` — gain applied, per-power magnitude, multi-tick accumulation, clamp at 1.0, default fallback, un-anointed no-op, dissolved skip, no-members no-throw); repointed 1 stale THR-509 test (it asserted `star` → null power; now that all reaches map, the null-power fail-soft path is exercised via a non-actor node type). Verification: full suite **11768 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw; `action.anoint` passes the `unified-action-templates.test.ts` structural validation in the green run. Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components`/`src/views`/HexMapV2 surface touched — the card surfaces through the existing generic drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants. Systemic-wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-512** (2026-06-28, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **bestow power** expression card + agent-boon subsystem. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs (imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint), bestow is the per-graph-type verb for **agents**, and the largest of the four because the plan flagged it as needing a genuinely-new consumer: "no agent-castable-ability store exists." The issue posed two open design questions, both resolved here. **(1) Agent-ability store — option (a) over option (b):** rather than build a new agent-side ability store + casting hook (option b, bigger), bestow **mints a "Divine Gift" `artifact` node and binds it to the agent with a `possesses` edge** (option a — "cheapest, keeps it wired"), reusing the imbue/effect-walker path end-to-end. The gift carries two `AttachmentEffect`s read by walkers that already ship, so the boon genuinely applies (not dead content) with zero new consumer subsystem: a `passive` reach bonus in the ascendant's **primary reach** (new `getAscendantPrimaryReach` reads the highest-affinity entry of `AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`, persisted by THR-503) summed by `collectAttachmentEffects`/`effectResolver` into the holder's reach modifiers; and a per-tick `resource_manipulate` quintessence restore applied each tick by `tickEffects`/`tickResourceManipulate` (`effectTick.ts`). **(2) Quintessence-regen semantic** (flagged "resolve in the issue" in plan §4.4): quintessence is a 0–1 health-scale value, not an accumulator, so the boon is a **regen-rate boost** via the per-tick `resource_manipulate` effect, **clamped to the holder's `quintessenceMax`** by `tickResourceManipulate` — never overfills. **Awareness gate:** thread `awareness` lives on the **thread edge** (`'unaware'|'intuition'|'faith'|'communion'`), not a node property, so it can't be a `requiredNodeProperties` filter; gated fail-soft **at resolution** — read the `thread` edge (`getOutgoingEdges(ascendantId,'thread').find(e=>e.target===agentId)`) and no-op when `awareness < BESTOW_MIN_AWARENESS` (`'faith'`). **Engine:** new `applyBestowPower(graph, ascendantId, agentId, tick)` + `getAscendantPrimaryReach` in `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts`; new `'bestow_power'` `GraphOpType` (`src/types/graphOp.ts`) intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` (sibling to `imbue_item`; deterministic — no PRNG, the effects are fixed). Fail-soft cases (missing agent / non-actor / no thread / insufficient awareness / no reach) each emit an `ascendant_expression` trace and no-op, never throw. **Content:** authored `action.bestow` (`targetCategories:['agent']`, `essenceCost: BESTOW_COST`=5, Threadbare prose, unlockable-generic). New constants `BESTOW_COST`/`BESTOW_REACH_BONUS`/`BESTOW_QUINTESSENCE_REGEN`/`BESTOW_MIN_AWARENESS` (`ascendant-expression-constants.ts`). **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); structured trace incl. fail-soft markers, reuses the registered `ascendant_expression` category (#2); deterministic, no PRNG (#3); five fail-soft no-op paths (#4); all additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyBestowPower`); Content (the `action.bestow` template); UI (renders through the existing generic AscendantHand/ActionDrawer once unlocked — no new component; card→beat-grant wiring is THR-515). 15 new tests (`ascendantExpression.test.ts`): `getAscendantPrimaryReach` highest-affinity + fail-soft; gift-artifact + `possesses` creation with both effects; communion passes / intuition + unset fail the faith gate; four fail-soft cases; and **two integration proofs** — `collectAttachmentEffects` surfaces the reach passive for the holder, and `tickEffects` actually restores quintessence by `BESTOW_QUINTESSENCE_REGEN` (and clamps at `quintessenceMax`). Verification: full suite **11753 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components`/`src/views`/HexMapV2 surface touched — the card surfaces through the existing generic drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants. Systemic-wiring guide updated (the bestow agent-targeting "mint a possessed gift" pattern, distinct from the imbue GraphOp-intercept + consecrate sustained-control patterns). PR pending.

✅ **THR-511** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **consecrate** sustained-control spawn bridge + card. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs, consecrate is the per-graph-type verb for **locations**: it converts a temple/shrine into a faith-spreading site via a sustained `ControlEffect`. The blocker (called out in plan §4.4 and the THR-508 split note): every prior sustained control was **hex-targeted**, but consecrate targets a location node, and `spawnControlEffect` neither accepted a location target nor carried the THR-509 spec fields. The consumer side already shipped and was tested in THR-509 — `phaseControlEffects` reads `effect.perTickThreadAuras` + `effect.targetNodeId` and applies `applyCoLocatedThreadAura`, and `getUpkeepStatus` handles `upkeepArtifactId` — so the only gap was the **establishment bridge**. **Engine:** extended `spawnControlEffect` (`src/engine/controlEffectSpawn.ts`) with an optional `graph` param and a location-target branch — when the target id is not a `hex_COL_ROW` but resolves to a `location` node with numeric `hexCol`/`hexRow`, it anchors the effect on those coords and sets `targetNodeId = locationId`; both target shapes now carry `spec.perTickThreadAuras` + `spec.upkeepArtifactId` onto the spawned effect. Fail-soft: a non-hex target with no graph, a location missing hex coords, or a non-location node all return null (no effect, no throw). The 3 call sites in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` pass `state.graph`. **Content:** authored `action.consecrate` (`durationMode:'sustained'` + `controlSpec` with `perTickCost {spirit: CONSECRATE_PERTICK}` and `perTickThreadAuras [{magnitude: CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK}]` faith-spread, `targetCategories:['location']`, `targetSubtypes:['temple','shrine']`, upfront `essenceCost: CONSECRATE_ESTABLISH_COST`). New constants `CONSECRATE_ESTABLISH_COST`/`CONSECRATE_PERTICK` in `ascendant-expression-constants.ts`; reuses the existing `CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`. **NFP:** all magnitudes named (#1); the spawn trace now carries `targetNodeId` (#2); arithmetic-only resolution (#3); the four fail-soft null paths above (#4); purely additive — the existing hex-target path and all 13 prior `controlEffectSpawn` tests are unchanged, only the optional param and a new branch were added (#6). Three pillars: Engine (the spawn bridge); Content (the consecrate template); UI N/A (the card→drawer/bucket wiring is THR-515; no new UI surface). 5 new tests in `controlEffectSpawn.test.ts`: location-target resolves coords + sets `targetNodeId` + carries auras; fail-soft no-graph / no-coords / wrong-node-type; and an end-to-end test feeding the spawned effect through `phaseControlEffects` that proves a co-located threaded pilgrim's `ticksAtCurrentTier` advances by the aura magnitude. Plus a passthrough test asserting both THR-509 fields ride through. Verification: full suite **11740 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw. Browser-verify exempt: engine + content/data only, no `src/components` or HexMapV2 surface touched (the card surfaces in the drawer once THR-515 wires it into a beat's grants + `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS`). **Deferral:** the **relic-mint variant** (high upfront `CONSECRATE_RELIC_UPFRONT` mints a permanent `lossCondition:'permanent'` artifact and injects its *dynamic* id into the effect's `upkeepArtifactId` for zero ongoing upkeep) needs a new mint + dynamic-id-injection path the static `controlSpec.upkeepArtifactId` can't express — split to THR-518. The bridge already carries the field through, so THR-518 is purely the minting card. Systemic-wiring guide updated (the consecrate sustained-control authoring pattern, distinct from the imbue GraphOp-intercept pattern). PR pending.

✅ **THR-517** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #8) — **Beat resolve path: offer→enter→resolve wiring.** The Director (THR-500) only *offered* beats — it set `ascendantBeats.pending` but nothing in the running sim ever cleared it, so no beat (spine or pool) was playable. This closes the loop. **Engine:** new `resolvePendingBeat(state, {chosenActionId?, outcome?}, templateResolver?)` in `src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts` — looks the pending beat up across spine ∪ pool ∪ delivery (new exported `getBeatDefinitionById`, wrapping the private `findBeatDefinition`), applies its grants into `state.unlockedActionIds` (dedup; one `action.unlock.granted` trace `via: 'beat'` per newly-revealed id), then records the `BeatRecord` + clears `pending` by delegating to the existing, already-tested pure `resolveAscendantBeat` (left untouched). Selection beats (`kind:'selection'`) grant exactly one option — `chosenActionId` must be one of the beat's `grantsActionIds`, else it's a no-op (`resolved:false`, the beat stays pending so the player must choose); non-selection beats grant all of `grantsActionIds`. **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** nothing pending → no-op; a pending beat whose definition is unknown (stale save / removed catalogue entry) or whose declared `templateId` fails the injected `templateResolver` clears gracefully via a new `ascendant.beat.skipped` reason `'missing_template'` (the `BeatSkippedTrace` union extended additively) so the queue never wedges; the whole body is try/caught (emits `engine_warning`, leaves the beat pending) so a thrown resolver can't crash a tick. The engine stays registry-free — GameView injects `(id) => getUnifiedTemplateById(id) !== undefined` at the UI boundary. **UI** (new `src/components/Game/AscendantBeatModal.tsx`, mirrors `StoryBeatModal`): a pending pool/optional beat surfaces as a top-center thread-tug **offer banner** (`AscendantBeatOfferBanner`, z-40, simplified THR-340 handoff) the player clicks to enter; spine beats **auto-open modally** (onboarding not missable, gated by `interruptsSuppressed`, non-dismissable). The entered-beat modal renders a kind-derived eyebrow/title/prose placeholder (real Threadbare prose is content — TODO(THR-514)) plus either a single **Receive** CTA (non-selection, shows "You will learn: …" with grant names resolved via `getUnifiedTemplateById`) or a generic **choose-1-of-N picker** (selection) — each option a card with the template's name + description; resolving flows through `handleResolveBeat` → `resolvePendingBeat` → `setGameState`, and the granted card simply appears in the drawer (which already filters on `unlockedActionIds`). **Debug:** `__DEBUG.resolveBeat(chosenActionId?)` (new `DebugResolveBeatResult`; added to `BeatBridge` in `debug-bridge.ts` + the `DebugBridge`/`_registerBeatBridge` shapes in `debug-bridge.d.ts`) resolves the pending beat headlessly via the same engine path; a BeatsDebugTab "Enter & Resolve" control (with a chosen-action-id input for selection beats) drives it from the panel. **NFP:** outcome strings/`missing_template` are inspectable (#2); pure over inputs aside from trace emission, no PRNG (#3); fail-soft table above (#4); all additive — the pure `resolveAscendantBeat` and existing tests are unchanged (#6). Three pillars: Engine (`resolvePendingBeat` + `getBeatDefinitionById`); Content N/A here (grant-only beats; prose/encounter-template handoff is THR-514); UI (modal + banner + picker + debug control). 7 new engine tests (`ascendantBeat.test.ts`): grant-all + history + traces, selection needs-a-valid-choice / grants-exactly-one / rejects-bad-choice, no-duplicate-grant, no-op-when-nothing-pending, fail-soft unknown-beat → `missing_template`, fail-soft templateId-resolver-false → `missing_template`. Verification: full suite **11734 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5174, `?view=game&seeded&size=medium`, fresh-code probed via `__DEBUG.resolveBeat` existence): fired `beat.spine.opening` → modal auto-opened ('A DIVINE CADENCE / Opening' → Receive), `resolveBeat()` → `pending` cleared, `unlockedActionIds` grew `[]→[bind_thread_agent, observe_agent]`, history recorded the `BeatRecord` (outcome 'received'); fired `beat.pool.select.first_true_gift` → offer banner → entered → 3-option picker → clicking 'Imbue' granted **only** `action.imbue` (outcome 'chosen'), unlock set 2→3; **0 console errors** (38 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). Plumbing only — the prose-rich encounter-screen path for template-backed beats is content (TODO(THR-514)). Unblocks THR-504 (scripted spine beats) + THR-514 (pool prose + delivery encounter handoff). Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-506** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Delivery-beat adapter for THR-452 branching content. New `src/engine/deliveryBeatAdapter.ts` maps each registered branching encounter (`LOCATION_BRANCHING_ENCOUNTER_TEMPLATES` — 23 of THR-452's ~30 templates that never fire in normal sim because their reputation/mark/court prereqs are never matured by ambient mortal pathing) into a `delivery` `BeatDefinition`: id `beat.delivery.<sourceId>`, `templateId` = the source encounter, `cadence` trigger, and a normalised `DELIVERY_BEAT_WEIGHT` (0.1) so the 23-entry group stays ~10% of pool draws rather than swamping the base pool (a per-beat weight of 1 would give delivery ~half of all draws). The point of a delivery beat is to **present the encounter to the player-god as a divine vision** — the Director hosts it directly, *sidestepping the mortal-pathing prereqs* that make it unreachable — so `eligibleDeliveryBeats(history)` filters only on dedup-against-already-delivered (the live "currently-valid" gate), deliberately **not** re-applying the encounter's own gates. The Director (`ascendantBeat.ts`) merges those eligible delivery beats into the cadence draw pool (the base `ASCENDANT_BEAT_POOL` stays delivery-free, so the THR-505 pool tests are untouched); `forceOfferBeatById` extended to host delivery beats; `BeatScheduledTrace`/`BeatOfferedTrace` gain optional `templateId` so traces name the wrapped encounter. A new CLI `beat` command (`status` / `list [delivery]` / `fire <beatId>`) is the headless equivalent of THR-507's browser `__DEBUG.fireBeat`; `__DEBUG.listBeats` adds a `delivery` source and `beatSchedule.eligiblePool` reflects the merged set. Resolution (offer→enter→resolve) is still THR-514, so delivery beats fire via force-offer today and naturally once that lands. **Done-when demonstrated:** in a seed-42 CLI run, `beat fire beat.delivery.enc.courtyard_duel` offered the delivery beat with `source template: enc.courtyard_duel` and emitted `ascendant.beat.scheduled … → enc.courtyard_duel`. 9 new tests (`deliveryBeatAdapter.test.ts`): adapter mapping (kind/trigger/templateId/weight/unique ids, no spine/pool collision), id round-trip, eligibility dedup, merged-pool reachability, and force-offer trace carrying the source `templateId`. Verification: full suite **11727 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (THR-489 red baseline — all flagged lines pre-existing, none in the changed code). Browser-verify exempt: engine/data/CLI + types only (the debug-bridge change is a headless read, no UI render). PR pending.

✅ **THR-505** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — Pool beat starter library + unlock catalog. Populated the empty `ASCENDANT_BEAT_POOL` (`src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts`) with 14 cadence-triggered beats (6 introduction / 6 investment / 2 selection) so the Director surfaces living-world beats once the spine is exhausted instead of skipping `empty_pool`; added the §4.3 unlock catalogue — `ASCENDANT_ACTION_BUCKETS` (generic / unlockable-generic / reach-gated + `requiresReach` field for future cards) plus `collectGrantedActionIds()` deriving `beatId→grants` from the `BeatDefinition`s (no drift-prone second map). All grants grounded in **real shipping templates only** (`bind_thread_agent`/`bind_thread_location`/`observe_agent`/`action.imbue`) — no vapor ids. Authored against the **shipped** minimal `BeatDefinition`/`drawFromPool` shape (no eligibility-predicate or identity-bias fields exist on the descriptor; `drawFromPool` takes no world state) → deferred: prose-rich templates + offer→enter→resolve UI path (THR-514), pool/bucket expansion as consecrate/bestow/anoint + reach-gated cards ship (THR-515), eligibility + identity (reach/sphere) draw bias needing a Director draw-signature change (THR-516). 9 new tests (`ascendantBeatPool.test.ts`): Director draws ≥3 distinct pool beats over a deterministic 60-tick run, catalogue covers every granted action, every granted + bucketed id resolves to a real `UnifiedActionTemplate`. Verification: full suite **11718 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3483 baseline). Browser-verify exempt: content/data-only change (`src/data` + tests), no UI surface touched. PR pending.

✅ **THR-508** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence) — **imbue** expression card. Of the four §4.4 expression verbs (imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint), imbue is the one that composes the already-shipped THR-509 primitives end-to-end with **zero new consumer wiring** — so it ships fully-wired here and the other three split into THR-511/512/513 (the split plan §4.4 explicitly anticipated). **Engine/content:** new `action.imbue` `UnifiedActionTemplate` (`targetCategories:['artifact','artifact_legendary']`, `essenceCost: IMBUE_ESSENCE_COST`=4, an unlockable-generic card hidden until a beat grants it) in `ATTACHMENT_ACTION_TEMPLATES`; its `onSuccess` fires a new `imbue_item` GraphOp type, intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts`'s `executeStepResult` (sibling to `faction_verb`/`anoint_successor`/`plant_schism`) and dispatched to `applyImbueItem` (new `src/engine/ascendantExpression.ts`). `applyImbueItem` reads the ascendant's **primary sphere** (`getAscendantPrimarySphere`, off `AscendantProperties.sphereAlignment.primary`), picks a sphere-flavored `AttachmentEffect` via THR-509 `pickSphereFlavoredEffect` fed a locally-derived seeded PRNG (`mulberry32(seed + tick·53 + hash(actor) + hash(target))` — deterministic, doesn't disturb the resolution rng stream), and **appends it to the target artifact node's `properties.effects`**. **Wiring proof (not dead content):** `collectAttachmentEffects` walks an agent's `possesses` edges and reads `properties.effects` off the held artifact, so an imbued artifact's new power applies to whoever carries it — an integration test asserts `collectAttachmentEffects` surfaces the imbued effect for the possessing mortal. New `ascendant_expression` trace category registered in both `TraceCategory` union + `TRACE_CATEGORIES` (THR-509's `ascendant_primitive` was emitted via `as never` and never registered — fixed for this one). **NFP:** `IMBUE_ESSENCE_COST` named constant (#1, new `src/data/ascendant-expression-constants.ts`); structured trace incl. fail-soft markers (#2); injected seeded PRNG (#3); fail-soft no-op + trace on missing/non-artifact target or sphereless ascendant, never throws (#4); all additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (verb dispatch + `applyImbueItem`); Content (the `action.imbue` template + sphere→effect flavoring); UI (renders through the existing ActionCard/drawer once unlocked — no new component; data+engine change, UI-exempt by the file-path rule, still browser-verified). 10 new tests (`ascendantExpression.test.ts`): sphere-match, append-not-replace, determinism, three fail-soft cases, and the effect-walker integration. Verification: full suite **11709 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) → tick 30 / 398 agents / 42 events / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 == 3483; THR-489 baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5191): a dynamic `import('/src/data/unified-action-templates.ts')` confirmed `action.imbue` live in *this* build with `onSuccess` op `imbue_item` and correct fields (freshness-probe per the worktree-preview caveat); `__DEBUG.grantUnlock('action.imbue')` accepted; the drawer correctly shows only the turn-1 Core floor (Move+Investiture) with imbue hidden until unlocked; 0 console errors (66 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). **Split:** `consecrate` → THR-511 (needs a location-targeted sustained-control spawn bridge — `spawnControlEffect` is hex-only and doesn't carry the THR-509 `perTickThreadAuras`/`upkeepArtifactId` fields), `bestow` → THR-512 (needs an agent-castable-ability subsystem — no agent ability store exists; + the unresolved quintessence-regen semantic), `anoint` → THR-513 (needs a `chosen`-status power consumer — confirmed nothing reads `properties.chosen`; THR-509's `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` is an explicit scaffold). Systemic-wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-463** (2026-06-22, Encounter Experience) — interaction-gated camera centering. `cameraInteractionGate.ts` pure predicate; camera pans to the followed agent only on a pending encounter/aftermath or a populated arrival. `'camera_center'` TraceCategory + `CameraCenterTrace`. PR #368.

✅ **THR-501** (2026-06-27, Action System & Unlocks) — Turn-1 floor = Move + Investiture across every action surface; retired the legacy intervention wheel + gated the agent hand and AscendantBar Self/Rare so the floor holds coherently (not a half-state). Substrate: `STARTER_ACTION_IDS=[]`/`STARTER_ACTION_COUNT=0`, stripped `starter:true` from the 12 ex-Starter-12 templates (empty floor + `isActionRevealed` hide every former starter until a beat unlocks it). Agent hand: retired `getAgentWheelSlots` (tier-based radial wheel, no unlock concept, on the rejected-approaches list; flagged `@deprecated`); interventions now flow through unified `divine.*` templates via `getTargetActionSlots` (curated `AGENT_INTERVENTION_TEMPLATES` = 8 divine + observe + thread-creation), now passing `unlockedActionIds` (was omitted → the gate's backward-compat branch revealed all).
