✅ **THR-507** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #8) — `__DEBUG` beat controls + DebugPanel Beats tab. The inspection/test surface for the Director (THR-500): four headless `window.__DEBUG` functions and a live debug tab, so beats can be driven and observed without waiting for natural cadence. **Engine:** new exported `forceOfferBeatById(beats, beatId, turn)` in `src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts` — reuses the Director's internal `offer()` (same scheduled/offered traces, same cursor logic) to force-offer any spine/pool beat by id, advancing the spine cursor only when the fired beat is the one the cursor points at (so debug-firing a passed/future spine beat never corrupts the cursor); returns `null` for unknown ids. **Debug bridge (`src/debug-bridge.{ts,d.ts}`):** `listBeats()` (catalogue: spine + pool with kind/trigger/grants/weight), `beatSchedule()` (live Director snapshot: turn, spineCursor/length, nextSpineBeatId, lastBeatTurn, pending, eligible pool, run-unlocked set, history) — both read-only via `_gameStateProvider` + a dynamic content import; `fireBeat(beatId)` and `grantUnlock(actionId)` are mutating, delegated to a new `_beatBridge` registered by GameView. `grantUnlock` pushes into the existing `unlockedActionIds` (the same set `unlock_action` writes) and emits `action.unlock.granted` with `via: 'debug'` (the trace type already allowed it). **UI:** new `BeatsDebugTab.tsx` + a `Beats` tab in `DebugTabContent` — self-contained (reads via the four `__DEBUG` fns, no prop plumbing; mirrors `ActionUnlocksView`), re-reads on tick change, renders Director state / pending beat / fire+grant controls / run-unlocked list / spine catalogue (with ▶ cursor marker) / pool / history. **NFP:** the only randomness path (`offer` jitter) is untouched/seeded (#3); `forceOfferBeatById` returns `null` rather than throwing on unknown ids and the bridge fns no-op when the game isn't loaded (#4); all changes additive (#6); inspectability is the entire point (#2). Three pillars: Engine (`forceOfferBeatById`); Content N/A (no prose — this is dev tooling); UI (the Beats tab). 3 new tests (force-offer + trace emission, cursor-advance-only-at-cursor, unknown-id → null); existing 14-test beat suite green. Verification: full suite **11699 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, `tsc -b` exits 0 with only the THR-489 baseline errors (none in touched files). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5174): all four `__DEBUG` fns served + working headlessly (`fireBeat('beat.spine.opening')` → pending set + spineCursor 0→1; `grantUnlock('invest.thread_actor')` → run-unlock set grows), Beats tab renders the live state, **0 console errors** (38 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings). Wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-502** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #3) — Home seat (throne) + mana income. The throne is the ascendant's seat of power: a location flagged on the god that yields steady essence and shows a signifier on the map. This is the engine + UI half; the *setting* of the seat in normal play is the scripted spine beat's job (child #5). **Engine/state:** new optional `AscendantProperties.homeSeatLocationId` (`src/types/influence.ts`) — additive, a run without a seat omits it. New tunable `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT` (1.0) in `src/data/influence-content.ts`, re-exported from `influence.ts` and exposed in both CMS surfaces (`registry.ts` + `tunableConstants.ts`, NFP #1). Both essence computations gain one seat term: the engine generator `computeEssenceGeneration` (influence.ts) and the view-layer HUD mirror `computeEssenceIncome` (essenceIncome.ts) each add `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT` for the location named by `homeSeatLocationId` **only when it resolves to a live node** (fail-soft §3.8: a seat pointing at a destroyed location contributes 0), and **exclude that same location from the place-of-power loop** so the seat is a *named higher-yield place of power* that replaces (not stacks with) the 0.5 place-of-power bonus — threading a fresh non-place-of-power location as the seat raises per-tick essence by exactly `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT`. **Throne modeling:** a `location` node + a `controls` edge (ascendant → location), **not a new node type** (load-bearing rule honored). New `setHomeSeat(graph, ascendantId, locationRef?)` engine helper sets the property + ensures the controls edge (deterministic edge id, guarded against duplicates); `locationRef` resolves by exact id → id prefix → exact name → name-substring, and when omitted auto-picks capital → city → first location. **Decision (noted for spine beat #5):** the plan also names a `thread` edge from the ascendant to the seat location; this was deliberately **not** created — thread edges are actor-targeting (carry `ThreadEdgeProperties` tier/maintenance and are iterated by maintenance/promotion/thread-digest/UI as if the target is a mortal), so rippling them onto location nodes is left to #5 with full cross-system analysis. This also keeps the income delta exactly `ESSENCE_PER_SEAT`. **Debug (NFP #2):** `setHomeSeat` wired into the encounter debug bridge (`GameView` — has `touchStructure`/`setGameState` in scope) and exposed as `__DEBUG.setHomeSeat(locationRef?)` (+ `.d.ts` type). **UI:** HexMapV2 seat signifier — a gold ring + crown texture (`buildSeatMarkerTexture`/`addSeatMarker` in `LocationIconMesh.ts`, mirroring the capital-ring pattern, rendered above the base icon and capital ring) keyed off the new `LocationNode.isHomeSeat`, which GameView's `locationNodes` memo sets by comparing each node id to the ascendant's `homeSeatLocationId` (memo now also depends on `gameState.ascendantId`; refreshes via `structuralCacheVersion` which `touchStructure` bumps). **NFP:** seat yield is a named constant (#1); deterministic (#3); fail-soft on missing seat/location (#4); all changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (field + income term on both paths + `setHomeSeat`); Content N/A (no prose — the seat is set mechanically; the constant is the authoring knob); UI (HexMapV2 signifier + auto-reflecting essence HUD via the income mirror). 10 new tests — seat income on both paths (exact-delta, no-stack-with-place-of-power, fail-soft-missing-location), `setHomeSeat` (property + controls edge + raised generation, capital-preferred default, fail-soft no-locations), and `LocationIconMesh` (+1 seat sprite for `isHomeSeat`, crown-path no-throw). Verification: full suite **11696 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (the one `essenceIncome` unused-local at GameView:1275 is the pre-existing memo, untouched; THR-489 baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5173): `__DEBUG.setHomeSeat()` → "Ardenmor Keep" (loc_0), "+1 essence/tick"; the seat hex renders the gold ring + crown signifier (confirmed on Thornhaven when re-seated there); 0 console errors. Observation: changing the seat rebuilds the location layer (existing locations-array-change behavior) → a one-time camera-zoom reset — acceptable since the spine sets the seat once early in normal play. Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-503** (2026-06-27, Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence #4) — Reach gate in `targetActions` + persisted ascendant reach. The ascendant's reach affinities lived only in the Remembrance `AscendantIdentity` and `targetActions.ts` had only a sphere gate; this persists the reaches on the graph node and adds the matching reach gate so reach-locked investment cards (catalog #6, future) can be authored. **Engine/state:** both creation paths (`createAscendant` from `archetype.startingDomainAffinities`, `createAscendantFromIdentity` from `identity.domainAffinities`) now write `domainAffinities` onto the ascendant node via the new optional `AscendantProperties.domainAffinities`; new reader `getAscendantDomainAffinities(graph, ascendantId)` makes the node the single source of truth post-creation. New optional `UnifiedActionTemplate.requiresReach?: ReachDomain` (additive, backward compatible) and tunable `REACH_GATE_MIN_AFFINITY` (0.2) in `src/data/influence-content.ts`. New **filter-9 reach gate** in `getTargetActionSlots()`, placed right after the unlock gate: when a template declares `requiresReach` and affinities are supplied, the card is **hidden entirely** (a `continue`, like the unlock gate — never shown dimmed as aspiration) unless the ascendant's affinity in that reach ≥ the floor. This realizes the director's settled **two-domain lock** (2026-06-26 refinement comment): every ascendant holds a fixed primary+secondary reach for the whole run, so the threshold over the fixed-at-creation affinity map is mathematically a permanent set-membership check (present reaches score 2–5 ≫ 0.2; absent reaches resolve to 0). **Fail-soft (NFP #4):** affinities omitted → gate skipped (fail-open — tests / no-ascendant contexts see all reach cards); `requiresReach` not in `REACH_DOMAINS` → ungated + `console.warn`. **Inspectability (NFP #2):** `FilterCounts.byReach` + optional `TargetActionFilterTrace.filteredByReach`; the filter trace now also emits when reach-hides occur. Both drawer hooks (`useTargetActions`, `useAgentInteraction`) read affinities from the node and pass `ascendantDomainAffinities`. **NFP:** floor is a named constant (#1); deterministic pure filter (#3); all type changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (gate + persistence + reader); Content (`requiresReach` is the new authoring field — added to the systemic-wiring-guide so authors know reach-gating exists); UI (drawer hooks thread the value; browser-verified the drawer still renders + the live witness ascendant node carries `{eye:4,veil:3,shadow:2}`). 8 new tests (6 gate: hidden-off-reach / visible-on-reach / below-floor-hidden / fail-open / ungated-always-visible, + 2 persistence/reader). Verification: full suite **11685 green**, `vite build` green, 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42 medium) reached tick 30 / 398 agents / no throw, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (deterministic stash-diff: 3483 baseline == 3483 with changes; THR-489 red baseline). Browser-verified at 1920×1080 via this-worktree vite (5179): 0 console errors (66 pre-existing GraphSchema/THREE warnings), served-code gate hides off-reach card / shows on-reach + ungated, live ascendant node carries persisted affinities. Systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-509** (2026-06-27) — Reusable ascendant action primitives (Ascendant Beats — Divine Cadence, child issue; blocks the THR-508 expression cards). Built the four general-purpose building blocks the imbue/consecrate/bestow/anoint cards (and future ascendant verbs) sit on, *before* the cards, so authoring stays cheap — the verb is universal, the magic it produces is flavored by the ascendant's domain + sphere. All four live in new `src/engine/ascendantPrimitives.ts`, are pure (graph mutation aside), fail-soft (unknown node/sphere/artifact → no-op + warn, never throw), deterministic (only the sphere picker randomizes, via an injected seeded PRNG), and emit an `ascendant_primitive` trace. **(1) `relic_upkeep_substitute`** — `getUpkeepStatus(effect, graph)` classifies a control effect's upkeep against its sustaining relic (`no_relic`/`waived`/`relic_lost`); `ControlSpec` + `ControlEffect` gain an additive `upkeepArtifactId?`, and `phaseControlEffects` now waives `perTickCost` each tick while that artifact exists and lapses the effect with a new `LapseReason` `'upkeep_relic_destroyed'` when it's gone — the engine half of "mint a permanent relic that sustains a consecration with zero ongoing upkeep." **(2) `co_located_thread_aura`** — `applyCoLocatedThreadAura(graph, locationId, ascendantId, spec, tick)`, a location-scoped per-tick mutation that, for every threaded agent co-located with the target location (and, by default, its `contains`-child sublocations — the three-tier hex→location→sublocation model), adds `spec.magnitude` to a thread-edge field (default `ticksAtCurrentTier`, which drives `checkTierPromotion`). It rides new `ControlEffect.perTickThreadAuras` and is applied every tick by `phaseControlEffects`; first consumer is consecrate's faith-spread (`CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`). **(3) `chosen_status_grant`** — `applyChosenStatusGrant(graph, nodeId, domain, byAscendantId, tick)` flags a node `chosen` (writes `node.properties.chosen` with patron/domain/tick/power) and records a power picked from a `(NodeType × ReachDomain)` `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` scaffold (factions are `actor` nodes; iron→leadership / heart→faith / gold→reputation / shadow→veiled, others null-power but still flagged); re-granting overwrites (latest patron wins) and reports `alreadyChosen`. First consumer: anoint. **(4) `sphere_flavored_effect`** — `pickSphereFlavoredEffect(sphere, rng, tick?)` reads `SPHERE_EFFECT_TABLE` (8 of 12 spheres scaffolded → thematic passive reach bonuses) and picks one `AttachmentEffect` via the injected `() => number` seeded PRNG (`mulberry32(seed)`), so one verb (imbue) yields domain-appropriate magic. The shared spec type (`CoLocatedThreadAuraSpec`) + tuning constants (`CO_LOCATED_AURA_DEFAULT_FIELD`, `CONSECRATE_DEVOTION_PER_TICK`) live in new `src/types/ascendantPrimitives.ts` to avoid an engine→types import cycle from `controlEffect.ts`, and are re-exported from the engine module so consumers (cards, tests, the control phase) have a single import site. **Placement decision (noted for THR-508):** the issue's "new effect kind in the effect union" was realized as reusable resolvers + a per-tick `ControlEffect` rider rather than new members of the 40-type `AttachmentEffect` union or the aftermath-reaction union — this keeps the change fully additive (no ripple through the giant effect resolver, no mutex collision with THR-508's card work) and makes each primitive unit-testable in isolation; THR-508 wires the cards to these resolvers (and may add thin aftermath wrappers then). **NFP:** every magnitude/value is a named constant (#1); each resolver traces, incl. fail-soft markers (#2); the only randomness is an injected seeded PRNG (#3); missing targets no-op + warn (#4); all type changes additive (#6). Three pillars: Engine (the four primitives + phase wiring); Content (the `CHOSEN_POWER_TABLE` / `SPHERE_EFFECT_TABLE` scaffolds + systemic-wiring-guide entry are the authoring surface); UI N/A this foundation (no player-facing surface — browser-verify exempt: engine plumbing, no UI render). 18 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantPrimitives.test.ts`) cover each primitive in isolation plus a faith-spread→tier-promotion integration (a tier-2 thread promotes to tier-3 via accumulated devotion over ticks — the issue's "Done when" CLI-smoke proof) and relic waive/lapse through `phaseControlEffects`. Verification: full suite **11678 green**, `vite build` green, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (the only two errors in touched files — a `checkThreshold` cast and an unused test import — are pre-existing on `origin/main`, THR-489 baseline), and the 30-tick CLI smoke (seed 42, medium) reached tick 30 with 398 agents and no throw. Systemic wiring guide + wiring-checklist updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-500** (2026-06-26) — Ascendant Beat Director foundation (Divine Cadence substrate; child issue #1 of the Ascendant Beats project, blocks the rest). An *Ascendant Beat* is an encounter addressed to the player-god rather than resolved between mortals ("The First" is the hand-wired archetype). This lays the engine substrate: a Director that decides *which* beat fires *when*, the flat state it keeps, and the aftermath effect that turns a resolved beat into a new player capability. **Engine:** new `phaseAscendantBeatDirector` orchestrator phase (`src/engine/ascendantBeat.ts`), registered inline after the post-doom slot and before encounter resolution — a pure `(state, rng)` function that (1) no-ops while a beat is pending (max-one-pending), (2) offers the next scripted spine beat once its trigger is met (advancing `spineCursor`, with `-1` marking spine exhaustion), else (3) does a cadence-gated, seeded-PRNG weighted pool draw. It **only offers** (sets `pending` + emits traces); resolution flows through the existing encounter pipeline, and the exported `resolveAscendantBeat` closes the loop (clears `pending`, appends a `BeatRecord`, emits `ascendant.beat.resolved`). New flat `GameState.ascendantBeats?: AscendantBeatState` (spineCursor/pending/history/lastBeatTurn) — additive/optional so old saves + fixtures fail soft (the Director no-ops when absent); types in new `src/types/ascendantBeat.ts` (`AscendantBeatState`, `PendingBeat`, `BeatRecord`, `BeatKind`, `BeatTrigger`, `BeatDefinition`); init via `createInitialAscendantBeatState()` in `gameInit`. New `unlock_action` aftermath effect (`EncounterAftermathReactionEffect` union member in `unifiedAction.ts`; resolved in `encounterAftermath.ts`): pushes its `actionId` into the run-scoped unlock set, dedup/idempotent, fail-soft on an unknown id (harmless — no template matches so nothing reveals), emits `action.unlock.granted`. **Key coordination decision:** the plan named a new `runUnlockedActionIds` field, but the handoff overrode with "additive fields only on `gameState.ts`" + "mutex with THR-501 (unlock field)"; since the existing `unlockedActionIds` **is** the run-scoped set the action drawer already filters via `isActionRevealed()`, `unlock_action` writes to it directly rather than renaming a 176-importer field — the rename + 2-generic floor init stays THR-501's scope. **Content:** tunables + catalogue in new `src/data/ascendant-beat-content.ts` (`BEAT_BASE_INTERVAL` 9, `BEAT_INTERVAL_JITTER` ±2, `BEAT_MIN_GAP` 4, `BEAT_MAX_PENDING` 1, `SPINE_TRIGGER_TURNS` [0,2,4,6,8], `BEAT_KIND_WEIGHTS`) + a minimal turn-gated spine and an empty pool — the rich prose-first spine (Beats 0–4) and starter pool library are deferred to the child content issues. **Inspectability (NFP #2):** 5 trace categories (`ascendant.beat.{scheduled,offered,skipped,resolved}`, `action.unlock.granted`) added to `TraceCategory`, `TRACE_CATEGORIES`, and the `TraceEntry` union with typed interfaces; a small `emitBeatTrace` wrapper localizes the one cast needed because `Omit<TraceEntry, 'id'|'timestamp'>` collapses the union to base keys. **NFP:** all cadence/spine numbers named constants (#1); seeded PRNG `mulberry32(seed + tick*59 + 503)` for jitter + draw, deterministic spine (#3); the whole phase is try/caught so the tick loop never crashes, with a fallback table for empty pool / missing template / unknown unlock id / destroyed seat / unknown reach (#4); additive throughout except the Starter-12 rework which is explicitly THR-501 (#6). UI pillar (notifications, card-flight reveal, debug tab, hex signifier) is deferred to later child issues — this is engine substrate only, no UI surface (browser-verify N/A). 11 new tests (`src/engine/__tests__/ascendantBeat.test.ts`): Director offer/skip/fail-soft/spine-hold, trigger eval (turn + first_bonded via a thread edge), deterministic weighted `drawFromPool`, `resolveAscendantBeat`, `unlock_action` grow + idempotency, and trace-category registration. Verification: full suite **11660 green**, `vite build` green, **zero net-new `tsc -b` errors** (3483 pre-existing baseline, THR-489 — all my files compile clean), and the 30-tick CLI smoke (`seed 42`, medium) reached tick 30 with 398 agents and no throw, with the Director firing spine beat `beat.spine.opening` on turn 1 (`pending` set, `spineCursor` → 1). Wiring-checklist + systemic-wiring-guide updated. PR pending.

✅ **THR-494** (2026-06-26, THR-493 G1) — Ascendant-reach test fixtures + harness identity param. Built the foundation for the Content Health Report matrix (per-seed × per-ascendant-reach trigger coverage): eight deterministic `AscendantIdentity` fixtures, one per primary Reach, plus a reusable identity-path state builder and a `--ascendant` flag on the gameplay-report harness. New `src/data/__fixtures__/ascendant-reach-fixtures.ts` declares the canonical 1:1 `REACH_PRIMARY_SPHERE` map (Force→Iron, Matter→Stone, Energy→Eye, Life→Gold, Mind→Veil, Spirit→Heart, Time→Star, Entropy→Shadow — authoritative for the fixtures; deliberately distinct from the fuzzy one-to-many affinity maps in `meetingEncounter.ts`/`agendaGenerator.ts`), the ordered `REPORT_ASCENDANT_REACHES` axis (the NFP constant), a pure `buildReachAscendantIdentity(reach, map)` (primary sphere = the reach's paired sphere; secondary sphere = the next Creation sphere cyclically, always distinct; `domainAffinities` top out at the pinned reach so it's the dominant reach), the baked `ASCENDANT_REACH_FIXTURES` record, and `getAscendantReachIdentity(reach)` which fails soft on an unknown reach with a clear error listing the valid reaches. New `src/testing/ascendantReachHarness.ts` exposes `buildAscendantReachState(reach, seed, {mapSize?, cosmology?})` over the engine's `initializeGameStateFromIdentity` (cosmology derived from the identity by default; map size honors the caller). Wired `--ascendant <reach>`, `--ascendants iron,gold`, and `--all-ascendants` into `scripts/gameplay-report.ts` (runs the identity path, expands an ascendant × seed matrix, prints the resolved `primaryReach=` per run as the acceptance check, tags JSON output with the reach) and added `resetDecisionCache()` to `runOnce` so every report run is deterministic regardless of order — this also fixed cross-run `npc_NNNN` ID drift on the existing random-archetype path. NFP: all magic numbers named (`PRIMARY_DOMAIN_AFFINITY`, `SECONDARY_DOMAIN_AFFINITY`); determinism via fixed fields + counter resets; fail-soft on unknown reach. Three pillars: Engine (fixtures + helper + harness param); Content N/A (config, not prose); UI N/A. 5 fixture/determinism tests (`src/testing/__tests__/ascendantReachFixtures.test.ts`) — all 8 fixtures × 4 seeds build valid state without throwing, same (reach, seed) → identical structural fingerprint, unknown reach throws a listing error. Full suite 11649 green; `vite build` green; zero net-new `tsc -b` errors (3482 pre-existing baseline, THR-489, no errors mention the new files); acceptance run `npm run gameplay-report -- --ascendant heart --seed 42 --ticks 60` reached tick 60 and reported `primaryReach=heart`. Browser-verify N/A (no UI surface). Unblocks the rest of THR-493.

✅ **THR-476** (2026-06-23) — Find-first content-lint (THR-414 verdict #3 — "Find gates Change/Control" kept as a soft per-template convention enforced by lint, not a hard engine invariant). New advisory `scripts/lint-find-first.ts` + `npm run lint:find-first` flags `crudType:'update'` (Change/Control) `UnifiedActionTemplate`s that lack a Find gate. **Load-bearing interpretation:** there is no per-template Find-prerequisite field; the engine's "perceive before you act" gate is layer revelation (`narrativeLayer`, Gate 7 in `getTargetActionSlots`), so the lint treats **"Found" = `narrativeLayer` present** — and deliberately adds no schema field. A template passes if `narrativeLayer` is set, or `starter === true` (THR-419 starter-floor), or its id is in the curated script-local `FIND_GATE_EXEMPT_IDS` (empty by default — every entry needs a rationale). Severity is warning-only and the script **always exits 0**: it's advisory, the flagged list is the content-author worklist, and promoting it to CI-blocking is a deliberate follow-up (mirrors `lint:encounter-content`, which fails only on errors). Iterates `UNIFIED_ACTION_TEMPLATES` (already aggregates every encounter-content array), groups warnings by reach, prints a summary. Baseline: 226 Change/Control templates → 27 gated, 5 starter, 0 exempt, 194 flagged. Exported pure `classifyFindGate`/`runFindFirstLint` with 6 fixture tests (`scripts/__tests__/lint-find-first.test.ts`). Dev-tooling only (lint reads templates; Content/UI/Engine N/A per the verdict — not a runtime invariant): `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `vite build` green, full suite 11644 green. PR pending.

✅ **THR-487** (2026-06-23) — CI cleanup, merge = Done. Deleted the dead `.github/workflows/claude-review.yml` — its review verdict was a permanent `skip` placeholder (THR-182/183 never replaced it), so its `transition-to-done` job (gated on `verdict == 'pass'`) never fired and any issue reaching In Review stalled forever. Rewrote `.github/workflows/linear-autoclose.yml`: it now transitions referenced issues straight to **Done** (resolving the team's `completed`-type state at runtime, robust to renames) instead of the dead-end In Review, and gained a `pull_request: [closed]` merged trigger that scans the PR **title + body** for `(Fixes|Closes|Resolves) THR-XX` so a PR-body keyword closes the issue even when a non-squash merge commit drops it (impediment #140); the original push-to-main commit-message scan is retained as a fallback. Both trigger paths share one idempotent transition loop (already-completed/cancelled issues skip), so the double-fire on a merged PR is harmless. `ci.yml` left untouched — still the required `Test · Typecheck · Build` gate (job name preserved → no branch-protection change). YAML + embedded github-script JS syntax-validated; 11638 tests green; vite build clean. Workflow-only change (no `src/`, no UI, no engine) — engine-smoke and browser-verify N/A. CLAUDE.md DoD wording left to WS1 (THR-486) per the plan's mutex note. PR pending.

Then authored values per `Docs/plans/2026-06-23-content-census-value-authoring-rubric.md`: `censusTag.scale` on all 177 applicable entries (6 conditions personal, 44 omens regional/cosmic, 119 attachments via subcategory→tier, 5 spells via targeting, 3 artifacts cosmic) and `censusTag.reach` on the 47 no-derivation entries (44 omens spread across all 8 reaches — veil 11 / stone 9 / eye 6 / star 6 / heart 4 / iron 4 / gold 2 / shadow 2; 7 cosmic / 37 regional; plus 2 effect-less attachments + 1 effect-less spell). Derivable reach left to the adapter (single source of truth, NFP #2/#3). Browser-verify exempt (data-only / offline census artifact, no UI surface). Landed the bouncing rubric plan doc in the same PR.

✅ **THR-491** (2026-06-23) — CI skips the heavy `Test · Typecheck · Build` suite on doc-only changes. Added a cheap `detect` job (`dorny/paths-filter@v3`) to `.github/workflows/ci.yml` that flags whether any non-doc file changed, and gated the heavy `check` job on `if: needs.detect.outputs.code == 'true'`. A required job skipped via a job-level `if:` reports `skipped`, which GitHub counts as **success** for branch protection — so doc-only PRs merge with no 20-min run, while code/mixed PRs run the full suite exactly as before. The `check` job keeps the exact name `Test · Typecheck · Build`, so the branch-protection required-context config needs no change. Skips `**/*.md`, `Docs/**`, `.planning/**`, `Design/**`; still gates `src/**`, `world-model.json`, `package.json`/lockfile, `.github/**`, and any mixed doc+code PR. Workflow-level `permissions: { contents: read, pull-requests: read }` lets paths-filter list changed files via the GitHub API. **Two-PR history:** the initial implementation (PR #385) shipped a non-functional filter — under dorny/paths-filter's default `predicate-quantifier: 'some'` (OR), the leading `'**'` pattern matched every file, so `code` was always `true` and doc-only PRs still ran the full suite. The original test PR #386 was a false positive: it predated the `detect` job on its base, so it reported no `check` job at all and merely looked CLEAN. Diagnosed via a real doc-only PR (#388) whose `detect` log showed `Filter code = true` on three `Docs/*.md` files. Fixed by adding `predicate-quantifier: 'every'` (each file must match `'**'` AND none of the negations → only non-doc files qualify), then re-verified that a doc-only PR reports `check` = skipped. PRs #385 + #388.

✅ **THR-451** (2026-06-12) — Outcome Economy Retune: eliminate failure-dominant world. two divine actions `action.mentorship.inspire` (sets `mentorshipInspireBonus` mirroring `initiativeInspireBonus`) and `action.mentorship.sever` (flag picked up by `phaseMentorship` to force Falling Out). **UI:** new `getMentorships(graph, agentId)` query reading both edge directions; ✅ **THR-433** (2026-05-14) — Kindle a Calling, internal-pressure divine action. New `action.faction.kindle_a_calling` (heart/force, cost 10, tier 2) replaces a faction's `pursues` ambition with one drawn by a four-signal bias resolver: (a) member axiological pulls — sum of `axiologicalProfile` across living non-army members, normalized for faction size; (b) leader bias — the seat's profile weighted 2× higher; (c) doctrine pressure — `recoveredDoctrineId` adds bias toward cultural_dominance + divine_mandate; (d) recent dissent — dissentLevel ≥ KINDLE_CALLING_DISSENT_BIAS_THRESHOLD (0.35) pulls toward defensive_consolidation and away from territorial_expansion. The bias map is added to `scoreEligibleAmbitions` weights (newly exported from `factionAmbitions.ts`, along with `selectAmbitionType`, `mulberry32`, `hashString`). KINDLE_CALLING_ESSENCE_SHARPENING (1.4) scales bias contributions. A seeded PRNG draws the chosen candidate using `state.seed + state.tick * 47 + hashString(factionId) + KINDLE_CALLING_PRNG_SALT`. The old ambition node is removed; a new `amb_${factionId}_${tick}_kindled` ambition node is added with `kindled: true` properties + a new `pursues` edge. `getArmyLockedAmbitionId` refuses to overwrite an ambition any army (`armyState != null` member) is pursuing — `lockedByArmy: true` trace branch fires instead. Plants `faction.encounter.calling_named` (2 steps, 2 aftermath reactions: commit → reputation tally + follow-on encounter seed (faction_internal_pressure, delay 5); stall → hidden mark `calling_stalled` revealable to faction_internal_pressure encounters) on the leader. Leader gets the `kindled_calling_pending` condition for KINDLED_CALLING_PENDING_DURATION (18) ticks. New `FactionKindleCallingTrace` with `previousAmbitionType`, `newAmbitionType`, `candidates` (bias-weighted), `lockedByArmy`, `noEligibleCandidates`, `seededEncounterId`, `leaderId`. 11 new constants in `faction-action-constants.ts`. New `FactionGovernanceVerbKind: 'kindle_a_calling'` dispatch added to `factionGovernanceVerbs.ts`; `unifiedActionResolution.ts` type assertion widened. `FactionNetworkAmbition.kindled?: boolean` added — `FactionSheet.tsx` Current Agenda section shows a ▲ glyph next to the ambition name + "kindled" sublabel when the active ambition was rolled by Kindle a Calling. 9 new unit tests in `factionGovernanceVerbs.test.ts` (no-faction, no-definition, no-eligible-candidates, replacement, previous-ambition-removal, army-lock refusal, determinism across identical setups, dissent biases away from territorial_expansion, doctrine biases toward cultural_dominance). Verification: 11309 tests pass; tsc clean; vite build 12.14s; 30-tick CLI smoke green (379 agents, 79 events); browser load at 1920×1080 with 0 console errors, __DEBUG state assertion confirms template+encounter+trace+constants wired. Cowork handoff comment (2026-05-14 09:27) served as plan doc — the formal plan doc was untracked locally (third flush-plan-docs bounce noted on the issue); sibling THR-432 plan used as architectural reference. Mutex with THR-400, THR-430, THR-432 (all Done before pickup) and THR-431 (still In Design — no file collision). PR pending.

✅ **THR-430** (2026-05-14) — Schism, deferred-resolution faction-split divine action. New `action.faction.schism` (star/chaos, cost 14, tier 3) plants `schismPendingResolutionTick` + `schismBaselineCohesion` on the faction; SCHISM_PENDING_DURATION_TICKS later, `phaseSchismResolution` decides reform-or-split via `splitPressure = clamp01(0.4·cohesionDrop + 0.4·spread + 0.2·dissent)` against a seeded sample. New `performFactionSplit` mints splinter actor node, partitions the lowest-rep half of `member_of` edges, copies `relates_to`, creates hostile back-edge — observationally identical to the existing `faction_splinter` aftermath. New `performFactionReform` applies reputation penalty + SCHISM_REFORM_EXPULSION_COUNT most-misaligned expulsions (clamped to leave ≥1 standing). 9 new tunable constants in `game-config.ts`. 3 new trace categories (`schism_planted` / `schism_resolved` / `faction_reformed`) with typed interfaces in `factionAction.ts`. `plant_schism` GraphOp intercepted in `unifiedActionResolution.ts` sibling to `faction_verb`. Debug: `window.__DEBUG.schism.list()`. 15 new tests; 11257 total green; tsc clean; vite build 8.75s; 30-tick CLI smoke green (379 agents, 79 events). Deferred to follow-ups: HexMap pending-schism signifier ring, `faction.encounter.schism_resolution` 2-branch encounter template with 3 new conditions (`schismatic_loyalty`/`reformist_survivor`/`between_doctrines`), `__DEBUG.schism.forceResolve()`. Plan: `Docs/plans/2026-05-12-thr-430-schism-faction-split.md`.

✅ **THR-12** (2026-05-14) — Hex Vignette Phase 4: Raycaster-based landmark interaction and GPU-attribute selection easing. New `LandmarkRaycaster.pickLandmark()` replaces screen-space projection with `THREE.Raycaster.intersectObjects` across all InstancedMesh batches — precise at any camera angle. New `VignetteSelectionState` tracks hover/selection per `(batchKey, instanceIndex)`, eases `aHoverMix`/`aSelectionMix` toward target via `tickEasing(deltaMs, layer)` — per-frame smooth GPU-attribute transitions without React re-renders. Shader (`VignetteInstanceMaterial`) gained `aSelectionMix` attribute + `vSelectionMix` varying + `uSelectionColor` uniform (gold tint, blended after hover). `ChunkedLandmarkLayer` gained `getBatchInfos()` (readonly view of all batches), `setInstanceAttribute()` (updateRange-scoped GPU upload), and `aSelectionMix` zero-initialization in `applyInstances`. Canvas: `pointermove` calls `pickLandmark` → `setHovered`, cursor flips to `pointer` on hit; `pointerdown` calls `pickLandmark` → `setSelected` + fires `onLandmarkSelect`; `keydown:Escape` calls `clearAll()` + `onLandmarkSelect('', '')`; render loop passes `deltaMs` to `tickEasing` each frame. Debug: `showClickTargetSpheres` toggle (in `VignetteDebugOverlay`) renders translucent fill circles at click-target world positions. `window.__TERRAIN_LAB` extended with `selectLandmark(id)`, `gotoLandmark(id)`, `getSelectionState()`, `clearSelection()`. 7 new Phase-4 constants (`HOVER_MIX_TARGET=0.45`, `SELECTION_MIX_TARGET=0.9`, `HOVER_MIX_EASE_MS=120`, `SELECTION_MIX_EASE_MS=160`, `CLICK_DEBUG_SPHERE_OPACITY=0.55`, `RAYCASTER_LANDMARK_RECURSIVE=false`, `LANDMARK_HOVER_CURSOR='pointer'`). 15 new tests (5 raycaster: miss, no-registry, hit, stale-registry, negative-instanceId; 10 selection-state: hover/select/clear, easing progression, fail-soft on stale key, reverse on clear). Verification: 11257/11257 tests pass (734 files), tsc clean, vite build 7.55s. PR pending.

✅ **THR-11** (2026-05-12) — Hex Vignette Phase 3: Landmark Batch Layer. Replaced clone-per-placement GLB rendering with `InstancedMesh` batching via new `ChunkedLandmarkLayer` (mirrors the `ChunkedFillerLayer` pattern from Phase 2). One draw call per submesh across all instances of a given model. New `VignetteClickRegistry` stores `LandmarkClickEntry` (click target + `batchKey` + `instanceIndex`) pre-wired for Phase 4 raycaster. - Agent Behavior Phase 1 — Encounter Cache + Unified Decision Pipeline: ✅ Complete (2026-03-18) — 8 new engine modules replacing the disconnected movement heuristic + action selection with a unified encounter-driven decision pipeline. distanceMatrix.ts (BFS hop-distance precomputation), encounterCache.ts (global cache with event-based invalidation, EncounterCacheEntry with pre-computed scoring data), encounterAwareness.ts (per-reach awareness filter — capability-scaled range, flesh capped at 1 hop, max 5 hops), factionAwareness.ts (rank-gated reach-filtered faction network intelligence, up to 20 entries at max rank), encounterFilterPipeline.ts (5-stage: awareness→visibility→prerequisites→threat→performance cap with diversity floor), encounterScoring.ts (value/tick × desire multiplier, deterministic selection of highest finalScore), idleBehavior.ts (Heart axis personality-driven drift vs stay), phaseAgentDecision.ts (Phase 2b orchestrator function). Orchestrator wired: phaseAgentDecision replaces phaseIdleSelection. Multi-tick step mechanics (occupiedUntilTick). 5 new trace categories. Lazy-init cache + distance matrix with resetDecisionCache(). 126 new tests across 9 test files, 5,248 total tests pass. Design doc: Docs/plans/2026-03-18-agent-decision-and-encounter-awareness-design.md.

- Agent Behavior Phase 0 Prerequisites: ✅ Complete (2026-03-18) — 9 tasks preparing for unified decision pipeline. Type extensions: EncounterStep.duration (tick cost per step), EncounterTemplate.remoteAttempt (faction-scale encounters), EncounterProgress.occupiedUntilTick (multi-tick occupation). Data: SPHERE_OPPOSITIONS lookup (8 symmetric pairs), duration backfill on 220 encounter steps (quick=1, multi-day=3-5, siege=5-10). Social infrastructure: RelatesToEdgeProperties with trust field (init from sentiment×0.5), MemberOfEdgeProperties with rank+joinedTick, faction reachPreferences (normalized from domainCapabilities). Graph utility: findAllPaths bounded DFS (max hops + 100-node safety cap) for reputation walks. 11 new tests. Unblocks Phase 1 (encounter cache + decision pipeline).

- Agent Behavior Phase 3 — Tier Promotion & Capability Growth: ✅ Complete (2026-03-18) — 2 new engine modules (capabilityGrowth.ts, tierPromotion.ts) implementing encounter-driven domain capability progression. capabilityGrowth: applyEncounterGrowth creates/accumulates synthetic experience traits on the graph (per-domain, per-agent), computeGrowthAmount with difficulty scaling (5 bands), promotion-eligible multiplier (×2), diminishing returns (×0.7 factor), failure growth fraction (×0.2). tierPromotion: handleTierPromotion grants named trait signifiers at even tiers for all 9 reaches (45 total: "Blooded"→"Avatar of Iron", etc.), bumps faction rank (primary ×0.1, secondary ×0.05). Wired into legacy encounter pipeline (encounter.ts resolveEncounter) and unified action resolution (unifiedActionResolution.ts executeStepResult). encounterScoring.ts growthValue stub replaced with real tier-proximity estimation. tier_promotion TickEvent type added. 19 new tests across 2 test files, 5,320 total tests pass. Design doc: Docs/plans/2026-03-18-tier-promotion-and-capability-growth-design.md.

- Agent Behavior Phase 2 — Resolution Modifier Pipeline: ✅ Complete (2026-03-18) — 1 new engine module (resolutionModifiers.ts) replacing hardcoded ENCOUNTER_SPHERE_FACTOR/ENCOUNTER_DIFFICULTY_MODIFIER with contextual modifier pipeline. 5 sub-functions: computeSphereAlignmentBonus (match +0.10, opposition -0.10 via SPHERE_OPPOSITIONS), computeEquipmentModifier (walks possesses/bonded_to edges for reachBonus, per-item cap 0.08, total cap 0.15), computeTerrainModifier (TERRAIN_RESOLUTION_MODIFIERS lookup + faction control +0.05 / hostile territory -0.05, cap 0.10), computeTraitBonus (walks has_trait edges for resolutionBonus, per-trait cap 0.05, total cap 0.10), divine intervention stub (returns 0). encounter.ts resolveEncounter now uses full pipeline; encounterScoring.ts estimateStepProbability accepts optional modifierTotal. 4 starter attachments enriched with reachBonus (Iron Blade, Ashenmane's Fang, Road-Worn Mule, Whispering Eye). 30 new tests, 5,275 total tests pass.

- Agent Sprite Scale Bug + Zoom Threshold Unification: ✅ Complete (2026-03-25) — Settle animation preserves base scale via userData.baseScale multiplier; deleted AGENT_ZOOM_THRESHOLDS in favor of centralized ZOOM_VISIBILITY_MATRIX; continental retinue group now correctly shown.

- Road-Aware Agent Movement: ✅ Complete (2026-03-25) — Full mechanical integration of roads and trails into gameplay, implemented per design doc (6 decisions). (1) Road-aware pathfinding: Dijkstra considers road edges (both outgoing and incoming) with discount multipliers (major 0.4×, trail 0.7×), PathResult extended with RoadSegmentInfo[]. (2) Hex-level movement state: MovementState gains currentHexPosition, roadHexQueue, roadHexCost, currentRoadType (all optional, backward-compat). (3) Road mode in tickMovement: agents advance hex-by-hex along road hexPath, located_at only updates on arrival at location nodes, mixed path support. (4) Gated re-evaluation: moving agents get 5-guard decision check (tick gating, score comparison, 1.5× reroute threshold, action-type guard, target invalidation) instead of blanket skip. (5) Animation road mode: startRoadHopAnimation factory (300ms major / 500ms trail), reduced wobble (0.3×), hop chaining without settle bounce until final hex. (6) Trace types: RoadHexTransitionTrace and AgentRerouteTrace. Design doc: Docs/plans/2026-03-25-road-aware-movement-design.md. 5,896 tests pass.

New `action.faction.anoint_successor` (iron/force, cost 12, tier 3) creates a `will_succeed: agent → faction` edge; thread is silent until the current leader exits. New `phaseFactionSuccession` (post-narrative slot) detects leader exit via `leaderSnapshotId` cache and runs recency-wins resolution, setting a `leads: agent → faction` edge (authoritative when present, existing score derivation as the untouched fallback) and planting a `faction.encounter.inheritance` seed. `getAnointedLeaderId` is the single seam consulted by both leader-resolution paths in the codebase. 2 new EdgeTypes, 7 new tunable constants, 2 new trace categories with 5 succession outcome variants. Inheritance encounter has 2 aftermath reactions (accept / refuse) with effects (reputation tally + follow-on seed / hidden mark `refused_inheritance`). ✅ THR-74 (PR 2b, reachability gate) — party-exclusive content became reachable: a grouped agent is still `actorType: 'individual'`, so a `['group']`-only template never matched them and `minGroupMembers` had no consumer. `generateUnifiedCandidates` now lets a grouped individual draw a group-affinity template when their company can field `minGroupMembers` living members (new `livingGroupMemberCount` helper, deceased echoes excluded). Pure additive seam — no `['group']`-only template ships yet, so live behavior is unchanged and the smoke's outcome split is identical; the gate lands one PR ahead of its delve content so that content is reachable not orphaned. ✅ THR-74 (PR 2b part 6, Seeking Companions) — a threaded company's founding stopped being a silent ledger line: the last engine+content group seam. When the systemic formation scan binds a company holding at least one threaded mortal, `phaseGroups` fires an authored founding moment — **eager** (kindred spirits, a quick clasp of hands) or **wary** (strangers who bargained first), chosen by starting cohesion — sibling of The Parting and the fray moment. New cause `seeking_companions` (`groupFormation.ts`, precedence draw_together > seeking_companions > systemic), composer `groupSeeking.ts`, content `group-formation-content.ts`; the `group_formed` trace now surfaces the real cause instead of a hardcoded `systemic`. The plan's interactive 5-step branching drawer encounter (Rejection → negative `relates_to`) is deferred to THR-759, the same authored-moment-now / interactive-dilemma-later split The Shared Spoils took.
✅ THR-74 (PR 3, UI pillar — DONE) — companies became visible, closing the ticket. Three player-facing surfaces: a HexMapV2 cluster (enclosing ring + central bond glyph per active company, gold when the ascendant threads a member, neutral otherwise — a new `CompanyClusterMesh` overlay below the agent dots, so the member dots the agent layer already draws complete the "ring + dots + glyph" at close zoom and fall away to a ringed glyph at far zoom, no counts ever); an AgentProfileModal "Company" section (generated name, cohesion as prose — bound/holding/frayed/breaking, never a number — and a click-through roster) fed by a new public `company` field on the agent card; and a DebugPanel Companies tab rendering the `getGroups()` readout with numeric cohesion. Zero new GameState fields — everything reads the graph via worldVersion. Browser-verified live at 1920×1080 (3 companies, `companyClusters` layer rendering 6 marks, Company section screenshot, no console errors). The last of THR-74's multi-PR arc: engine core → resolution hook + template sweep → reachability gate → party-exclusive delves → The Parting / The Shared Spoils / Old Wounds → Bless this Company → Draw Together → Seeking Companions → this UI.
✅ THR-701 — the design-audit-pipeline stopped auditing against less than it advertised: three inputs had silently drifted while all three fallbacks degraded gracefully, so the pipeline reported PASS regardless. The Vision-file fallback path in `vision-prompt.md` pointed at `Design/Vision/*` (the files live at `TheFantasyWorldSimulator/Vision/*`; the `vision-audit.ts` tool was already correct); `taste-profile.md` was in the Obsidian vault but missing from the git-tracked repo mirror, making the vision auditor's taste check unassessable — restored byte-identical; and `nfp-prompt.md` read a `Docs/design-brief.md § NFPs` section that never existed, so it fell back to CLAUDE.md every run — repointed at `CLAUDE.md § Non-Functional Priorities` permanently rather than duplicating the NFP table into a brief that would just re-drift.
✅ THR-738 (live verification — DONE) — the phantom-Done class was proven dead rather than assumed dead. THR-74 had been swept to Done ×2–3 in one day by references that only *mentioned* it, including a sentence documenting the discipline; the repo half (PR #823) line-anchored our own workflow, and Christian disabled Linear's native auto-close on 2026-07-25 (per-team **Workflows & automations → Pull request and commit automations**, not the Settings → Integrations → GitHub path this file and ~20 briefs had wrongly recorded). Two settings changed by two parties with no test between them is exactly the shape of a fix that looks done and isn't, so this pass ran the live end-to-end proof against a throwaway scratch issue (THR-765, parked in `Idea` + assigned so it could never enter the pickup queue). **PR #836 (negative control)** fired all three vectors simultaneously — bare id in the branch name *and* the PR title, close keyword inside a prose sentence — and left the scratch issue untouched: a single `stateHistory` entry with `endedAt: null`, `completedAt: null`, both auto-close runs green and logging `No Linear issues referenced — nothing to close`. **PR #837 (positive control)**, on a branch with no `thr-` prefix and a title with no bare id so the standalone keyword line was the only possible signal, closed it: `Issues to close (→ Done): THR-765` → `✅ THR-765 → Done`, with the second run correctly reporting `already closed — skipping` (idempotency confirmed). The negative control is load-bearing precisely because our own workflow was *provably incapable* of acting on it — checked pre-merge against the shipped helper, `extractCloseableIssueIds` returned `[]` while the native-shaped detector still matched `THR-765` — so the issue surviving isolates the native integration as genuinely disabled, not merely unexercised. PR linking still fires (both PRs and commits attached to the scratch issue), which is the intended end state: links without auto-move.
✅ THR-731 (PR 1, band spawner) — companies got opposition their own size. Factions now field **bands**: three to six of their own named members, mustered from a colocated cluster and sent out as an ordinary `faction_band` company (THR-74), marked `bandRole`/`bandFactionId`. "Ordinary" is load-bearing — `isCompanyNode` accepts a band, so `phaseGroups` moves, frays and dissolves it unchanged, and its members are real agents whose deaths are real graph deaths. Two substrate corrections against the plan, both grep- and runtime-verified: the specified **lair raider** trigger cannot fire (a lair mints exactly one named elite ever, monster factions carry no `member_of` roster — 13 lairs / 0 elites at tick 0), deferred to THR-767 with the guild path shipped instead as the ticket's founding assassins-guild fantasy; and the plan's `BAND_SPAWN_LAIR_STAGE` names a numeric stage the lair system doesn't have (it gates on `lairTier`). Band eligibility deliberately bypasses `isGroupEligibleAgent`'s `spotlightTier` check — that gate is an attention budget for player-facing formation, and applying it left exactly one faction in the world able to muster. Structural eligibility gates the spawn roll rather than the reverse, after a roll-first sweep spent its whole probability budget on barren guilds and produced zero bands. Bands are named for whose they are ("The Arcane Circle's Ravens"), not where they met.
✅ THR-731 (PR 2, opposed group resolution) — a company that walks into a band now fights it. The pairing rides identity, not template lists: a completing company encounter whose hex holds a foreign band gets a synthesized band counter-action (`bandRole` → `encounter.band_defend` / `band_raid`), and `detectContestations` gains a group pass that emits the pair with the initiator attacking and the band defending — bands answer, they never open, so no scale/FIFO tiebreak is needed. The group pass runs first and *claims*, which deliberately gives an authored `contestsWith` contest the stronger hold on any action it wants. Resolution stayed where it was: `resolveContestationPair` (TB-044) does the rolling, extended only so each side prices its step through `resolveGroupStep` — a six-strong band used to fight exactly as hard as its leader alone, the one place in the engine where company strength silently did not apply. Two substrate finds, both grep-verified: `opposingGroupId` was named by THR-74's plan and never implemented (zero hits), so this PR adds it rather than populating it; and `contested_won`/`contested_lost` had shipped with TB-044 carrying ChapterView display strings, a `playerReceipts` severity mapping and an `isActionSuccess` branch, with **zero producers** — group contests are its first, so losing a fight finally reads differently from failing one. Consequences beyond the step's own ops: cohesion ±, a casualty on a decisive loss (marked deceased, never the leader while anyone else stands, so `reconcileLostMembers` closes the edge next tick and the shipped cascade can carry the loser to dissolution), and a mutual `hostile_to` grudge carrying its tick and cause. The band counter-templates are ordinary group-exclusive encounters rather than a synth-only pool — fencing them off would have been exactly the special-casing this ticket is under orders to resist, and would have left them unreachable in a live run. Proven organically, no forcing: at t81 (seed 42, medium) Company of the Inn met the Temple of the Spheres' Sparrows, killed Bagaabraa and took the ground; Flintlock's Band met the same span at t84; the band fell 0.70 → 0.19 and disbanded.
✅ THR-731 (PR 3, confrontation content) — the fight got a face, and a reachability hole closed on the way. Four authored group-exclusive confrontations — **The Ambush**, **Den Assault**, **The Guild Falls**, **The Standoff** — each gated on a live opponent by a new `requiresOpposingBand` template flag, because an encounter *about* fighting a particular band is a scene with an empty chair in it when no band is there. Reaches rotate across every template's steps so best-member substitution spotlights a different companion each step rather than the same fighter three times; all four score 100/100 on the prose scorer with zero flags. Enforcing the gate surfaced the finding worth more than the content: company templates travel **two** draw paths and only one gated. `generateUnifiedCandidates` has checked `minGroupMembers` since THR-74, but the location-cache path (`getEncountersByLocationType` → `encounterCache` → `encounterFilterPipeline`) had **no actor-affinity stage whatsoever** — measured at implementation time, `getEncountersByLocationType("ruins")` returned 7 group-exclusive templates, including THR-74's own shipped `sunken_vault`/`broken_span`/`hollow_watch`, with nothing downstream reading `actorAffinities`. Party-exclusive delves were reachable by solo agents in direct contradiction of the authoring contract written in their own source comment. Stage 3 — the pipeline's documented prerequisites stage, previously a "placeholder for future trait prerequisites" — now enforces `minGroupMembers` and `requiresOpposingBand`, both resolved lazily at most once per agent. The Standoff's non-lethal rung is made structural rather than left to a low roll: a `contestNonLethal` flag skips the casualty roll entirely, so losing costs cohesion, standing and the ground but never a life, and an unknown template id defaults to lethal so a missing lookup cannot silently make band conflict bloodless. `PendingEncounterSeed.opposingGroupId` shipped in PR 2 with **zero readers** — a seed naming its enemy dropped it in silence — so the seed → action carry now re-validates at spawn (node exists ∧ `isBandNode` ∧ active ∧ ≥1 living member) and degrades to an ordinary encounter rather than pointing at a corpse. **Not yet observed firing organically:** contests keep firing on their own (t85, seed 42 medium), but no confrontation drew in runs to 250 ticks — the family needs company ∧ live foreign band ∧ matching location subtype ∧ winning the scoring pass simultaneously, and bands and companies are rarely colocated *at a matching subtype* on this seed. Reachability is unit-proven at both gates; deterministic live proof waits on PR 4's `spawn band`, which the plan's Done-when already anticipates.
✅ THR-731 (PR 4, band CLI + Rivals line — ticket complete) — the two observability gaps closed, and with them a defect that had been hiding behind them. `spawn band <faction> [--role raider|defender]` reaches all three surfaces (headless CLI, in-game CLI, `__DEBUG.spawnBand`) and forces a muster while keeping **every** structural precondition — per-faction cap, member reserve, colocated cluster, monster-faction skip — with each refusal naming the precondition that failed, because "why is this guild not mustering?" is the real question. Deliberately **no `--hex`** despite the plan mirroring `spawn npc`: a band musters from its faction's own colocated members, so a hex would either be ignored or mean teleporting a muster into being, the one thing the shipped spawner refuses. It is currently the only way a `raider` band exists at all (THR-767 owes that role a population). The Company section gained a prose **Rivals** line — `agentDetail` reads `hostile_to` off the company node in *both* directions and dedupes the mutual pair the contest writes, rendering "There is blood between them and The Errant Keys of The Arcane Circle": never a count, never the `since` tick, and absent entirely for a company that has never fought. **PR 3's predicted suspect was correct.** `encounter.confront_guild_falls` and `encounter.band_defend` both gated on `sublocation-type.guildhall`; worldgen mints `sublocation-type.guild-hall` — 34 on seed 42 medium, and zero of the unhyphenated id on any seed. One missing hyphen made the ticket's own capstone and the band's counter-encounter **unreachable by construction**, with a green suite throughout. Now locked by a derived content-integrity test whose two design choices are both load-bearing: the mintable set is computed from the five sources that actually mint sublocations (never hand-listed, so it cannot drift from the generators), and it reads the merged `locationSubtypes` rather than the authored `sublocationTypes` — because `toUnifiedTemplate` folds the latter into the former, so a check on the authored field matches nothing and passes vacuously. The first draft did exactly that and "passed" with the bug reintroduced; the shipped version fails on the typo, on nothing else, and asserts against its own vacuity. Done-when #4 demonstrated headlessly: a company relocated into a Great Silverhold guild hall resolved The Guild Falls against a colocated Arcane Circle defender band — outcomes [failure, success, success], company cohesion 0.54 → 0.70, band 0.70 → 0.46, mutual grudges written at t61. **Honest negative, kept in canon rather than dropped:** the family is now gate-reachable (all four assert against `generateUnifiedCandidates` with a real band present) and the loop is proven, but no confrontation was observed winning a scoring pass unforced — a company parked beside two foreign bands drew 7 other encounters over 100 ticks. That is a base-rate/tuning question, not a wiring one, and the rulebook says so rather than implying the family fires on its own.
✅ THR-732 (Reunite + Sunder — the company arc closes) — the god's two remaining company verbs, completing the triangle Draw Together and Bless left open: one that reaches backward, one that breaks. **Reunite** is cast on a company that has *already ended* — the only card in the game whose target is a piece of history. It opens a `reuniteUntilTick` window on the dead node and stamps **Draw Together's own** `convergePullHexCol/_HexRow/_UntilTick` triple on every gatherable former member, so the scattered bend their own roads homeward through `computeConvergenceBonus` and no second convergence mechanism exists to drift; a `REUNITE_COMPAT_BONUS` lets two old comrades clear the formation threshold on shared history alone, and the re-formed company takes a variant of the old name (*The Quiet Wardens, Re-formed*) under a new `reunite` formation cause that deliberately outranks `draw_together` in precedence — judged on the pull alone every reunion would have been told as an ordinary divine gathering. Both endings are authored: **The Reunion** when they find each other, **The Road Not Taken** when the window lapses unanswered, because a god who calls and is not answered is the premise working, not a failure to hide. **Sunder** is Bless's exact mirror — same target class, same window shape, every read amplifying where Bless suppresses: dissent bites ×2 (`groupCohesion`), a fraying company's members leave ×2 as readily (`groupDissolution`), and the fray drama pool treats the company as frayed whatever the number says (`phaseGroups`), so the visible output is scenes rather than a falling gauge. The two windows **do not cancel** — both may be open at once, each read independently, and the tug-of-war is left unresolved by design. **The finding that would have made Reunite inert:** the plan named its input as the disbanded company's `roster`, and verified that property exists and is persisted — but `dissolveGroup` sets `roster: []` on the way out, so a faithful implementation finds zero gatherable members forever, silently, on exactly the input the verb takes. What survives dissolution is the `member_of` edges it stamps with `leftAtTick`, which `groupDissolution.ts`'s own header calls the historical record; `getFormerGroupMembers` walks those, and the shipped test asserts `roster === []` and the edge-walk returning all three riders in the same body, so a roster-based implementation fails the test that documents why. Also caught: `sphereAffinity: 'dusk'` — a word borrowed from a neighbouring content pool whose keys turn out not to be sphere ids at all (6 of 8 are unreachable; filed THR-770) — which crashed the milestone-beat render sweep and is now `entropy`, the sphere `reach: 'shadow'` actually maps to. Both verbs are breadth unlocks granted together by *An Empty Road*, the milestone that fires the first time a company the player was bound to comes apart — which is Reunite's own precondition, so neither card arrives before the world holds something for it to act on. Three interface rows 🟢 LIVE, the convergence one falsified during authoring (renaming the written key fails the guard). Card art deferred to THR-769: none of the four company verbs has art, including the two THR-74 shipped.
✅ THR-734 (UL: group-layer terms) — the group layer finally has canonical words. Five entries into the Agents shard: **Group** (the engine object — `actorType: 'group'` + `groupType`, no `located_at` edge because position derives from the leader, and `armyState` absent is what separates it from an army sharing the same actorType), **Company** (the player-facing word; "party" survives only as an internal `groupType` value and must never reach prose or UI), **Group Cohesion** (event-driven 0–1 rendered as bound/holding/frayed/breaking, never as a number outside the DebugPanel), **Draw Together** and **Bless this Company**. The handoff's instruction to mark the two verbs not-yet-implemented was reversed against the code: THR-74 PR 2b has since landed and all four `company.*` verbs are live UATs, so entries claiming design-only status would have written drift into the artifact that exists to prevent it — the handoff's own governing clause said "reconciled against shipped code". Every constant, threshold and symbol cited was grep-verified before it was written down. Filed on the way: THR-771 (`GROUP_DISSOLUTION_THRESHOLD`'s doc comment has the two lower cohesion bands swapped relative to `getCohesionState`).
✅ THR-737 (trait_grant consumers — the leak one layer up, closed) — `trait_grant` was the textbook write-only contract: 22 authored payloads across five catalogs, a correct resolver collecting `grantedTraits`, and **zero production readers**. `hasGrantedTrait` was called only by its own definition, a re-export, and tests, so an item advertising `intimidate` conferred nothing a mortal, an encounter, or a spell could act on. THR-722 had retired the parallel `possesses`-edge `grants[]` property for exactly this reason and migrated its payload onto `effects[]` — which moved the leak rather than closing it, and the interface row said so in as many words. Closed by a `collectGrantedTraits` aggregate wrapping `hasGrantedTrait`, consumed by the three production gates that ask "does this agent have capability X?": encounter eligibility in `filterByPrerequisites`, spell prerequisites in `checkPrerequisites`, and ambition eligibility via `buildAmbitionAgentSnapshot` plus the parallel `worldSeed` initial-assignment path. **The ticket's own suggested read site would have shipped a vacuous gate.** It cites `encounterScoring.ts:376` (`grantsTraitWhileHeld` → `ruin_seeker`) as the precedent shape, and wiring the aggregate there types cleanly, tests green, and changes nothing — because not one of the 22 `trait_grant` payloads grants `ruin_seeker`. The enumeration that mattered was of the *granted* values against the *required* ones: `master_smith` appears on both sides (The Worldforge Anvil grants it; `ambition_forge_legend` gates on it), and that intersection is now asserted directly, so re-authoring either side fails the suite instead of silently re-emptying the gate. `blockedByTraits` was wired alongside `requiredTraits` for symmetry — a grant that can unlock scarred-gated content while leaving scarred-*blocked* content open is half a gate. Granted traits carry no level, so they satisfy level-bearing requirements at `GRANTED_TRAIT_EFFECTIVE_LEVEL` (1), matching the `grantedTraitLevel ?? 1` default the sibling possession path already uses; the aggregate is fail-soft and lazily cached per agent, since the encounter pipeline runs for every agent every tick. Contract `attachment-trait-grant-effects` 🔴 LEAKED → 🟢 LIVE with dated evidence. Live proof it is reachable in a normal run, not just in tests: seed 42 / medium at tick 30 carries 2 agents holding 3 granted traits (`cavalry_charge`, `rapid_retreat`, `intimidate`) from starter attachments — grants that, until this change, every gate in the game ignored.
✅ THR-754 (Browser-verify: sanctioned evidence routes) — the DoD's 1920×1080 screenshot clause was called "binary" while the unattended lane could satisfy it by no route at all: the Browser pane refuses to composite with no pane displayed, Playwright's capture returned its own dimensions regardless of `browser_resize`, and a bare `filename` wrote the PNG outside the repo. Five recurrences each rediscovered a different workaround. The clause stays binary; it now carries a ladder naming which capture discharges it per lane and surface — attended unchanged, unattended via Playwright MCP with an **absolute** `filename` inside the session worktree, portal-rendered overlays proven by accessibility-tree capture (CDP omits the portal layer entirely, on shipped surfaces too), and a capture whose real size differs from 1920×1080 stating its actual dimensions and discharging the viewport contract by DOM assertion from the Browser pane. Deviations are declared as `Browser-verify substitution: <route> — <reason>`, mirroring `Browser-verify exempt:`. Answers impediment #199's durable-fix line and decides the THR-696 retro question in favour of naming the split. Requirement 1 also stopped naming `preview_resize`/`preview_screenshot`, which no longer exist.
✅ THR-755 (gate-credibility sweep) — six rows, one shape: a gate whose output contradicts its verdict. `lint:plan-doc`'s scoping had already been fixed upstream (THR-686/692), leaving a subtler defect — a *waived* doc and a *mistyped path* printed the identical `skipped (no candidate files found)`, so the exemptions that fix was made of could not be verified from the gate's own output; it now names every waived doc. `check:wiki-freshness:blocking` printed `FAIL` plus the full remediation text and only afterwards discovered the `Wiki-freshness-exempt:` token and exited 0, so the last thing a reader saw was a failure and a to-do already satisfied; the exemption is now resolved before any verdict prints, a hard-wrapped reason is joined across continuation lines instead of being truncated mid-sentence (the audited half of the escape hatch, and what `weekly-project-hygiene` reviews), and blocking mode asserts `is stale` rather than hedging `may be stale` about a claim it is about to exit 1 over. `check:process`'s `deferred-ref` rule flagged the drift scanner's own report template forever, since that template renders a row labelled with the marker it counts. The interface-map generator's `nothing produces this contract` / `the consumer is starving` verdicts now name the cheaper second cause — a mis-declared `mechanism.symbols` — with the literal grep to run. And `social-encounter-content.ts` carried a duplicate `import type { UnifiedActionTemplate }` at line 2462 that was a hard PARSE_ERROR for vite-node. **The row-3 fix reproduced row 3 on its first run:** the comment written to explain the exclusion quoted the marker literally, and the gate reported two fresh false errors on those exact lines — a marker lint cannot tell a marker from a mention of one, the same vector THR-738 closed for `Fixes THR-XX`. That also decided the ticket's open question in favour of path-scoping over literal-detection, since the explanatory comment would have been *code* rather than a literal and would have failed either way. Guard falsified before trusting it (a probe under `src/` still flagged, one under `scripts/drift-scan/` not). Typecheck 3519 → 3517; baseline refreshed.
✅ THR-786 (traits trigger unification — one predicate, six sites) — the six trait-predicate vocabularies (encounter filter pipeline, effect-predicate `has_trait:` sugar, `graphConditions`, ambition snapshot eligibility, spell prerequisites, item-granted keys) now resolve through one `collectBearerTraitRefs`/`bearerMatchesPredicate` on ANY-match across four ref forms (node id, short id, display name, tag), with `resolveTraitPredicate` as the bearer-agnostic entry point and a `SimulationRuntime`-owned `TraitRefIndex` for content sweeps. Unchanged-behavior contract suite written first as the kill criterion: 31 PRESERVED assertions green pre- and post-migration, 6 labelled widening/repair cases each verified failing beforehand. New `__DEBUG.validateTraitRefs()` sweeps all six authored surfaces and reported the finding that outgrew the ticket — 62 authored trait gates resolve to no trait definition (THR-800), because authored refs are bare snake_case while every definition uses `trait.<category>.<kebab>` ids / Title Case names / `#tags`; masked all this time by the self-closing grant-key path. Two dead display-name reads repaired in passing; template gate fields still undeclared (THR-801). Predicate-only floor — no minting, tick phases, PRNG, or new node/edge types.
✅ THR-801 (template trait-gate fields declared — the phantom API made real) — `filterByPrerequisites` had read `template.requiredTraits`/`blockedByTraits` every tick since THR-773, but neither field was declared on `UnifiedActionTemplate`: a gate evaluated by the engine and authorable by nobody, where any template that tried to declare one simply failed to typecheck. Two additive optional fields (`readonly requiredTraits?: readonly TraitPredicate[]`, `readonly blockedByTraits?: readonly string[]` — the asymmetry deliberate, since holding a blocking trait at any level blocks) cleared **13** baseline errors, 3517 → 3504, with **zero net-new**, proven by diffing the full `tsc -b` error set either side of the change rather than trusting the count. Both halves of the ticket's "two errors in one file" prediction were low: 8 sat in `encounterFilterPipeline.ts` (six TS2339 plus the TS7006 implicit-any cascades they caused) and the other 5 in a **second production reader nobody had identified** — `src/engine/kpi/branchingDistance.ts`, the diagnostic reporting how many agents a gate excludes — which THR-786's migration could not see because its reads sat inside the red baseline (THR-489) as "property does not exist"; it still compares refs by raw node id instead of the shared ANY-match resolver, latent while no gate is authored (THR-802). Authoring deferred to THR-778 (WS5) under the Done-when's stated alternative, because THR-800 had just shown all 62 authored trait refs to be dead — minting a gate now would author the 63rd and pre-empt the vocabulary THR-788 is still settling. The regression guard is type-level with the ratchet as its teeth, not vitest: the existing site-1 cases mock `getAnyEncounterById` with untyped literals and pass whether the interface declares the fields or not, which is precisely how the gap survived two shipping tickets.
✅ THR-797 (reaper no longer reaps live sessions) — the hourly `clean-stale-git.sh` judged liveness only by git-admin-dir mtime, which stops moving during a closeout that is still writing Linear/doc/impediment files; at the 08:40 run on 2026-07-26 it removed a worktree ~5h after that session's merge, and because `.claude/worktrees/<name>` lives inside the home tree's working copy, the session's next `git checkout -b` silently landed on the home tree. `is_live` now also probes working-tree file mtimes (the same signal the orphan sweep already trusted — that sweep called the very same path "recent activity" two lines after the removal WARN in that one run), a 90-minute `WORKTREE_MERGE_GRACE_MINUTES` hold covers the narrower post-merge case, and the branch pass keeps any branch whose unregistered worktree corpse is still active, closing the THR-673 chain that deleted the branch an hour later.
✅ THR-803 (encounter chains work for the first time) — `recordChainStageCompletion` had zero production callers, so `agent.properties.chainProgress` was read by four modules and written by none; and `ENCOUNTER_CHAINS` named stages as bare keys (`knowledge_test`) while every real template is `encounter.knowledge_test`, so `templateToChains` never matched and the gate returned `true` for all nine stages. The subsystem was inert, not gating — the filed symptom ("6 of 9 stages permanently undrawable") was the inverse of the truth, the same never-intersecting-vocabularies shape as THR-800. Stage ids repointed to live template ids and guarded by a test resolving each through `getAnyEncounterById`; `applyChainStageCompletion` wired into `executeStepResult` (the resolution site that owns graph writes, gated on the whole encounter landing rather than a step passing), persisting progress in place, granting `CHAIN_COMPLETION_CAPABILITY_BONUS` once via an idempotent `chain_mastery_*` trait, touching `worldVersion`, and emitting one aggregate `chain_progress` trace per advance. Verified live at tick 150 of seed-42 medium: five agents with written progress, two at stage 1. Guild rank gating deferred to THR-805 — chains may be the wrong mechanism (rank is already first-class on `member_of`, `faction_rank:` predicate exists), a chain stage holds one template id where each guild tier holds several, and `MAX_ACTIVE_CHAINS=2` would make it a balance change.
